Article 1. Objectives of the Criminal Code of Ukraine

1. The Criminal Code of Ukraine has as its objective the legal provision of protection of the rights and freedoms of man and citizen, property, public order and public safety, the environment, the constitutional order of Ukraine from criminally unlawful encroachments, ensuring peace and security of mankind, as well as the prevention of criminal offenses.
2. To accomplish this objective, the Criminal Code of Ukraine defines which socially dangerous acts are criminal offenses and which punishments are applied to persons who have committed them.
{Article 1 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 2. Basis of criminal liability

1. The basis of criminal liability is the commission by a person of a socially dangerous act that contains the elements of a criminal offense provided for by this Code.
2. A person is considered not guilty of committing a criminal offense and may not be subjected to criminal punishment until their guilt has been proven in the manner prescribed by law and established by a conviction of a court.
3. No one may be brought to criminal liability for the same criminal offense more than once.
{Article 2 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section II

LAW ON CRIMINAL LIABILITY

Article 3. Legislation of Ukraine on criminal liability

1. The legislation of Ukraine on criminal liability consists of the Criminal Code of Ukraine, which is based on the Constitution of Ukraine and universally recognized principles and norms of international law.
2. Laws of Ukraine on criminal liability adopted after this Code enters into force shall be incorporated into it after they enter into force.
3. The criminal unlawfulness of an act, as well as its punishability and other criminal-legal consequences, are determined only by this Code.
4. Application of the law on criminal liability by analogy is prohibited.
5. Laws of Ukraine on criminal liability must comply with the provisions contained in effective international treaties, consent to the binding nature of which has been given by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine.
6. Amendments to the legislation of Ukraine on criminal liability may be made exclusively by laws on amendments to this Code and/or to the criminal procedural legislation of Ukraine, and/or to the legislation of Ukraine on administrative offenses.
{Article 3 as amended according to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 619-IX of 19.05.2020}

Article 4. Effect of the law on criminal liability in time

1. A law on criminal liability enters into force ten days from the day of its official promulgation, unless otherwise provided by the law itself, but not earlier than the day of its publication.
2. The criminal unlawfulness and punishability, as well as other criminal-legal consequences of an act, are determined by the law on criminal liability that was in effect at the time of commission of that act.
3. The time of commission of a criminal offense is recognized as the time of commission by a person of an act or omission provided for by the law on criminal liability.
{Article 4 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 5. Retroactive effect of the law on criminal liability in time

1. A law on criminal liability that abolishes the criminal unlawfulness of an act, mitigates criminal liability or otherwise improves the situation of a person has retroactive effect in time, that is, it extends to persons who committed the relevant acts before such law entered into force, including persons who are serving punishment or have served punishment but have a criminal record.
2. A law on criminal liability that establishes the criminal unlawfulness of an act, increases criminal liability or otherwise worsens the situation of a person does not have retroactive effect in time.
3. A law on criminal liability that partially mitigates criminal liability or otherwise improves the situation of a person, and partially increases criminal liability or otherwise worsens the situation of a person, has retroactive effect in time only in the part that mitigates criminal liability or otherwise improves the situation of a person.
4. If after a person commits an act provided for by this Code, the law on criminal liability has been changed several times, the law that abolishes the criminal unlawfulness of the act, mitigates criminal liability or otherwise improves the situation of the person has retroactive effect in time.
{Article 5 as worded by Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 6

Effect of the law on criminal liability with respect to a criminal offense committed on the territory of Ukraine
1. Persons who have committed criminal offenses on the territory of Ukraine are subject to criminal liability under this Code.
2. A criminal offense is recognized as committed on the territory of Ukraine if it was initiated, continued, completed or terminated on the territory of Ukraine.
3. A criminal offense is recognized as committed on the territory of Ukraine if its perpetrator or at least one of the accomplices acted on the territory of Ukraine.
4. The issue of criminal liability of diplomatic representatives of foreign states and other citizens who, under the laws of Ukraine and international treaties, consent to the binding nature of which has been given by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, are not subject in criminal cases to the courts of Ukraine, in the event they commit a criminal offense on the territory of Ukraine, is resolved through diplomatic channels.
{Article 6 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 7

Effect of the law on criminal liability with respect to criminal offenses committed by citizens of Ukraine or stateless persons outside Ukraine
1. Citizens of Ukraine and stateless persons permanently residing in Ukraine who have committed criminal offenses outside its borders are subject to criminal liability under this Code, unless otherwise provided by international treaties of Ukraine, consent to the binding nature of which has been given by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine.
2. If the persons specified in part one of this article have been subjected to criminal punishment outside Ukraine for the criminal offenses committed, they may not be brought to criminal liability in Ukraine for these criminal offenses.
{Article 7 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 8

Effect of the law on criminal liability with respect to criminal offenses committed by foreigners or stateless persons outside Ukraine
1. Foreigners or stateless persons not permanently residing in Ukraine who have committed criminal offenses outside its borders are subject to liability in Ukraine under this Code in cases provided for by international treaties or if they have committed serious or particularly serious crimes provided for by this Code against the rights and freedoms of citizens of Ukraine or the interests of Ukraine.
2. Foreigners or stateless persons not permanently residing in Ukraine are also subject to liability in Ukraine according to this Code if they have committed outside Ukraine, in complicity with officials who are citizens of Ukraine, any criminal offense provided for in Articles 368, 368-3, 368-4, 369 and 369-2 of this Code, or if they have offered, promised, provided an unlawful benefit to such officials, or accepted an offer, promise of an unlawful benefit or received such benefit from them.
{Article 8 as amended according to Laws No. 3316-IV of 12.01.2006, No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 9. Legal Consequences of Conviction of a Person Outside Ukraine

1. A judgment of a court of a foreign state may be taken into account if a citizen of Ukraine, a foreigner or a stateless person was convicted of a criminal offense committed outside Ukraine and has again committed a criminal offense on the territory of Ukraine.
2. In accordance with part one of this Article, recidivism of criminal offenses, unserved punishment or other legal consequences of a judgment of a court of a foreign state shall be taken into account in the qualification of a new criminal offense, sentencing, release from criminal liability or punishment.
{Article 9 as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 10

Resolution of the issue of criminal liability of persons who are subject to criminal liability under the legislation of a foreign state and are on the territory of Ukraine, and execution of sentences rendered by foreign courts or international judicial institutions
1. Citizens of Ukraine who have committed crimes outside Ukraine may not be extradited to a foreign state for prosecution and trial.
2. Foreigners and stateless persons who have committed crimes outside Ukraine and are on the territory of Ukraine may be extradited to a foreign state for prosecution and trial.
3. Ukraine may take over criminal proceedings in which a judgment has not been rendered by judicial authorities of a foreign state, with respect to citizens of Ukraine and foreigners who have committed crimes outside Ukraine and are on the territory of Ukraine, but who cannot be extradited to a foreign state or whose extradition has been refused, if the act in connection with which the transfer of criminal proceedings is requested is recognized as a crime under this Code.
4. Execution in Ukraine of a judgment of a foreign court or international judicial institution is possible if the act as a result of which the judgment was rendered is recognized as a criminal offense under this Code or would be a criminal offense if committed on the territory of Ukraine.
{Article 10 as worded by Law No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013; as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section III

CRIMINAL OFFENSE, ITS TYPES AND STAGES
{Title of Section III as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 11. Concept of Criminal Offense

1. A criminal offense is a socially dangerous culpable act (action or omission) provided for by this Code, committed by a subject of a criminal offense.
2. An action or omission which, although formally containing the elements of any act provided for by this Code, but due to insignificance does not constitute a social danger, that is, has not caused and could not cause substantial harm to a natural or legal person, society or the state, is not a criminal offense.
{Article 11 as worded by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 12. Classification of Criminal Offenses

1. Criminal offenses are divided into criminal misdemeanors and crimes.
2. A criminal misdemeanor is an act (action or omission) provided for by this Code, for the commission of which the main punishment is a fine not exceeding three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or another punishment not related to deprivation of liberty.
3. Crimes are divided into minor, serious and particularly serious.
4. A minor crime is an act (action or omission) provided for by this Code, for the commission of which the main punishment is a fine not exceeding ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years.
5. A serious crime is an act (action or omission) provided for by this Code, for the commission of which the main punishment is a fine not exceeding twenty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding ten years.
6. A particularly serious crime is an act (action or omission) provided for by this Code, for the commission of which the main punishment is a fine exceeding twenty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, deprivation of liberty for a term exceeding ten years or life imprisonment.
7. The degree of gravity of a crime for the commission of which both a main punishment in the form of a fine and deprivation of liberty are provided shall be determined based on the term of punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty provided for the respective crime.
{Article 12 as worded by Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 13. Completed and Uncompleted Criminal Offense

1. A completed criminal offense is an act that contains all the elements of the corpus delicti of a criminal offense provided for by the relevant article of the Special Part of this Code.
2. An uncompleted criminal offense is preparation for a criminal offense and attempt to commit a criminal offense.
{Article 13 as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 14. Preparation for a Criminal Offense

1. Preparation for a criminal offense is the search for or adaptation of means or instruments, search for accomplices or conspiracy to commit a criminal offense, removal of obstacles, as well as other intentional creation of conditions for the commission of a criminal offense.
2. Preparation for a criminal misdemeanor or a crime for which an article of the Special Part of this Code provides for punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or another, more lenient punishment, does not entail criminal liability.
{Article 14 as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 15. Attempt to Commit a Criminal Offense

1. An attempt to commit a criminal offense is the commission by a person with direct intent of an act (action or omission) directly aimed at committing a criminal offense provided for by the relevant article of the Special Part of this Code, if the criminal offense was not completed for reasons beyond the person's control.
2. An attempt to commit a criminal offense is completed if the person performed all actions that the person considered necessary to complete the criminal offense, but the criminal offense was not completed for reasons beyond the person's control.
3. An attempt to commit a criminal offense is uncompleted if the person, for reasons beyond the person's control, did not perform all actions that the person considered necessary to complete the criminal offense.
{Article 15 as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 16. Criminal Liability for an Uncompleted Criminal Offense

Criminal liability for preparation for a criminal offense and attempt to commit a criminal offense arises under Article 14 or 15 and under the article of the Special Part of this Code that provides for liability for a completed criminal offense.
{Article 16 as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 17. Voluntary Abandonment in an Uncompleted Criminal Offense

1. Voluntary abandonment is the final cessation by a person of their own volition of preparation for a criminal offense or attempt to commit a criminal offense, if the person was aware of the possibility of completing the criminal offense.
2. A person who voluntarily abandoned the completion of a criminal offense is subject to criminal liability only if the act actually committed by the person contains the corpus delicti of another criminal offense.
{Article 17 as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section IV

PERSON SUBJECT TO CRIMINAL LIABILITY (SUBJECT OF A CRIMINAL OFFENSE)
{Title of Section IV as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 18. Subject of a criminal offense

1. The subject of a criminal offense is a natural sane person who committed a criminal offense at an age from which, in accordance with this Code, criminal liability may arise.
2. A special subject of a criminal offense is a natural sane person who committed, at an age from which criminal liability may arise, a criminal offense the subject of which can only be a specific person.
3. Officials are persons who permanently, temporarily or by special authority exercise the functions of representatives of government or local self-government, and also permanently or temporarily hold in bodies of state power, bodies of local self-government, at enterprises, in institutions or organizations positions related to the performance of organizational-administrative or administrative-economic functions, or perform such functions by special authority with which a person is vested by an authorized body of state power, a body of local self-government, a central body of state administration with special status, an authorized body or an authorized official of an enterprise, institution, organization, a court or by law.
4. Officials are also recognized as officials of foreign states (persons who hold positions in a legislative, executive or judicial body of a foreign state, including jurors, other persons who perform functions of the state for a foreign state, in particular for a state body or state enterprise), foreign arbitrators, persons authorized to resolve civil, commercial or labor disputes in foreign states in a procedure alternative to judicial, officials of international organizations (employees of an international organization or any other persons authorized by such organization to act on its behalf), as well as members of international parliamentary assemblies in which Ukraine participates, and judges and officials of international courts.
{Article 18 as amended in accordance with Law No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009, No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. A person is recognized as sane who at the time of committing a criminal offense was able to be aware of their actions (inaction) and control them.
2. A person is not subject to criminal liability who at the time of committing a socially dangerous act provided for by this Code was in a state of insanity, that is, could not be aware of their actions (inaction) or control them due to chronic mental illness, temporary disorder of mental activity, mental deficiency or other morbid condition of the psyche. To such a person, by decision of a court, compulsory measures of a medical nature may be applied.
3. A person is not subject to punishment who committed a criminal offense in a state of sanity but before the pronouncement of a sentence became ill with a mental illness that deprives them of the possibility to be aware of their actions (inaction) or control them. To such a person, by decision of a court, compulsory measures of a medical nature may be applied, and after recovery such a person may be subject to punishment.
{Article 19 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. A person recognized by a court as having diminished sanity is subject to criminal liability, that is, one who at the time of committing a criminal offense, due to a mental disorder present in them, was not capable to the full extent of being aware of their actions (inaction) and (or) controlling them.
2. Recognition of a person as having diminished sanity is taken into account by the court when imposing punishment and may be grounds for the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature.
{Article 20 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 21

Criminal liability for criminal offenses committed in a state of alcoholic, narcotic or other intoxication or under the influence of medicinal drugs that reduce attention and speed of reaction
A person who committed a criminal offense in a state of alcoholic, narcotic or other intoxication or under the influence of medicinal drugs that reduce attention and speed of reaction is subject to criminal liability.
{Article 21 as worded by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 22. Age from which criminal liability may arise

1. Persons who had reached the age of sixteen by the time of committing a criminal offense are subject to criminal liability.
2. Persons who committed criminal offenses between the ages of fourteen and sixteen are subject to criminal liability only for intentional homicide (Articles 115-117), encroachment on the life of a state or public figure, a law enforcement officer, a member of a public formation for the protection of public order and the state border or a servicemember, a judge, a people's assessor or a juror in connection with their activity related to the administration of justice, a defense counsel or representative of a person in connection with activity related to the provision of legal assistance, a representative of a foreign state (Articles 112, 348, 379, 400, 443), intentional grievous bodily injury (

Article 121

, part three of Articles 345, 346, 350, 377, 398), cruel treatment of animals (

Article 299)

, intentional medium gravity bodily injury (

Article 122

, part two of Articles 345, 346, 350, 377, 398), sabotage (

Article 113)

, banditry (

Article 257)

, terrorist act (

Article 258)

, hostage-taking (Articles 147 and 349), rape (

Article 152)

, sexual violence (

Article 153)

, theft (

Article 185

, part one of Articles 262, 308), robbery (Articles 186, 262, 308), armed robbery (

Article 187

, part three of Articles 262, 308), extortion (Articles 189, 262, 308), intentional destruction or damage to property (part two of Articles 194, 347, 352, 378, parts two and three of Article 399), damage to communication routes and means of transport (

Article 277)

, hijacking or seizure of railway rolling stock, aircraft, sea or river vessel (

Article 278)

, unlawful seizure of a vehicle (parts two, three of Article 289), hooliganism (

Article 296)

{Article 22 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1684-IX of 15.07.2021 - enters into force from 08.11.2021}
Guilt is the mental attitude of a person toward an action or inaction committed, provided for by this Code, and its consequences, expressed in the form of intent or negligence.
1. Intent is divided into direct and indirect.
2. Intent is direct if a person was aware of the socially dangerous nature of their act (action or inaction), foresaw its socially dangerous consequences and desired their occurrence.
3. Intent is indirect if a person was aware of the socially dangerous nature of their act (action or inaction), foresaw its socially dangerous consequences and although did not desire, but consciously allowed their occurrence.

Article 25. Negligence and its types

1. Negligence is divided into criminal unlawful overconfidence and criminal unlawful carelessness.
2. Negligence is criminal unlawful overconfidence if a person foresaw the possibility of the occurrence of socially dangerous consequences of their act (action or inaction), but frivolously counted on their prevention.
3. Negligence is criminal unlawful carelessness if a person did not foresee the possibility of the occurrence of socially dangerous consequences of their act (action or inaction), although should have and could have foreseen them.
{Article 25 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section VI

COMPLICITY IN A CRIMINAL OFFENSE
{Title of Section VI as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
Complicity in a criminal offense is the intentional joint participation of several subjects of a criminal offense in the commission of an intentional criminal offense.
{Article 26 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. Accomplices to a criminal offense, along with the perpetrator, are the organizer, instigator and accessory.
2. The perpetrator (co-perpetrator) is a person who, in complicity with other subjects of a criminal offense, directly or through the use of other persons who, in accordance with the law, are not subject to criminal liability for what was committed, committed a criminal offense provided for by this Code.
3. The organizer is a person who organized the commission of a criminal offense (criminal offenses) or directed its (their) preparation or commission. The organizer is also a person who formed an organized group or criminal organization or directed it, or a person who provided financing or organized the concealment of the criminally unlawful activity of an organized group or criminal organization.
4. The instigator is a person who, by persuasion, bribery, threat, coercion or other means, induced another accomplice to commit a criminal offense.
5. The accessory is a person who, by advice, instructions, provision of means or instruments, or removal of obstacles, facilitated the commission of a criminal offense by other accomplices, as well as a person who promised in advance to conceal the person who committed the criminal offense, the instruments or means of committing the criminal offense, the traces of the criminal offense or objects obtained by criminally unlawful means, to acquire or dispose of such objects, or otherwise facilitate the concealment of the criminal offense.
6. Not constituting complicity is the concealment, not promised in advance, of a person who committed a criminal offense, the instruments and means of committing the criminal offense, the traces of the criminal offense or objects obtained by criminally unlawful means, or the acquisition or disposal of such objects. Persons who committed such acts are subject to criminal liability only in cases provided for by Articles 198 and 396 of this Code.
7. Not constituting complicity is the failure to report, promised before the completion of the commission of a criminal offense, about a reliably known criminal offense being prepared or committed. Such persons are subject to criminal liability only in cases where the act they committed contains elements of another criminal offense.
{Article 27 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 28

Commission of a criminal offense by a group of persons, by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, by an organized group or by a criminal organization
1. A criminal offense is recognized as committed by a group of persons if several (two or more) perpetrators participated in it without prior conspiracy among themselves.
2. A criminal offense is recognized as committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons if it was jointly committed by several persons (two or more) who in advance, that is, before the beginning of the criminal offense, agreed on its joint commission.
3. A criminal offense is recognized as committed by an organized group if several persons (three or more) participated in its preparation or commission, who previously organized themselves into a stable association for the commission of this and other criminal offense(s), united by a single plan with distribution of functions of the group members, directed toward achieving this plan, known to all members of the group.
4. A criminal offense is recognized as committed by a criminal organization if it was committed by a stable hierarchical association of several persons (five or more), whose members or structural parts of which, by prior conspiracy, organized themselves for joint activity with the purpose of directly committing grave or especially grave crimes by members of this organization, or directing or coordinating the criminally unlawful activity of other persons, or ensuring the functioning of both the criminal organization itself and other criminally unlawful groups.
{Article 28 as amended pursuant to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 29. Criminal liability of accomplices

1. The perpetrator (co-perpetrator) is subject to criminal liability under the article of the Special Part of this Code that provides for the criminal offense committed by him or her.
2. The organizer, instigator and accessory are subject to criminal liability under the corresponding part of Article 27 and the article (part of the article) of the Special Part of this Code that provides for the criminal offense committed by the perpetrator.
3. Characteristics that characterize the person of an individual accomplice to a criminal offense are imputed only to that accomplice. Other circumstances that aggravate liability and are provided for in the articles of the Special Part of this Code as elements of a criminal offense that affect the qualification of the perpetrator's actions are imputed only to the accomplice who was aware of these circumstances.
4. In the event the perpetrator commits an uncompleted criminal offense, the other accomplices are subject to criminal liability for complicity in an uncompleted criminal offense.
5. Accomplices are not subject to criminal liability for an act committed by the perpetrator if it was not covered by their intent.
{Article 29 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 30

Criminal liability of organizers and participants of an organized group or criminal organization
1. The organizer of an organized group or criminal organization is subject to criminal liability for all criminal offenses committed by the organized group or criminal organization if they were covered by his or her intent.
2. Other participants of an organized group or criminal organization are subject to criminal liability for criminal offenses in the preparation or commission of which they participated, regardless of the role that each of them performed in the criminal offense.
{Text of Article 30 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 31. Voluntary abandonment by accomplices

1. In the event of voluntary abandonment of the commission of a criminal offense, the perpetrator (co-perpetrator) is not subject to criminal liability if the conditions provided for by Article 17 of this Code are present. In this case, the other accomplices are subject to criminal liability for preparation for the criminal offense or attempt at the criminal offense from the commission of which the perpetrator voluntarily abandoned.
2. The organizer, instigator or accessory are not subject to criminal liability upon voluntary abandonment if they averted the commission of the criminal offense or timely notified the relevant state authorities about the criminal offense being prepared or committed. Voluntary abandonment by an accessory also includes his or her failure to provide means or instruments for committing the criminal offense or failure to remove obstacles to the commission of the criminal offense.
3. In the event of voluntary abandonment by any of the accomplices, the perpetrator is subject to criminal liability for preparation for a criminal offense or for attempt at a criminal offense, depending on which of these stages his or her act was terminated at.
{Article 31 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section VII

REPETITION, AGGREGATE AND RECIDIVISM OF CRIMINAL OFFENSES
{Title of Section VII as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 32. Repetition of criminal offenses

1. Repetition of criminal offenses means the commission of two or more criminal offenses provided for by the same article or part of an article of the Special Part of this Code.
2. Repetition provided for in part one of this article is absent in the commission of a continuing criminal offense, which consists of two or more identical acts united by a single criminally unlawful intent.
3. The commission of two or more criminal offenses provided for by different articles of this Code is recognized as repetition only in cases provided for in the Special Part of this Code.
4. Repetition is absent if for a previously committed criminal offense the person was released from criminal liability on grounds established by law, or if the conviction for this criminal offense was extinguished or removed, as well as after serving the punishment for committing a criminal misdemeanor.
{Article 32 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 33. Aggregate of criminal offenses

1. An aggregate of criminal offenses means the commission by a person of two or more criminal offenses provided for by different articles or different parts of one article of the Special Part of this Code, for none of which the person has been convicted. In this case, criminal offenses for which the person was released from criminal liability on grounds established by law are not taken into account.
2. In the case of an aggregate of criminal offenses, each of them is subject to qualification under the relevant article or part of an article of the Special Part of this Code.
{Article 33 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 34. Recidivism of criminal offenses

Recidivism of criminal offenses means the commission of a new intentional criminal offense by a person who has a conviction for an intentional criminal offense.
{Article 34 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 35

Legal consequences of repetition, aggregate and recidivism of criminal offenses
Repetition, aggregate and recidivism of criminal offenses are taken into account in the qualification of criminal offenses and sentencing, in deciding the issue of the possibility of release from criminal liability and punishment in cases provided for by this Code.
{Article 35 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Розділ VIII

CIRCUMSTANCES EXCLUDING CRIMINAL UNLAWFULNESS OF AN ACT
{Title of Section VIII as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. Necessary defense means actions committed for the purpose of protecting legally protected rights and interests of the person defending themselves or another person, as well as public interests and interests of the state from a socially dangerous encroachment by inflicting harm on the encroacher that is necessary and sufficient in the given situation to immediately avert or stop the encroachment, provided that the limits of necessary defense were not exceeded.
2. Every person has the right to necessary defense regardless of the possibility to avoid the socially dangerous encroachment or to seek assistance from other persons or authorities.
3. Exceeding the limits of necessary defense means intentionally inflicting on the encroacher serious harm that clearly does not correspond to the danger of the encroachment or the circumstances of defense. Exceeding the limits of necessary defense entails criminal liability only in cases specifically provided for in Articles 118 and 124 of this Code.
4. A person is not subject to criminal liability if, due to strong emotional disturbance caused by a socially dangerous encroachment, they could not assess the correspondence of the harm inflicted by them to the danger of the encroachment or the circumstances of defense.
5. The use of weapons or any other means or objects to defend against an attack by an armed person or an attack by a group of persons, as well as to avert unlawful violent intrusion into a dwelling or other premises, is not an exceeding of the limits of necessary defense and does not entail criminal liability, regardless of the severity of the harm inflicted on the encroacher.
1. Imaginary defense means actions related to inflicting harm in circumstances where there was no real socially dangerous encroachment, and the person, incorrectly assessing the actions of the victim, only mistakenly assumed the existence of such encroachment.
2. Imaginary defense excludes criminal liability for the harm inflicted only in cases where the circumstances that arose gave the person sufficient grounds to believe that there was a real encroachment, and they did not realize and could not realize the erroneousness of their assumption.
3. If the person did not realize and could not realize the erroneousness of their assumption, but at the same time exceeded the limits of defense permitted in the conditions of a corresponding real encroachment, they are subject to criminal liability for exceeding the limits of necessary defense.
4. If in the circumstances that arose the person did not realize but could have realized the absence of a real socially dangerous encroachment, they are subject to criminal liability for inflicting harm through negligence.

Стаття 38. Apprehension of a person who has committed a criminal offense

1. Actions of the victim and other persons immediately after the commission of an encroachment, aimed at apprehending a person who has committed a criminal offense and delivering them to the relevant authorities, are not recognized as criminally unlawful if the measures necessary for apprehending such person were not exceeded.
2. Exceeding the measures necessary for apprehending a person who has committed a criminal offense means intentionally inflicting on the person who committed a criminal offense serious harm that clearly does not correspond to the danger of the encroachment or the circumstances of apprehending the person who committed the criminal offense. Exceeding the measures necessary for apprehending a person who has committed a criminal offense entails liability only in cases specifically provided for in Articles 118 and 124 of this Code.
{Article 38 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 39. Extreme necessity

1. Inflicting harm on legally protected interests in a state of extreme necessity is not a criminal offense, that is, to eliminate a danger that directly threatens a person or legally protected rights of this person or other persons, as well as public interests or interests of the state, if this danger in the given situation could not be eliminated by other means and if the limits of extreme necessity were not exceeded.
2. Exceeding the limits of extreme necessity means intentionally inflicting harm on legally protected interests if such harm is more significant than the harm averted.
3. A person is not subject to criminal liability for exceeding the limits of extreme necessity if, as a result of strong emotional disturbance caused by the threatening danger, they could not assess the correspondence of the harm inflicted to this danger.
{Article 39 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 40. Physical or mental coercion

1. An action or inaction of a person that inflicted harm on legally protected interests, committed under the direct influence of physical coercion as a result of which the person could not control their actions, is not a criminal offense.
2. The issue of criminal liability of a person for inflicting harm on legally protected interests, if this person was subjected to physical coercion as a result of which they retained the ability to control their actions, as well as mental coercion, is resolved in accordance with the provisions of Article 39 of this Code.
{Article 40 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 41. Execution of an order or instruction

1. An act or omission of a person that caused harm to legally protected interests is recognized as lawful if it was committed for the purpose of executing a lawful order or instruction.
2. An order or instruction is lawful if it was issued by the appropriate person in the proper manner and within their authority and does not contradict current legislation in content and is not related to the violation of constitutional rights and freedoms of man and citizen.
3. A person who refused to execute a manifestly criminally unlawful order or instruction is not subject to criminal liability.
4. A person who executed a manifestly criminally unlawful order or instruction is subject to criminal liability on general grounds for acts committed for the purpose of executing such order or instruction.
5. If a person did not realize and could not have realized the criminally unlawful nature of the order or instruction, then for acts committed for the purpose of executing such order or instruction, only the person who issued the criminally unlawful order or instruction is subject to liability.
{Article 41 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 42. Act related to risk

1. An act (action or omission) that caused harm to legally protected interests is not a criminal offense if this act was committed under conditions of justified risk to achieve a significant socially beneficial goal.
2. Risk is recognized as justified if the goal that was set could not be achieved in the given situation by an action (omission) not associated with risk, and the person who took the risk reasonably expected that the measures taken by them were sufficient to avert harm to legally protected interests.
3. Risk is not recognized as justified if it knowingly created a threat to the lives of other people or a threat of environmental catastrophe or other extraordinary events.
{Article 42 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 43

Execution of a special assignment for the prevention or detection of criminally unlawful activity of an organized group or criminal organization
1. Forced causing of harm to legally protected interests by a person who, in accordance with the law, was executing a special assignment by participating in an organized group or criminal organization for the purpose of preventing or detecting their criminally unlawful activity is not a criminal offense.
2. The person specified in part one of this article is subject to criminal liability only for committing, as part of an organized group or criminal organization, a particularly serious crime committed intentionally and combined with violence against the victim, or a serious crime committed intentionally and related to causing serious bodily injury to the victim or the occurrence of other serious or particularly serious consequences.
3. A person who committed a crime provided for in part two of this article may not be sentenced to life imprisonment, and punishment in the form of imprisonment may not be imposed on them for a term exceeding half of the maximum term of imprisonment provided by law for this crime.
{Article 43 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 43-1

Fulfillment of the duty to defend the Motherland, independence and territorial integrity of Ukraine
1. An act (action or omission) committed under conditions of martial law or during an armed conflict and aimed at repelling and deterring armed aggression of the Russian Federation or aggression of another country is not a criminal offense if it caused harm to the life or health of a person carrying out such aggression, or caused harm to legally protected interests, in the absence of signs of torture or use of means of warfare prohibited by international law, other violations of the laws and customs of war provided for by international treaties, the binding nature of which has been approved by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine.
2. Every person has the right to defend the Motherland, independence and territorial integrity of Ukraine regardless of the possibility of avoiding confrontation, causing harm or seeking assistance from other persons or state authorities, the Armed Forces of Ukraine.
3. A person is not subject to criminal liability for the use of weapons (armament), ammunition or explosives against persons carrying out armed aggression against Ukraine, and for damage or destruction of property in connection with this.
4. An act (action or omission) aimed at repelling and deterring armed aggression of the Russian Federation or aggression of another country is not considered fulfillment of the duty to defend the Motherland, independence and territorial integrity of Ukraine if it clearly does not correspond to the danger of aggression or the situation of repelling and deterring, was not necessary to achieve a significant socially beneficial goal in a specific situation and created a threat to the lives of other people or a threat of environmental catastrophe or the occurrence of other extraordinary events of a larger scale.
{Section VIII supplemented with Article 43-1 according to Law No. 2124-IX of 15.03.2022}

Section IX

RELEASE FROM CRIMINAL LIABILITY

Article 44. Legal grounds and procedure for release from criminal liability

1. A person who committed a criminal offense is released from criminal liability in cases provided for by this Code.
2. Release from criminal liability in cases provided for by this Code is carried out exclusively by a court. The procedure for release from criminal liability is established by law.
{Article 44 as amended according to Laws No. 3465-VI of 02.06.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 45. Release from criminal liability in connection with active repentance

A person who committed a criminal misdemeanor or a negligent minor crime for the first time, except for corruption criminal offenses or criminal offenses related to corruption, violations of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medications that reduce attention and reaction speed, is released from criminal liability if, after committing the criminal offense, they sincerely repented, actively contributed to the detection of the criminal offense and fully compensated for the losses caused by them or eliminated the harm caused.
Note. Corruption criminal offenses under this Code are considered to be criminal offenses provided for in Articles 191, 262, 308, 312, 313, 320, 357, 410, in case of their commission by abuse of official position, as well as criminal offenses provided for in Articles 210, 354, 364, 364-1, 365-2, 368-369-2 of this Code.
Criminal offenses related to corruption under this Code are considered to be criminal offenses provided for in Articles 366-2, 366-3 of this Code.
{Article 45 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015; as amended according to Law No. 140-IX of 02.10.2019, No. 524-IX of 04.03.2020; as worded by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018 taking into account amendments introduced by Law No. 321-IX of 03.12.2019; as amended according to Laws No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 46

Release from criminal liability in connection with reconciliation of the guilty person with the victim
A person who has committed a criminal misdemeanor or a negligent minor crime for the first time, except for corruption criminal offenses, criminal offenses related to corruption, violations of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, shall be released from criminal liability if such person has reconciled with the victim and compensated the damages caused by such person or eliminated the harm caused.
{Article 46 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 47

Release from criminal liability in connection with transfer of a person on bail
1. A person who has committed a criminal misdemeanor or a minor crime for the first time, except for corruption criminal offenses, criminal offenses related to corruption, violations of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, and has sincerely repented, may be released from criminal liability with transfer of such person on bail to a collective of an enterprise, institution or organization upon their petition, provided that such person within one year from the day of transfer on bail justifies the trust of the collective, does not evade educational measures and does not violate public order.
2. In case of violation of the conditions of transfer on bail, the person shall be brought to criminal liability for the criminal offense committed by such person.
{Article 47 as amended according to Laws No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 48

Release from criminal liability in connection with change of circumstances
A person who has committed a criminal misdemeanor or a minor crime for the first time, except for corruption criminal offenses, criminal offenses related to corruption, violations of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, may be released from criminal liability if it is recognized that at the time of criminal proceedings, as a result of a change of circumstances, the act committed by such person has lost its public danger or this person has ceased to be publicly dangerous.
{Article 48 as amended according to Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 49

Release from criminal liability in connection with expiration of limitation periods
1. A person shall be released from criminal liability if from the day of commission of a criminal offense by such person until the day when the judgment enters into legal force the following periods have elapsed:
1) two years - in case of commission of a criminal misdemeanor for which a punishment less severe than restriction of liberty is provided;
2) three years - in case of commission of a criminal misdemeanor for which punishment in the form of restriction of liberty is provided, or in case of commission of a minor crime for which punishment in the form of imprisonment for a term not exceeding two years is provided;
3) five years - in case of commission of a minor crime, except for the case provided for in point 2 of this part;
4) ten years - in case of commission of a grave crime;
5) fifteen years - in case of commission of a particularly grave crime.
2. The running of the limitation period shall be suspended if the person who committed a criminal offense evaded pre-trial investigation or court. In these cases, the running of the limitation period shall resume from the day of appearance of the person with confession or detention of such person, and from the time of commission of a criminal misdemeanor - five years. In this case, the person shall be released from criminal liability if fifteen years have elapsed since the commission of the criminal offense.
3. The running of the limitation period shall be interrupted if before the expiration of the periods specified in parts one and two of this Article the person committed a new crime, except for a minor crime for which punishment in the form of imprisonment for a term not exceeding two years is provided.
4. The issue of application of the limitation period to a person who committed a particularly grave crime for which, according to the law, life imprisonment may be imposed, shall be decided by the court. If the court does not consider it possible to apply the limitation period, life imprisonment may not be imposed and shall be replaced by imprisonment for a definite term.
5. The limitation period shall not apply in case of commission of crimes against the foundations of national security of Ukraine provided for in Articles 109-114-2, torture provided for in part three of Article 127, against peace and security of mankind provided for in Articles 437-439 and part one of Article 442 of this Code.
6. In case of commission of a criminal offense provided for in Articles 151-2-156-1, 301-1-303 of this Code against a minor or juvenile person, the calculation of the periods defined by parts one and two of this Article shall begin from the day when such victim reached the age of majority or, in case of death of such person, would have reached the age of majority.
{Article 49 as amended according to Laws No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013, No. 1183-VII of 08.04.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021, No. 2160-IX of 24.03.2022, No. 2812-IX of 01.12.2022}

Розділ X

PUNISHMENT AND ITS TYPES

Стаття 50. Concept of punishment and its purpose

1. Punishment is a measure of coercion applied on behalf of the state by a court judgment to a person found guilty of committing a criminal offense, and consists in the restriction of rights and freedoms of the convicted person provided for by law.
2. Punishment has as its purpose not only retribution, but also correction of convicted persons, as well as prevention of commission of new criminal offenses both by convicted persons and by other persons.
3. Punishment does not have as its purpose infliction of physical suffering or humiliation of human dignity.
{Article 50 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
To persons found guilty of committing a criminal offense, the court may apply the following types of punishments:
2) deprivation of military, special rank, grade, title or qualification class;
3) deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities;
6) service restrictions for military servicemen;
10) detention in a disciplinary battalion of military servicemen;
11) imprisonment for a definite term;
{Article 51 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 52. Principal and Additional Punishments

1. The principal punishments are community service, correctional labour, service restrictions for military personnel, arrest, restriction of liberty, detention in a disciplinary battalion for military personnel, deprivation of liberty for a definite term, life imprisonment.
2. The additional punishments are deprivation of military or special rank, grade, title or qualification class and confiscation of property.
3. A fine and deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities may be applied both as principal and as additional punishments.
4. For one criminal offence, only one principal punishment provided for in the sanction of an article (sanction of a part of an article) of the Special Part of this Code may be imposed. One or several additional punishments may be added to the principal punishment in the cases and manner provided for by this Code.
5. Evasion of punishment imposed by a court sentence entails liability provided for by Articles 389 and 390 of this Code.
{Article 52 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. A fine is a monetary penalty imposed by a court in the cases and amount established in the Special Part of this Code, taking into account the provisions of part two of this article.
2. The amount of the fine is determined by the court depending on the gravity of the criminal offence committed and taking into account the property status of the guilty person within the range from thirty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens to fifty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, unless a higher amount of fine is provided for by articles of the Special Part of this Code. For committing a criminal offence for which the principal punishment in the form of a fine exceeding three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens is provided, the amount of the fine imposed by the court may not be less than the amount of property damage caused by the criminal offence or income received as a result of committing the criminal offence, regardless of the maximum amount of the fine provided for by the sanction of an article (sanction of a part of an article) of the Special Part of this Code. If the court establishes that such a criminal offence was committed in complicity and the role of the perpetrator (co-perpetrator), instigator or accomplice in its commission is insignificant, it may impose on such persons a punishment in the form of a fine in the amount provided for by the sanction of an article (sanction of a part of an article) of the Special Part of this Code, without taking into account the amount of property damage caused by the criminal offence or income received as a result of committing the criminal offence.
3. A fine as an additional punishment may be imposed only when it is specifically provided for in the sanction of an article (sanction of a part of an article) of the Special Part of this Code.
4. Taking into account the property status of a person, the court may impose a fine with instalment payment in certain parts for a term of up to one year.
5. In case of non-payment of a fine in the amount of not more than three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens and absence of grounds for instalment of its payment, the court shall replace the unpaid amount of the fine with punishment in the form of community service at the rate of one hour of community service per one non-taxable minimum income of citizens established by law, or correctional labour at the rate of one month of correctional labour per twenty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens established by law, but for a term of not more than two years.
In case of non-payment of a fine in the amount exceeding three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, imposed as a principal punishment, and absence of grounds for instalment of its payment, the court shall replace the unpaid amount of the fine with punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty at the rate of one day of deprivation of liberty per eight non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens within the following limits:
1) from one to five years of deprivation of liberty - in case of imposing a fine for committing a minor crime;
2) from five to ten years of deprivation of liberty - in case of imposing a fine for committing a serious crime;
3) from ten to twelve years of deprivation of liberty - in case of imposing a fine for committing a particularly serious crime.
If, when calculating the term of deprivation of liberty, this term exceeds the limits established by this part of the article, the court shall replace the punishment in the form of a fine with punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for the maximum term provided for a crime of the corresponding gravity by this part of the article.
{Article 53 as amended in accordance with Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as worded by Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 54

Deprivation of Military or Special Rank, Grade, Title or Qualification Class
A person convicted of a serious or particularly serious crime who holds a military or special rank, grade, title or qualification class may be deprived by court sentence of this rank, grade, title or qualification class.

Article 55. Deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities

1. Deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities may be imposed as a principal punishment for a term of two to five years or as an additional punishment for a term of one to three years.
Deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions as an additional punishment in cases provided for by the Law of Ukraine "On Lustration" shall be imposed for a term of five years.
Deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities as an additional punishment for committing a criminal offense against electoral rights and freedoms of a citizen, provided for in Articles 157-160 of this Code, shall be imposed for a term of five years.
Deprivation of the right to drive vehicles as an additional punishment shall be imposed for a term of up to ten years.
Deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities as a principal or additional punishment for committing criminal offenses against the foundations of national security of Ukraine, provided for in Articles 111-1, 111-2 of this Code, shall be imposed for a term of ten to fifteen years.
2. Deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities as an additional punishment may be imposed even in cases where it is not provided for in the sanction of the article (sanction of part of the article) of the Special Part of this Code, provided that, taking into account the nature of the criminal offense committed in office or in connection with engaging in certain activities, the personality of the convicted person and other circumstances of the case, the court deems it impossible to preserve his right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities.
3. When imposing deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities as an additional punishment to arrest, restriction of liberty, detention in a disciplinary battalion of military servicemen or deprivation of liberty for a definite term - it shall extend to the entire period of serving the principal punishment and, in addition, for the term established by the court judgment that has entered into legal force. In this case, the term of additional punishment shall be calculated from the moment of serving the principal punishment, and when imposing punishment in the form of deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities as additional to other principal punishments, as well as in case of application of Article 77 of this Code - from the moment the judgment enters into legal force.
{Article 55 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1682-VII of 16.09.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 2108-IX of 03.03.2022, No. 2198-IX of 14.04.2022}
1. Community service consists in the performance by the convicted person, during time free from work or study, of unpaid socially useful work, the type of which is determined by local self-government bodies.
2. Community service is established for a term of sixty to two hundred and forty hours and is served no more than four hours per day.
3. Community service shall not be imposed on persons recognized as persons with disabilities of the first or second group, pregnant women, persons who have reached retirement age, as well as military servicemen of compulsory service.
{Article 56 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2581-VIII of 02.10.2018}
1. Punishment in the form of correctional labor is established for a term of six months to two years and is served at the place of work of the convicted person. From the earnings of the person convicted to correctional labor, a deduction is made to the income of the state in the amount established by the court judgment, within the limits of ten to twenty percent.
2. Correctional labor shall not be applied to pregnant women and women who are on maternity leave to care for a child, to incapacitated persons, to persons who have not reached sixteen years of age and those who have reached retirement age, as well as to military servicemen, persons of rank and file and command staff of the State Service for Special Communications and Information Protection of Ukraine, law enforcement officers, notaries, private executors, judges, prosecutors, lawyers, civil servants, officials of local self-government bodies.
3. For persons who became incapacitated after the court judgment was rendered, correctional labor may be replaced by the court with a fine calculated at three tax-free minimum incomes of citizens established by law for one month of correctional labor.
{Article 57 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1180-VI of 19.03.2009, No. 1403-VIII of 02.06.2016}

Article 58. Service restrictions for military servicemen

1. Punishment in the form of service restriction shall be applied to convicted military servicemen, except military servicemen of compulsory service, for a term of six months to two years in cases provided for by this Code, as well as in cases where the court, taking into account the circumstances of the case and the personality of the convicted person, deems it possible instead of restriction of liberty or deprivation of liberty for a term of no more than two years to impose service restriction for the same term.
2. From the monetary allowance of the person convicted to service restriction, a deduction is made to the income of the state in the amount established by the court judgment, within the limits of ten to twenty percent. During the serving of this punishment, the convicted person may not be promoted in position, in military rank, and the term of punishment shall not be credited to him in the length of service for awarding the next military rank.
1. Punishment in the form of confiscation of property consists in the compulsory gratuitous seizure into state ownership of all or part of the property that is the property of the convicted person. If part of the property is confiscated, the court must indicate which part of the property is confiscated, or list the items to be confiscated.
2. Confiscation of property is established for serious and particularly serious mercenary crimes, as well as for crimes against the foundations of national security of Ukraine and public safety regardless of their degree of gravity and may be imposed only in cases specifically provided for in the Special Part of this Code.
3. The list of property not subject to confiscation is determined by the law of Ukraine.
{Article 59 as amended in accordance with Law No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014}
1. Punishment in the form of arrest consists in keeping the convicted person in conditions of isolation and is established for a term of one to six months.
2. Military servicemen serve arrest in a guardhouse.
3. Arrest shall not be applied to persons under sixteen years of age, pregnant women and women who have children under seven years of age.
1. Punishment in the form of restriction of liberty consists in keeping a person in criminal-executive institutions of open type without isolation from society under conditions of supervision over her with mandatory involvement of the convicted person in labor.
2. Restriction of liberty is established for a term of one to five years.
3. Restriction of liberty shall not be applied to minors, pregnant women and women who have children under fourteen years of age, to persons who have reached retirement age, military servicemen of compulsory service and to persons with disabilities of the first and second group.
{Article 61 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2581-VIII of 02.10.2018}

Article 62. Detention in a disciplinary battalion of military servicepersons

1. Punishment in the form of detention in a disciplinary battalion is assigned to military servicepersons of compulsory service, military servicepersons serving under contract, persons of officer rank serving career military service, persons of officer rank serving military service by conscription, military servicepersons called up for military service during mobilization, for a special period, military servicepersons called up for military service by conscription of persons from among reservists in a special period (except female military servicepersons), for a term from six months to two years in cases provided for by this Code, and also if the court, taking into account the circumstances of the case and the personality of the convicted person, considers it possible to replace imprisonment for a term of not more than two years with detention in a disciplinary battalion for the same term.
2. Detention in a disciplinary battalion of military servicepersons instead of imprisonment may not be applied to persons who have previously served a sentence in the form of imprisonment.
{Article 62 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 158-VIII of 05.02.2015, No. 1357-IX of 30.03.2021}

Article 63. Imprisonment for a definite term

1. Punishment in the form of imprisonment consists in the isolation of the convicted person and his or her placement for a definite term in a closed-type penal institution.
2. Imprisonment is established for a term from one to fifteen years, except in cases provided for by the General Part of this Code.
{Article 63 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1254-VI of 14.04.2009}

Article 64. Life imprisonment

1. Life imprisonment is established for the commission of particularly grave crimes and is applied only in cases specifically provided for by this Code, if the court does not consider it possible to apply imprisonment for a definite term.
2. Life imprisonment is not applied to persons who committed crimes under the age of 18 and to persons over the age of 65, as well as to women who were pregnant at the time of committing the crime or at the time of pronouncement of the sentence, and also in the case provided for by part four of Article 68 of this Code.
{Article 64 as amended in accordance with Law No. 1492-VIII of 07.09.2016}

Section XI

IMPOSITION OF PUNISHMENT

Article 65. General principles of imposition of punishment

1) within the limits established in the sanction of the article (sanction of the part of the article) of the Special Part of this Code, which provides for liability for the committed criminal offence, except in cases provided for by part two of Article 53 of this Code;
2) in accordance with the provisions of the General Part of this Code;
3) taking into account the degree of gravity of the committed criminal offence, the personality of the guilty person and circumstances that mitigate and aggravate punishment.
2. A person who has committed a criminal offence must be assigned a punishment necessary and sufficient for his or her correction and prevention of new criminal offences. A more severe type of punishment from among those provided for the committed criminal offence is assigned only if a less severe type of punishment would be insufficient for the correction of the person and prevention of his or her commission of new criminal offences.
3. The grounds for imposition of a more lenient punishment than provided for by the relevant article of the Special Part of this Code for the committed criminal offence are determined by Article 69 of this Code.
4. A more severe punishment than provided for by the relevant articles of the Special Part of this Code for the committed criminal offence may be imposed in the case of a cumulation of criminal offences and a cumulation of sentences in accordance with Articles 70 and 71 of this Code.
5. In the case of approval by a sentence of an agreement on reconciliation or on admission of guilt, the court imposes the punishment agreed by the parties to the agreement.
{Article 65 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 66. Circumstances that mitigate punishment

1. When imposing punishment, circumstances that mitigate it are recognized as:
1) appearance with confession, sincere repentance or active assistance in solving the criminal offence;
2) voluntary compensation for the damage caused or elimination of the harm inflicted;
2-1) provision of medical or other assistance to the victim immediately after the commission of the criminal offence;
3) commission of a criminal offence by a minor;
4) commission of a criminal offence by a woman in a state of pregnancy;
5) commission of a criminal offence as a result of a combination of grave personal, family or other circumstances;
6) commission of a criminal offence under the influence of a threat, coercion or due to material, official or other dependence;
7) commission of a criminal offence under the influence of strong emotional disturbance caused by cruel treatment, or treatment that degrades the honour and dignity of the person, and also in the presence of a systematic nature of such treatment by the victim;
8) commission of a criminal offence with exceeding the limits of extreme necessity;
9) performance of a special task for the prevention or solving of criminal activity of an organized group or criminal organization, combined with the commission of a criminal offence in cases provided for by this Code.
2. When imposing punishment, the court may recognize as mitigating it other circumstances not specified in part one of this Article.
3. If any of the circumstances that mitigate punishment is provided for in an article of the Special Part of this Code as a feature of a criminal offence that affects its qualification, the court may not take it into account again when imposing punishment as one that mitigates it.
{Article 66 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 67. Circumstances that Aggravate Punishment

1. When imposing punishment, the circumstances that aggravate it are recognized as:
1) commission of a crime by a person repeatedly and recidivism of crimes;
2) commission of a criminal offense by a group of persons by prior conspiracy (part two or three of Article 28);
3) commission of a criminal offense on the grounds of racial, national, religious hatred or discord or on the grounds of sex;
4) commission of a criminal offense in connection with the victim's performance of official or public duty;
5) grave consequences caused by the crime;
6) commission of a criminal offense against an elderly person, a person with a disability or a person who is in a helpless state, or a person suffering from a mental disorder, including feeble-mindedness, having defects of mental development, as well as commission of a criminal offense against a minor child or in the presence of a child;
6-1) commission of a criminal offense against a spouse or former spouse or another person with whom the guilty person is (was) in family or close relations;
7) commission of a criminal offense against a woman who was knowingly to the guilty person in a state of pregnancy;
8) commission of a criminal offense against a person who is in material, official or other dependence on the guilty person;
9) commission of a criminal offense using a minor or a person suffering from mental illness or feeble-mindedness;
10) commission of a crime with particular cruelty;
11) commission of a crime using the conditions of martial or emergency state, other emergency events;
12) commission of a crime by a generally dangerous method;
13) commission of a criminal offense by a person who is in a state of alcohol intoxication or in a state caused by the use of narcotic or other intoxicating substances.
2. The court has the right, depending on the nature of the criminal offense committed, not to recognize any of the circumstances specified in part one of this Article, with the exception of the circumstances specified in points 2, 6, 6-1, 7, 9, 10, 12 as one that aggravates punishment, having stated the reasons for its decision in the verdict.
3. When imposing punishment, the court may not recognize as aggravating it circumstances not specified in part one of this Article.
4. If any of the circumstances that aggravate punishment is provided for in an Article of the Special Part of this Code as a feature of a criminal offense that affects its qualification, the court may not take it into account again when imposing punishment as one that aggravates it.
{Article 67 as amended according to Laws No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 68

Imposition of Punishment for an Uncompleted Criminal Offense and for a Criminal Offense Committed in Complicity
1. When imposing punishment for an uncompleted criminal offense, the court, guided by the provisions of Articles 65-67 of this Code, takes into account the degree of gravity of the act committed by the person, the degree of implementation of the criminally unlawful intent and the reasons due to which the criminal offense was not brought to completion.
2. For commission of preparation for a crime, the term or amount of punishment may not exceed half of the maximum term or amount of the most severe type of punishment provided for by the sanction of the Article (sanction of the part of the Article) of the Special Part of this Code.
3. For commission of an attempt at a criminal offense, the term or amount of punishment may not exceed two thirds of the maximum term or amount of the most severe type of punishment provided for by the sanction of the Article (sanction of the part of the Article) of the Special Part of this Code.
4. Life imprisonment for commission of preparation for a crime and commission of an attempt at a crime is not applied, except in cases of commission of crimes against the foundations of national security of Ukraine provided for in Articles 110, 111, 111-1, 112, 113, against peace, security of humanity and international legal order provided for in Articles 437-439, part one of Article 442 and Article 443 of this Code.
5. When imposing punishment on accomplices of a criminal offense, the court, guided by the provisions of Articles 65-67 of this Code, takes into account the nature and degree of participation of each of them in the commission of the criminal offense.
{Article 68 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1492-VIII of 07.09.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2160-IX of 24.03.2022, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Article 69. Imposition of a Milder Punishment than Provided for by Law

1. In the presence of several circumstances that mitigate punishment and substantially reduce the degree of gravity of the criminal offense committed, taking into account the personality of the guilty person, the court, having motivated its decision, may, except in cases of conviction for a corruption criminal offense, a criminal offense related to corruption, a criminal offense provided for in Articles 403, 405, 407, 408, 429 of this Code committed under conditions of martial law or in combat situation, for torture committed by a representative of the state, including a foreign one, impose a principal punishment below the lowest limit established in the sanction of the Article (sanction of the part of the Article) of the Special Part of this Code, or move to another, milder type of principal punishment not specified in the sanction of the Article (sanction of the part of the Article) of the Special Part of this Code for this criminal offense. In this case, the court does not have the right to impose a punishment below the lowest limit established for such type of punishment in the General Part of this Code. For commission of a criminal offense for which a principal punishment in the form of a fine in the amount of more than three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens is provided, the court on the grounds provided for in this part may impose a principal punishment in the form of a fine, the amount of which is no more than one quarter lower than the lowest limit established in the sanction of the Article (sanction of the part of the Article) of the Special Part of this Code.
2. On the grounds provided for in part one of this Article, the court may not impose an additional punishment that is provided for in the sanction of the Article (sanction of the part of the Article) of the Special Part of this Code as mandatory, except in cases of imposition of punishment for commission of a criminal offense for which a principal punishment in the form of a fine in the amount of more than three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens is provided.
{Article 69 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021, No. 2812-IX of 01.12.2022, No. 2839-IX of 13.12.2022}

Article 69-1. Imposition of Punishment in the Presence of Circumstances that Mitigate Punishment

1.
