In the presence of circumstances that mitigate punishment provided for in points 1 and 2 of part one of Article 66 of this Code, absence of circumstances that aggravate punishment, as well as upon the accused's acknowledgment of his guilt, the term or amount of punishment may not exceed two thirds of the maximum term or amount of the most severe type of punishment provided for by the corresponding sanction of the Article (sanction of the part of the Article) of the Special Part of this Code.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 69-1 according to Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as amended according to Law No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013}

Article 70. Imposition of Punishment for Aggregate of Criminal Offenses

1. In case of an aggregate of criminal offenses, the court, having imposed punishment (principal and additional) for each criminal offense separately, determines the final punishment by absorption of a less severe punishment by a more severe one or by complete or partial addition of the imposed punishments.
2. When adding punishments, the final punishment for an aggregate of criminal offenses is determined within the limits established by the sanction of the article (sanction of the part of the article) of the Special Part of this Code that provides for a more severe punishment. If at least one of the criminal offenses is an intentional grave or especially grave crime, the court may impose a final punishment for an aggregate of criminal offenses within the limits of the maximum term established for the given type of punishment in the General Part of this Code. If for at least one of the committed criminal offenses life imprisonment has been imposed, the final punishment for an aggregate of criminal offenses is determined by absorption of any less severe punishments by life imprisonment.
3. To the principal punishment imposed for an aggregate of criminal offenses, additional punishments imposed by the court for criminal offenses of which the person was found guilty may be added.
4. According to the rules provided for in parts one through three of this Article, punishment is imposed if after the pronouncement of a sentence in a case it is established that the convicted person is also guilty of another criminal offense committed by him or her before the pronouncement of the previous sentence. In this case, the punishment served in full or in part under the previous sentence is credited toward the term of punishment finally imposed for the aggregate of criminal offenses, according to the rules provided for in Article 72 of this Code.
{Article 70 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 71. Imposition of Punishment for Aggregate of Sentences

1. If a convicted person after the pronouncement of a sentence but before complete serving of the punishment has committed a new criminal offense, the court adds, in full or in part, the unserved part of the punishment under the previous sentence to the punishment imposed under the new sentence.
2. When adding punishments for an aggregate of sentences, the total term of punishment may not exceed the maximum term established for the given type of punishment in the General Part of this Code. When adding punishments in the form of imprisonment, the total term of punishment finally imposed for an aggregate of sentences must not exceed fifteen years, and in the event that at least one of the criminal offenses is an especially grave crime, the total term of imprisonment may be more than fifteen years but must not exceed twenty-five years. When adding punishments in the form of life imprisonment and any less severe punishments, the total term of punishment finally imposed for an aggregate of sentences is determined by absorption of less severe punishments by life imprisonment.
3. An additional punishment imposed under at least one of the sentences or its unserved part under the previous sentence is subject to addition to the principal punishment finally imposed for an aggregate of sentences.
4. The final punishment for an aggregate of sentences, except in cases where it is determined by absorption of one punishment by another imposed in the maximum amount, must be greater than the punishment imposed for the new criminal offense, as well as than the unserved part of the punishment under the previous sentence.
5. If a convicted person after the pronouncement of a sentence but before complete serving of the punishment has committed two or more criminal offenses, the court imposes punishment for these new criminal offenses according to the rules provided for in Article 70 of this Code, and then adds, in full or in part, the unserved part of the punishment under the previous sentence to the final punishment imposed for the aggregate of criminal offenses within the limits established in part two of this Article.
{Article 71 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 72. Rules for Adding Punishments and Crediting the Term of Pre-trial Detention

1. When adding punishments for an aggregate of criminal offenses and an aggregate of sentences, a less severe type of punishment is converted into a more severe type based on the following correlation:
1) one day of imprisonment corresponds to:
a) one day of detention in a disciplinary battalion for military servicemen or arrest;
v) three days of service restriction for military servicemen or three days of correctional labor;
h) eight hours of community service;
2) one day of detention in a disciplinary battalion for military servicemen or arrest corresponds to:
b) three days of service restriction for military servicemen or three days of correctional labor;
3) one day of restriction of liberty corresponds to three days of service restriction for military servicemen or three days of correctional labor;
4) one day of restriction of liberty or arrest corresponds to eight hours of community service.
2. When imposing punishment for an aggregate of criminal offenses or sentences in the form of correctional labor or service restrictions for military servicemen, only the terms of these punishments are subject to addition. The amounts of deductions from the convicted person's earnings are not subject to addition and are calculated separately for each sentence.
3. Principal punishments in the form of a fine and deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities when imposed for an aggregate of criminal offenses and for an aggregate of sentences are not subject to addition with other types of punishments and are executed independently.
4. Additional punishments of different types are in all cases executed independently.
5. Pre-trial detention is credited by the court toward the term of punishment in case of sentencing to imprisonment day for day or according to the rules provided for in part one of this Article. When imposing punishments not specified in part one of this Article, the court, taking into account pre-trial detention, may mitigate the punishment or completely release the convicted person from serving it.
6. The rules for correlation of types of punishments established in part one of this Article may be applied in other cases provided for by the General Part of this Code.
{Article 72 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 838-VIII of 26.11.2015, No. 2046-VIII of 18.05.2017, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 73. Calculation of Terms of Punishment

Terms of punishment are calculated respectively in years, months and hours. When replacing or adding punishments, as well as in case of crediting pre-trial detention, calculation of terms of punishment in days is permitted.

Chapter XII

RELEASE FROM PUNISHMENT AND ITS SERVING

Стаття 74. Release from Punishment and Its Serving

1. Release of a convicted person from punishment or its further serving, replacement with a milder one, as well as mitigation of the imposed punishment, except for release from punishment or mitigation of punishment on the basis of the law of Ukraine on amnesty or an act of pardon, may be applied only by a court in cases provided for by this Code.
2. A person convicted of an act the punishability of which has been eliminated by law is subject to immediate release from the punishment imposed by the court.
3. The measure of punishment imposed on a convicted person that exceeds the sanction of the new law is reduced to the maximum limit of punishment established by the sanction of the new law. If such limit provides for a milder type of punishment, the punishment served by the convicted person is credited with recalculation according to the rules established by part one of Article 72 of this Code.
4. A person who has committed a criminal misdemeanor or a minor crime, except for corruption criminal offenses, criminal offenses related to corruption, violations of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, may be released from punishment by a court verdict if it is recognized that, taking into account impeccable behavior and conscientious attitude to work, this person at the time of consideration of the case in court cannot be considered socially dangerous.
5. A person may also be released from punishment by a court verdict on the grounds provided for in Article 49 of this Code.
{Article 74 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 75. Release from Serving Punishment with Probation

1. If the court, except in cases of conviction for a corruption criminal offense, a criminal offense related to corruption, a criminal offense provided for in Articles 403, 405, 407, 408, 429 of this Code, committed under martial law or in combat conditions, violation of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, torture provided for in part three of Article 127 of this Code, when imposing punishment in the form of correctional labor, service restriction for military personnel, restriction of liberty, as well as imprisonment for a term not exceeding five years, taking into account the gravity of the criminal offense, the person of the guilty party and other circumstances of the case, reaches a conclusion about the possibility of correction of the convicted person without serving the punishment, it may make a decision on release from serving punishment with probation.
2. The court makes a decision on release from serving punishment with probation in case of approval of a reconciliation agreement or a plea agreement, if the parties to the agreement have agreed on punishment in the form of correctional labor, service restriction for military personnel, restriction of liberty, imprisonment for a term not exceeding five years, and have also agreed on release from serving punishment with probation.
3. In the cases provided for in parts one and two of this Article, the court decides to release the convicted person from serving the imposed punishment if during the determined probation period he or she does not commit a new criminal offense and fulfills the obligations imposed on him or her. The duration of the probation period and the obligations imposed on a person released from serving punishment with probation are determined by the court.
4. The probation period is established by the court for a duration from one year to three years.
{Article 75 as amended according to Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021, No. 2812-IX of 01.12.2022, No. 2839-IX of 13.12.2022}

Стаття 76

Obligations Imposed by the Court on a Person Released from Serving Punishment with Probation
1. In case of release from serving punishment with probation, the court imposes the following obligations on the convicted person:
1) to appear periodically for registration with the authorized body on probation matters;
2) to notify the authorized body on probation matters of a change of place of residence, work or study.
2. On persons convicted of crimes related to domestic violence, the court may impose other obligations and prohibitions provided for in Article 91-1 of this Code
3. On persons released from serving punishment with probation, the court may additionally impose the following obligations:
1) to ask for forgiveness from the victim publicly or in another form;
2) not to leave the territory of Ukraine without agreement with the authorized body on probation matters;
3) to obtain employment or, upon referral by the authorized body on probation matters, to apply to state employment service bodies for registration as unemployed and to obtain employment if an appropriate position (work) is offered;
4) to perform measures provided for by the probation program;
5) to undergo a course of treatment for mental and behavioral disorders resulting from the use of psychoactive substances or a disease that poses a danger to the health of other persons;
6) to comply with requirements established by the court regarding the performance of certain actions, restrictions on communication, movement and leisure activities.
On a person released from serving punishment with probation, the court imposes the obligations provided for in part two of this Article that are necessary and sufficient for his or her correction, taking into account the degree of gravity of the crime committed, the person of the guilty party and circumstances that mitigate or aggravate punishment.
4. Supervision over persons released from serving punishment with probation is carried out by the authorized body on probation matters at the place of residence, work or study of the convicted person, and with respect to convicted military personnel - by commanders of military units.
{Article 76 as amended according to Law No. 1254-VI of 14.04.2009; as worded by Law No. 1492-VIII of 07.09.2016 - provisions regarding the application of probation programs enter into force on January 1, 2018; as amended according to Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017}

Стаття 77

Application of Additional Punishments in Case of Release from Serving the Main Punishment with Probation
In case of release from serving punishment with probation, additional punishments in the form of a fine, deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities and deprivation of military, special rank, grade, title or qualification class may be imposed.

Стаття 78. Legal Consequences of Release from Serving Punishment with Probation

1. After the expiration of the probation period, a convicted person who has fulfilled the obligations imposed on him or her and has not committed a new criminal offense is released by the court from the punishment imposed on him or her.
2. If a convicted person does not fulfill the obligations imposed on him or her or systematically commits offenses that have resulted in administrative penalties and indicate his or her unwillingness to embark on the path of correction, the court sends the convicted person to serve the imposed punishment.
3. In case a convicted person commits a new criminal offense during the probation period, the court imposes punishment on him or her according to the rules provided for in Articles 71, 72 of this Code.
{Article 78 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 79

Release from serving a sentence with probation for pregnant women and women with children under seven years of age
1. In the case of imposing a sentence in the form of restriction of liberty or deprivation of liberty on pregnant women or women with children under seven years of age, except for those sentenced to deprivation of liberty for a term exceeding five years for grave and especially grave crimes, as well as for corruption criminal offenses, criminal offenses related to corruption, the court may release such convicted persons from serving both the principal and additional sentence with the establishment of a probationary period within the term for which, according to law, a woman may be released from work in connection with pregnancy, childbirth and until the child reaches seven years of age.
2. In the case of release from serving a sentence with probation for pregnant women and women with children under seven years of age, the court imposes on the convicted person obligations in accordance with Article 76 of this Code.
3. Supervision of convicted persons is carried out by the authorized body on probation matters.
4. After the expiration of the probationary period, the court, depending on the behavior of the convicted person, releases her from punishment or sends her to serve the sentence imposed by the verdict.
5. In the event that a woman released from serving a sentence with probation has refused the child, placed it in an orphanage, disappeared from the place of residence, evades raising the child, caring for it, does not fulfill the obligations imposed on her by the court, or systematically commits offenses that have resulted in administrative penalties and indicate her unwillingness to embark on the path of correction, the court, upon submission of the supervising body, sends the convicted person to serve the sentence in accordance with the court verdict.
6. If the convicted person commits a new criminal offense during the probationary period, the court imposes punishment on her according to the rules provided for in Articles 71 and 72 of this Code.
{Article 79 as amended according to Laws No. 1254-VI of 14.04.2009, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 1492-VIII of 07.09.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Article 80

Release from serving a sentence due to expiration of the statute of limitations for execution of a conviction verdict
1. A person is released from serving a sentence if, from the date the conviction verdict enters into force, it has not been executed within the following terms:
1) two years - in the case of sentencing to a punishment less severe than restriction of liberty;
2) three years - in the case of sentencing to a punishment in the form of restriction of liberty;
3) five years - in the case of sentencing to a punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a minor crime, as well as when sentencing to deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years for a grave crime;
4) ten years - in the case of sentencing to a punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a term exceeding five years for a grave crime, as well as when sentencing to deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding ten years for an especially grave crime;
5) fifteen years - in the case of sentencing to a punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a term exceeding ten years for an especially grave crime.
2. The statute of limitations for additional punishments is determined by the principal punishment imposed by the court verdict.
3. The running of the statute of limitations is suspended if the convicted person evades serving the sentence. In such cases, the running of the statute of limitations resumes from the date of the convicted person's appearance to serve the sentence or from the date of their detention. In this case, the statute of limitations periods provided for in points 1-3 of part one of this article are doubled.
4. The running of the statute of limitations is interrupted if, before the expiration of the terms specified in parts one and three of this article, the convicted person commits a new crime. The calculation of the statute of limitations in this case begins from the date of commission of the new crime.
5. The question of applying the statute of limitations to a person sentenced to life imprisonment is decided by the court. If the court does not deem it possible to apply the statute of limitations, life imprisonment is replaced by deprivation of liberty.
6. The statute of limitations does not apply in the case of sentencing for torture provided for in part three of Article 127, crimes against peace and security of humanity provided for in Articles 437-439 and part one of Article 442 of this Code.
{Article 80 as amended according to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2812-IX of 01.12.2022}

Article 81. Conditional early release from serving a sentence

1. {Part one of Article 81 has been found not to comply with the Constitution of Ukraine (is unconstitutional) in that it makes it impossible to apply it to persons sentenced to serve a sentence in the form of life imprisonment, according to the Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 6-р(II)/2021 of 16.09.2021} Conditional early release from serving a sentence may be applied to persons serving a sentence in the form of correctional labor, service restrictions for military personnel, restriction of liberty, detention in a disciplinary battalion for military personnel, or deprivation of liberty. A person may be conditionally and early released fully or partially from serving an additional sentence as well.
2. Conditional early release from serving a sentence may be applied if the convicted person has proven their correction through conscientious behavior and attitude to work.
3. Conditional early release from serving a sentence may be applied after the convicted person has actually served:
1) not less than half of the term of punishment imposed by the court for a criminal misdemeanor or minor crime, except for corruption criminal offenses or criminal offenses related to corruption, violation of traffic safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medications that reduce attention and reaction speed, as well as for a negligent grave crime;
2) not less than two-thirds of the term of punishment imposed by the court for a corruption minor crime or criminal offense related to corruption, violation of traffic safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medications that reduce attention and reaction speed, an intentional grave crime or negligent especially grave crime, as well as if the person previously served a sentence in the form of deprivation of liberty for an intentional criminal offense and, before the conviction was expunged or removed, again committed an intentional criminal offense for which they were sentenced to deprivation of liberty;
3) not less than three-quarters of the term of punishment imposed by the court for an intentional especially grave crime, in the case of replacement of a sentence in the form of life imprisonment with a sentence in the form of deprivation of liberty for a definite term, as well as a sentence imposed on a person who was previously conditionally and early released and again committed an intentional criminal offense during the unserved part of the sentence.
4. In the event that a person to whom conditional early release from serving a sentence was applied commits a new criminal offense during the unserved part of the sentence, the court imposes punishment on them according to the rules provided for in Articles 71 and 72 of this Code.
{Article 81 as amended according to Laws No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021, No. 2690-IX of 18.10.2022}

Article 82. Replacement of Punishment or Its Unserved Part with a More Lenient One

1. The unserved part of a punishment in the form of restriction, deprivation of liberty or a punishment in the form of life imprisonment may be replaced by the court with a more lenient punishment, the term of which is calculated from the day of replacement of the unserved part of the punishment or the punishment in the form of life imprisonment with a more lenient one. In these cases, the more lenient punishment is imposed within the limits of the terms established in the General Part of this Code for the given type of punishment, and must not exceed the unserved term of the punishment imposed by the sentence.
2. In case of replacement of the unserved part of the principal punishment with a more lenient one, the convicted person may also be released from the additional punishment in the form of deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities.
3. Replacement of the unserved part of the punishment with a more lenient one may be applied if the convicted person has embarked on the path of correction.
4. Replacement of the unserved part of the punishment with a more lenient one is possible after the convicted person has actually served:
1) not less than one third of the term of punishment imposed by the court for a criminal misdemeanor or minor crime, except for corruption criminal offenses or criminal offenses related to corruption, violation of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, as well as for a negligent grave crime;
2) not less than half of the term of punishment imposed by the court for a corruption minor crime or a criminal offense related to corruption, violation of road safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, an intentional grave crime or a negligent particularly grave crime, as well as in case the person previously served a punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for an intentional criminal offense and before the expungement or removal of the conviction again committed an intentional criminal offense for which he or she was sentenced to deprivation of liberty;
3) not less than two thirds of the term of punishment imposed by the court for an intentional particularly grave crime, as well as a punishment imposed on a person who was previously released on parole and committed a new intentional criminal offense during the unserved part of the punishment.
5. A punishment in the form of life imprisonment may be replaced with a punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a term of fifteen to twenty years if the convicted person has served not less than fifteen years of the punishment imposed by the court.
6. Parole may be applied to persons whose punishment has been replaced with a more lenient one according to the rules provided for in Article 81 of this Code.
7. If a person, while serving a more lenient punishment, commits a new criminal offense, the court shall add the unserved part of the more lenient punishment to the punishment for the newly committed criminal offense according to the rules provided for in Articles 71 and 72 of this Code.
{Article 82 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021, No. 2690-IX of 18.10.2022}

Article 83

Release from Serving Punishment of Pregnant Women and Women with Children Under Three Years of Age
1. Women sentenced to restriction of liberty or to deprivation of liberty who became pregnant or gave birth to children while serving their punishment, except for those sentenced to deprivation of liberty for a term of more than five years for intentional grave and particularly grave crimes, may be released by the court from serving their punishment within the term for which, according to law, a woman may be released from work in connection with pregnancy, childbirth and until the child reaches the age of three.
2. Release from serving punishment is applied to a convicted woman who has a family or relatives who have consented to live together with her, or who has the possibility to independently provide proper conditions for raising the child.
3. Supervision of pregnant women and women with children under three years of age released from serving punishment is carried out by the authorized body on probation matters at the place of residence.
4. After the child reaches the age of three or in case of its death, the court, depending on the behavior of the convicted woman, may release her from punishment or replace it with a more lenient punishment or send the convicted woman to serve the punishment imposed by the sentence. In this case, the court may fully or partially credit towards the term of serving the punishment the time during which the convicted woman did not serve the punishment.
5. If a convicted woman who was released from serving punishment refuses the child, transfers it to an orphanage, disappears from the place of residence or evades raising the child, caring for it, or systematically commits offenses that resulted in administrative penalties and indicate unwillingness to embark on the path of correction, the court may, upon submission of the supervising body, send the convicted woman to serve the punishment imposed by the sentence.
6. If during the period of release from serving punishment the convicted woman commits a new criminal offense, the court shall impose punishment on her according to the rules provided for in Articles 71 and 72 of this Code.
{Article 83 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1254-VI of 14.04.2009, No. 1492-VIII of 07.09.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 84. Release from Punishment Due to Illness

1. A person who, while serving punishment, becomes ill with a mental illness that deprives him or her of the ability to be aware of his or her actions (inaction) or to control them is released from punishment. Compulsory measures of a medical nature may be applied to such a person in accordance with Articles 92-95 of this Code.
2. A person who, after committing a criminal offense or after the sentence is pronounced, becomes ill with another grave illness that prevents serving the punishment may be released from punishment or from its further serving. When deciding this issue, the court takes into account the gravity of the criminal offense committed, the nature of the illness, the personality of the convicted person and other circumstances of the case.
3. Military servicemen sentenced to service restriction, arrest or detention in a disciplinary battalion, in case of being recognized as unfit for military service due to health condition, are released from punishment.
4. In case of recovery of the persons specified in parts one and two of this Article, they must be sent to serve the punishment if the statutes of limitations provided for in Articles 49 or 80 of this Code have not expired, or there are no other grounds for release from punishment. In this case, the time during which compulsory measures of a medical nature were applied to the persons is credited towards the term of punishment according to the rules provided for in part five of Article 72 of this Code, and one day of deprivation of liberty equals one day of application of compulsory measures of a medical nature.
{Article 84 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 84-1

Release from serving a sentence in connection with a decision by an authorized body to transfer the convicted person for exchange as a prisoner of war
1. A convicted person, with respect to whom during the serving of a sentence in the form of correctional labor, arrest, restriction of liberty, detention in a disciplinary battalion of military servicemen, deprivation of liberty for a definite term, life imprisonment, an authorized body has made a decision on his or her transfer for exchange as a prisoner of war and who has provided written consent to conduct such an exchange, shall be released by the court from serving the imposed sentence.
2. If the exchange of such convicted person did not take place, the court, upon the motion of the prosecutor, shall make a decision on sending the convicted person released from serving the sentence in connection with his or her transfer for exchange as a prisoner of war, for further serving of the previously imposed sentence.
3. In the event that a person who was released by the court from serving a sentence in connection with a decision by an authorized body on his or her transfer for exchange as a prisoner of war and such exchange took place, commits a new criminal offense during the unserved part of the sentence, the court shall impose a sentence on him or her according to the rules provided for by Articles 71 and 72 of this Code.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 84-1 in accordance with Law No. 2472-IX of 28.07.2022; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2810-IX of 01.12.2022}

Стаття 85

Release from punishment on the basis of a law of Ukraine on amnesty or an act of pardon
On the basis of a law on amnesty or an act of pardon, a convicted person may be fully or partially released from the principal and additional punishments.
{Text of Article 85 as amended by Law No. 3465-VI of 02.06.2011}
1. Amnesty is declared by a law of Ukraine with respect to a certain category of persons.
2. By a law on amnesty, persons found guilty of committing a criminal offense by a conviction of a court, or criminal cases concerning whom have been considered by courts but the sentences concerning these persons have not entered into legal force, may be fully or partially released from serving the sentence.
{Part three of Article 86 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 3465-VI of 02.06.2011}
4. Persons found guilty of committing high treason, sabotage, corruption criminal offenses, criminal offenses related to corruption, violation of traffic safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and speed of reaction, the sentences concerning whom have not entered into legal force, may not be released from serving the sentence, and persons the sentences concerning whom have entered into legal force may not be fully released by a law on amnesty from serving the sentence. The specified persons may be released from serving the sentence after actual serving by them of the terms established by part three of Article 81 of this Code.
{Article 86 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 3465-VI of 02.06.2011, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021, No. 2113-IX of 03.03.2022}
1. Pardon is exercised by the President of Ukraine with respect to an individually determined person.
2. By an act of pardon, a replacement may be made for a convicted person of the sentence imposed by the court in the form of life imprisonment with deprivation of liberty for a term of not less than twenty-five years.
3. Persons convicted of committing corruption criminal offenses, criminal offenses related to corruption, violation of traffic safety rules or operation of transport by persons who operated vehicles while intoxicated by alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or were under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and speed of reaction, may be released from serving the sentence by way of pardon after actual serving by them of the terms established by part three of Article 81 of this Code.
{Article 87 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 88. Правові наслідки судимості

1. A person is recognized as having a criminal record from the day the conviction enters into legal force until the expungement or removal of the criminal record.
2. A criminal record has legal significance in the event of committing a new criminal offense, as well as in other cases provided for by the laws of Ukraine.
3. Persons convicted by a court sentence without imposition of punishment or with release from punishment or those who have served a sentence for an act the criminal unlawfulness and punishability of which has been eliminated by law, are recognized as not having a criminal record.
4. Persons who have been rehabilitated are recognized as not having a criminal record.
{Article 88 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 89. Строки погашення судимості

The following are recognized as not having a criminal record:
1) persons convicted in accordance with Article 75 of this Code, if during the probationary period they do not commit a new criminal offense and if during the specified period the decision on release from serving the sentence with probation is not revoked on other grounds provided for by law. If the term of additional punishment exceeds the duration of the probationary period, the person is recognized as not having a criminal record after serving this additional punishment;
2) women convicted in accordance with Article 79 of this Code, if during the probationary period they do not commit a new criminal offense and if after the expiration of this period a decision is not made on sending them to serve the sentence imposed by the court sentence. If the convicted woman was not released from additional punishment and its term exceeds the duration of the probationary period, then the woman is recognized as not having a criminal record after serving this additional punishment;
2-1) persons convicted of committing a criminal misdemeanor, after serving the sentence;
{Point 3 of Article 89 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014}
4) persons who have served a sentence in the form of service restriction for military servicemen or detention in a disciplinary battalion of military servicemen or have been released early from these punishments, as well as military servicemen who have served a sentence in a guardhouse instead of arrest;
5) persons convicted of committing a crime to a principal punishment in the form of a fine in the amount of not more than three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities, community service, correctional labor or arrest, if they within one year from the day of serving the sentence (principal and additional) do not commit a new criminal offense;
6) persons convicted of committing a crime to restriction of liberty, if they within two years from the day of serving the sentence (principal and additional) do not commit a new criminal offense;
7) persons convicted to deprivation of liberty or a principal punishment in the form of a fine for a minor crime, if they within three years from the day of serving the sentence (principal and additional) do not commit a new criminal offense;
8) persons convicted to deprivation of liberty or a principal punishment in the form of a fine for a grave crime, if they within six years from the day of serving the sentence (principal and additional) do not commit a new criminal offense;
9) persons convicted to deprivation of liberty or a principal punishment in the form of a fine for a particularly grave crime, if they within eight years from the day of serving the sentence (principal and additional) do not commit a new criminal offense.
{Article 89 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 90. Calculation of periods for expungement of conviction

1. Periods for expungement of conviction are calculated from the day of serving the principal and additional punishment.
2. The period for expungement of conviction includes the time during which the sentence was not executed, if the statute of limitations for execution of the sentence was not interrupted. If the sentence was not executed, the conviction is expunged upon expiration of the statute of limitations for execution of the sentence.
3. If a person was released early from serving punishment, the period for expungement of conviction is calculated from the day of early release from serving punishment (principal and additional).
4. If the unserved part of punishment was replaced by a milder punishment, the period for expungement of conviction is calculated from the day of serving the milder punishment (principal and additional).
5. If a person who has served punishment commits a criminal offence again before expiration of the period for expungement of conviction, the running of the period for expungement of conviction is interrupted and is calculated anew. In such cases, the periods for expungement of conviction are calculated separately for each criminal offence after actual serving of punishment (principal and additional) for the last criminal offence.
{Article 90 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. If a person, after serving punishment in the form of restriction of liberty or deprivation of liberty, has proved their correction by exemplary conduct and conscientious attitude to work, the court may remove the conviction before expiration of the periods specified in Article 89 of this Code.
2. Removal of conviction before expiration of the periods specified in Article 89 of this Code is not permitted in cases of conviction for intentional grave and especially grave, as well as corruption criminal offences or criminal offences related to corruption.
3. Removal of conviction is permitted only after expiration of at least half of the period for expungement of conviction specified in Article 89 of this Code.
4. The procedure for removal of conviction is established by the Criminal Procedure Code of Ukraine.
{Article 91 as amended pursuant to Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Section XIII-1

RESTRICTIVE MEASURES

Article 91-1

Restrictive measures applied to persons who have committed domestic violence
1. In the interests of the victim of a crime related to domestic violence, simultaneously with imposition of punishment not related to deprivation of liberty, or release on grounds provided for by this Code from criminal liability or punishment, the court may apply to a person who has committed domestic violence one or several restrictive measures, according to which the convicted person may be imposed the following obligations:
1) prohibition to stay in the place of joint residence with the person who suffered from domestic violence;
2) restriction of communication with a child in case domestic violence was committed against a child or in their presence;
3) prohibition to approach within a specified distance to the place where the person who suffered from domestic violence may permanently or temporarily reside, temporarily or systematically be present in connection with work, study, treatment or for other reasons;
4) prohibition of correspondence, telephone conversations with the person who suffered from domestic violence, other contacts through means of communication or electronic communications personally or through third persons;
5) referral for completion of a programme for offenders.
2. Measures provided for by part one of this Article are applied to a person who at the time of committing domestic violence has reached 18 years of age.
3. Measures provided for by part one of this Article may be applied for a period from one to three months and, if necessary, may be extended for a period determined by the court, but not more than 12 months.
{Part four of Article 91-1 excluded on the basis of Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}
{Article 91-1 as amended pursuant to Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}
{The General Part supplemented by Section XIII-1 pursuant to Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017}

Section XIV

OTHER MEASURES OF CRIMINAL-LEGAL CHARACTER
{Title of Section XIV as worded by Law No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013}

Article 92. Concept and purpose of compulsory measures of medical character

Compulsory measures of medical character are provision of outpatient psychiatric care, placement of a person who has committed a socially dangerous act falling under the characteristics of an act provided for by the Special Part of this Code in a special medical institution for the purpose of their mandatory treatment, as well as prevention of commission by them of socially dangerous acts.

Article 93. Persons to whom compulsory measures of medical character are applied

Compulsory measures of medical character may be applied by the court to persons:
1) who have committed socially dangerous acts in a state of insanity;
2) who have committed criminal offences in a state of diminished sanity;
3) who have committed a criminal offence in a state of sanity but became ill with mental illness before pronouncement of sentence or during serving of punishment.
{Article 93 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 94. Types of compulsory measures of medical character

1. Depending on the nature and severity of the illness, severity of the act committed, taking into account the degree of danger of the mentally ill person to themselves or other persons, the court may apply the following compulsory measures of medical character:
1) provision of outpatient psychiatric care on a compulsory basis;
2) hospitalisation to an institution providing psychiatric care with ordinary supervision;
3) hospitalisation to an institution providing psychiatric care with enhanced supervision;
4) hospitalisation to an institution providing psychiatric care with strict supervision.
2. Provision of outpatient psychiatric care on a compulsory basis may be applied by the court to a person suffering from mental disorders who has committed a socially dangerous act, if the person, according to their mental health condition, does not require hospitalisation to an institution providing psychiatric care.
3. Hospitalisation to an institution providing psychiatric care with ordinary supervision may be applied by the court to a mentally ill person who, according to their mental condition and the nature of the socially dangerous act committed, requires detention in an institution providing psychiatric care and treatment on a compulsory basis.
4. Hospitalisation to an institution providing psychiatric care with enhanced supervision may be applied by the court to a mentally ill person who has committed a socially dangerous act not related to encroachment on the life of other persons, and according to their mental condition does not pose a threat to society, but requires detention in an institution providing psychiatric care and treatment under conditions of enhanced supervision.
5. Hospitalisation to an institution providing psychiatric care with strict supervision may be applied by the court to a mentally ill person who has committed a socially dangerous act related to encroachment on the life of other persons, as well as to a mentally ill person who, according to their mental condition and the nature of the socially dangerous act committed, poses a particular danger to society and requires detention in an institution providing psychiatric care and treatment under conditions of strict supervision.
6. If it is not deemed necessary to apply compulsory measures of medical character to a mentally ill person, as well as in case of termination of application of such measures, the court may transfer them to the care of relatives or guardians with mandatory medical supervision.

Article 95

Extension, modification or termination of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature
1. Extension, modification or termination of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature is carried out by a court upon application of a representative of an institution providing psychiatric care (psychiatrist) that provides psychiatric care to the person. The application shall be accompanied by a conclusion of a commission of psychiatrists substantiating the necessity of extension, modification or termination of the application of such compulsory measures.
Modification or termination of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature may also be carried out by a court upon application of the person to whom compulsory measures of a medical nature are applied, their defender or legal representative in the event that such person, due to their state of health, cannot understand their actions (inaction) or control them, including cannot consciously submit a relevant application to the court. The application shall be accompanied by a conclusion of a commission of psychiatrists of the institution where the person is provided psychiatric care, or, if available, a conclusion of an independent psychiatrist chosen by them.
2. Persons to whom compulsory measures of a medical nature have been applied are subject to examination by a commission of psychiatrists at least once every 6 months to resolve the issue of the existence of grounds for applying to the court with an application for termination or modification of the application of such measure. In the event of absence of grounds for termination or modification of the application of a compulsory measure of a medical nature, the representative of the institution providing psychiatric care (psychiatrist) who provides such psychiatric care to the person shall submit to the court an application accompanied by a conclusion of the commission of psychiatrists containing substantiation of the necessity of extension of the application of the compulsory measure of a medical nature. In the event of necessity of extension of the application of a compulsory measure of a medical nature for more than 6 months, the representative of the institution providing psychiatric care (psychiatrist) who provides such psychiatric care to the person must submit to the court at the location of the institution providing psychiatric care an application for extension of the application of the compulsory measure. The application shall be accompanied by a conclusion of the commission of psychiatrists containing substantiation of the necessity of extension of provision of such psychiatric care to the person. Subsequently, extension of the application of a compulsory measure of a medical nature is carried out each time for a period that may not exceed 6 months.
Persons to whom compulsory measures of a medical nature are applied have the right to apply to the court with an application for modification or termination of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature no more than once every 6 months and regardless of whether this issue was considered by the court during the specified period.
A person to whom compulsory measures of a medical nature are applied has the right to apply to an independent psychiatrist chosen by them for the purpose of obtaining a conclusion on the state of their mental health. The conclusion of the independent psychiatrist must specify the grounds for modification or termination of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature. In the event of absence of such grounds, the conclusion shall substantiate the necessity of extension of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature.
3. In the event of termination of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature due to improvement of the mental state of the person, the court may transfer them to the care of relatives or guardians with mandatory medical supervision.
4. In the event of termination of the application of compulsory measures of a medical nature due to recovery of persons who committed criminal offenses in a state of sanity but became mentally ill before the pronouncement of a sentence, they are subject to punishment on general grounds, and persons who became mentally ill during the serving of punishment may be subject to further serving of punishment.
{Article 95 as amended according to Laws No. 2205-VIII of 14.11.2017, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 96. Compulsory treatment

1. Compulsory treatment may be applied by a court, regardless of the punishment imposed, to persons who have committed criminal offenses and have a disease that poses a danger to the health of other persons.
2. In the event of imposition of punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty or restriction of liberty, compulsory treatment is carried out at the place of serving of punishment. In the event of imposition of other types of punishments, compulsory treatment is carried out in special medical institutions.
{Article 96 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 96-1. Special confiscation

{Article 96-1 was found to comply with the Constitution of Ukraine (is constitutional), according to Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 1-r/2022 of 30.06.2022}
1. Special confiscation consists in compulsory gratuitous seizure by court decision into state ownership of money, valuables and other property in cases specified by this Code, provided that an intentional criminal offense or socially dangerous act falling under the characteristics of an act provided for by the Special Part of this Code has been committed, for which the main punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty or a fine exceeding three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens is provided, as well as provided for by part one of Article 150, Article 154, parts two and three of Article 159-1, part one of Article 190, Article 192, part one of Articles 204, 209-1, 210, parts one and two of Articles 212, 212-1, part one of Articles 222, 229, 239-1, 239-2, part two of Article 244, part one of Articles 248, 249, parts one and two of Article 300, part one of Articles 301, 302, 310, 311, 313, 318, 319, 362, Article 363, part one of Articles 363-1, 364-1, 365-2 of this Code.
2. Special confiscation is applied on the basis of:
1) a conviction judgment of a court;
2) a court order on release of a person from criminal liability;
3) a court order on application of compulsory measures of a medical nature;
4) a court order on application of compulsory measures of an educational nature.
3. In cases where the object of special confiscation is property withdrawn from civil circulation, it may be applied on the basis of:
1) a court order on closure of criminal proceedings on grounds other than release of a person from criminal liability;
2) a court order issued in accordance with part nine of Article 100 of the Criminal Procedural Code of Ukraine, upon motion of an investigator or prosecutor, if criminal proceedings are closed by them.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 96-1 according to Law No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013; as amended by Law No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014; as amended according to Law No. 731-VIII of 08.10.2015; text of Article 96-1 as amended by Law No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015; as amended according to Law No. 743-VIII of 03.11.2015; text of Article 96-1 as amended by Law No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016; as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

This excerpt sits under: (Vidomosti Verkhovnoi Rady Ukrainy (VVR), 2001, No. 25-26, Art.131) > Section XIV

Article 96-2. Cases of Application of Special Confiscation

{Article 96-2 was recognized as compliant with the Constitution of Ukraine (constitutional), according to the Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 1-r/2022 of 30.06.2022}
1. Special confiscation is applied in the event that money, valuables and other property:
1) were obtained as a result of committing a criminal offense and/or are proceeds from such property;
2) were intended (used) to induce a person to commit a criminal offense, to finance and/or materially support a criminal offense or as remuneration for its commission;
3) were the subject of a criminal offense, except those that are returned to the owner (lawful possessor), and in the event that the owner is not established, - pass into state ownership;
4) were sought, manufactured, adapted or used as means or instruments of committing a criminal offense, except those that are returned to the owner (lawful possessor) who did not know and could not have known about their illegal use.
2. In the event that money, valuables and other property specified in part one of this article were fully or partially converted into other property, the fully or partially converted property is subject to special confiscation. If confiscation of money, valuables and other property specified in part one of this article is impossible at the time the court adopts a decision on special confiscation due to their use or impossibility of separation from property acquired by lawful means, or alienation, or for other reasons, the court issues a decision on confiscation of a monetary amount corresponding to the value of such property.
3. Special confiscation is also applied in the event that a person is not subject to criminal liability due to not having reached the age from which criminal liability may arise, or insanity, or is released from criminal liability or punishment on grounds provided for by this Code, except for release from criminal liability due to expiration of the statute of limitations.
4. Money, valuables, including funds held in bank accounts or on deposit in banks or other financial institutions, other property specified in this article are subject to special confiscation from a third party if such party acquired such property from a suspect, accused person, person prosecuted for committing a socially dangerous act at an age from which criminal liability does not arise, or in a state of insanity, or another person free of charge, at market price or at a price higher or lower than market value, and knew or should have and could have known that such property corresponds to any of the characteristics specified in points 1-4 of part one of this article.
The above-mentioned information regarding the third party must be established in court on the basis of sufficiency of evidence.
Special confiscation may not be applied to property that is owned by a bona fide acquirer.
5. Special confiscation is not applied to money, valuables and other property specified in this article which according to law are subject to return to the owner (lawful possessor) or are intended for compensation of damage caused by a criminal offense.
{Part six of Article 96-2 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016}
{The Code was supplemented with Article 96-2 according to Law No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013; with amendments introduced according to Laws No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019}

Section XIV-1

MEASURES OF CRIMINAL-LEGAL CHARACTER REGARDING LEGAL ENTITIES

Article 96-3

Grounds for Application of Measures of Criminal-Legal Character to Legal Entities
1. The grounds for application of measures of criminal-legal character to a legal entity are:
1) commission by its authorized person on behalf of and in the interests of the legal entity of any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 209 and 306, parts one and two of Article 368-3, parts one and two of Article 368-4, Articles 369 and 369-2 of this Code;
2) failure to ensure performance by its authorized person of obligations imposed by law or constituent documents of the legal entity regarding taking measures to prevent corruption, which led to the commission of any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 209 and 306, parts one and two of Article 368-3, parts one and two of Article 368-4, Articles 369 and 369-2 of this Code;
3) commission by its authorized person on behalf of the legal entity of any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 111-1, 258-258-6 of this Code;
4) commission by its authorized person on behalf of and in the interests of the legal entity of any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 109, 110, 113, 114-2, 146, 147, parts two - four of Article 159-1, Articles 160, 260, 262, 436, 437, 438, 442, 444, 447 of this Code;
5) commission by its authorized person on behalf of and in the interests of the legal entity of any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 255, 343, 345, 347, 348, 349, 376-379, 386 of this Code;
6) commission by its authorized person on behalf of and in the interests of the legal entity in relation to a minor or juvenile person of any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 152-156-1, 301-1-303 of this Code.
Note 1. Authorized persons of a legal entity shall mean officials of the legal entity, as well as other persons who, in accordance with law, constituent documents of the legal entity or contract, have the right to act on behalf of the legal entity.
2. Criminal offenses provided for in Articles 109, 110, 113, 114-2, 146, 147, 152-156-1, parts two - four of Article 159-1, Articles 160, 209, 255, 260, 262, 301-1-303, 306, 343, 345, 347, 348, 349, parts one and two of Article 368-3, parts one and two of Article 368-4, Articles 369, 369-2, 376-379, 386, 436, 437, 438, 442, 444, 447 of this Code are recognized as committed in the interests of a legal entity if they resulted in its obtaining unlawful benefit or created conditions for obtaining such benefit, or were aimed at evading liability provided for by law.
{Article 96-3 with amendments introduced according to Laws No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 731-VIII of 08.10.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019, No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021, No. 2108-IX of 03.03.2022, No. 2160-IX of 24.03.2022, No. 2997-IX of 21.03.2023}

Стаття 96-4

Legal entities to which criminal law measures are applied
1. Criminal law measures, in cases provided for in points 1 and 2 of part one of Article 96-3 of this Code, may be applied by the court to an enterprise, institution or organization, except state bodies, authorities of the Autonomous Republic of Crimea, local self-government bodies, organizations created by them in the established procedure, which are fully maintained at the expense of the state or local budgets respectively, funds of mandatory state social insurance, the Deposit Guarantee Fund for individuals, as well as international organizations.
2. Criminal law measures, in cases provided for in points 3-6 of part one of Article 96-3 of this Code, may be applied by the court to subjects of private and public law, residents and non-residents of Ukraine, including enterprises, institutions or organizations, state bodies, authorities of the Autonomous Republic of Crimea, local self-government bodies, organizations created by them in the established procedure, funds, as well as international organizations, other legal entities created in accordance with the requirements of national or international law.
If the state or a subject of state property owns a share of more than 25 percent in a legal entity or a legal entity is under the effective control of the state or a subject of state property, then such legal entity bears full civil liability for unlawfully obtained benefit and damage caused by a criminal offense committed by the state, subjects of state property or state administration.
3. In case of reorganization of legal entities specified in parts one and two of this Article, criminal law measures may be applied to their legal successors to whom property, rights and obligations related to the commission of criminal offenses specified in points 1-6 of part one of Article 96-3 of this Code have been transferred.
{Article 96-4 as amended according to Laws No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020, No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}

Стаття 96-5

Grounds for exempting a legal entity from the application of criminal law measures
1. A legal entity is exempted from the application of criminal law measures to it if, from the day of commission by its authorized person of any criminal offense specified in Article 96-3 of this Code until the day the verdict enters into legal force, the following periods have elapsed:
1) three years - in case of commission of a criminal misdemeanor;
2) five years - in case of commission of a minor crime;
3) ten years - in case of commission of a serious crime;
4) fifteen years - in case of commission of a particularly serious crime.
2. The running of the statute of limitations for the application of criminal law measures to a legal entity is suspended if its authorized person who committed any criminal offense specified in Article 96-3 of this Code is hiding from pre-trial investigation bodies and the court in order to evade criminal liability and their whereabouts are unknown. In such cases, the running of the statute of limitations resumes from the day the whereabouts of this authorized person are established.
3. The running of the statute of limitations for the application of criminal law measures to a legal entity is interrupted if, before the expiration of the periods provided for in parts one and two of this Article, its authorized person repeatedly committed any criminal offense specified in Article 96-3 of this Code.
4. The calculation of the statute of limitations in this case begins from the day of commission by the authorized person of the legal entity of any criminal offense specified in Article 96-3 of this Code. In this case, the statute of limitations periods are calculated separately for each criminal offense.
{Article 96-5 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 96-6

Types of criminal law measures applied to legal entities
1. The following criminal law measures may be applied by the court to legal entities:
2. A fine and liquidation may be applied to legal entities only as principal criminal law measures, and confiscation of property - only as an additional measure. When applying criminal law measures, a legal entity is obliged to compensate for the losses and damage caused in full, as well as the amount of unlawfully obtained benefit that was obtained or could have been obtained by the legal entity.
1. A fine is a sum of money paid by a legal entity on the basis of a court decision.
The court applies a fine based on twice the amount of the unlawfully obtained improper benefit.
2. In cases where improper benefit was not obtained, or its amount cannot be calculated, the court, depending on the degree of gravity of the criminal offense committed by the authorized person of the legal entity, applies a fine in the following amounts:
for a criminal misdemeanor - from five to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens;
for a minor crime - from ten to twenty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens;
for a serious crime - from twenty to seventy-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens;
for a particularly serious crime - from seventy-five to one hundred thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
3. Taking into account the financial condition of the legal entity, the court may apply a fine with installment payment in certain parts for a period of up to three years.
{Article 96-7 as revised by Law No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014; as amended according to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019}

Стаття 96-8. Конфіскація майна

1. Confiscation of property consists in the compulsory gratuitous seizure into state ownership of property of a legal entity and is applied by the court in case of liquidation of a legal entity in accordance with this Code.
1. Liquidation of a legal entity is applied by the court in case of commission by its authorized person of any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 109, 110, 111-1, 113, 146, 147, 152-156-1, 160, 209, 255, 258-258-6, 301-1-303, 260, 262, 306, 436, 436-1, 437, 438, 442, 444, 447 of this Code.
{Article 96-9 as amended according to Laws No. 317-VIII of 09.04.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019, No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021, No. 2108-IX of 03.03.2022, No. 2997-IX of 21.03.2023}

Стаття 96-10

General rules for the application of criminal law measures to legal entities
1. When applying criminal law measures to a legal entity, the court takes into account the degree of gravity of the criminal offense committed by its authorized person, the degree of implementation of the criminal unlawful intent, the amount of damage caused, the nature and amount of improper benefit that was obtained or could have been obtained by the legal entity, measures taken by the legal entity to prevent the criminal offense.
{Article 96-10 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 96-11

Application of criminal law measures to legal entities for a cumulation of criminal offenses
1. For a cumulation of criminal offenses within one proceeding, the court, having applied criminal law measures to the legal entity for each criminal offense separately, determines the final principal measure by absorption of the less severe measure by the more severe one.
2. When applying criminal law measures to a legal entity for a crime in the presence of an unexecuted measure under a previous court verdict (verdicts), each of them is executed independently, except in cases of application by the court of liquidation of the legal entity in accordance with this Code.
{Article 96-11 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
{The Code was supplemented with Section XIV-1 according to Law No. 314-VII of 23.05.2013 taking into account amendments introduced by Law No. 1207-VII of 15.04.2014}

This excerpt sits under: (Відомості Верховної Ради України (ВВР), 2001, № 25-26, ст.131) > Розділ XIV-1

Section XV

SPECIAL FEATURES OF CRIMINAL LIABILITY AND PUNISHMENT OF MINORS

Article 97

Release from criminal liability with application of compulsory measures of educational character
1. A minor who has committed a criminal misdemeanor or a negligent minor crime for the first time may be released from criminal liability if his or her correction is possible without application of punishment. In these cases, the court shall apply to the minor compulsory measures of educational character provided for in part two of Article 105 of this Code.
2. Compulsory measures of educational character provided for in part two of Article 105 of this Code shall also be applied by the court to a person who, before reaching the age from which criminal liability may arise, has committed a socially dangerous act falling under the characteristics of an act provided for in the Special Part of this Code.
3. In case of evasion by a minor who has committed a criminal offense from application to him or her of compulsory measures of educational character, these measures shall be cancelled and he or she shall be brought to criminal liability.
{Article 97 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 98. Types of punishments

1. The following main types of punishments may be applied by the court to minors found guilty of committing a criminal offense:
5) deprivation of liberty for a definite term.
2. Additional punishments in the form of a fine and deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities may be applied to minors.
{Article 98 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 99. Fine

1. A fine shall be applied only to minors who have independent income, own funds or property against which recovery may be enforced.
2. The amount of the fine, including for committing a criminal offense for which the main punishment is provided only in the form of a fine exceeding three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, shall be established by the court depending on the gravity of the criminal offense committed and taking into account the property status of the minor within the limits of up to five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens established by law.
3. To a minor who does not have independent income, own funds or property against which recovery may be enforced, convicted of committing a criminal offense for which the main punishment is provided only in the form of a fine exceeding three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, punishment in the form of community service or correctional labor may be applied in accordance with the provisions of Articles 100, 103 of this Code.
{Article 99 as amended according to Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 100. Community service and correctional labor

1. Community service may be assigned to a minor aged 16 to 18 years for a term from thirty to one hundred twenty hours and consists in the performance by the minor of work during time free from study or main work. The duration of execution of this type of punishment may not exceed two hours per day.
2. Correctional labor may be assigned to a minor aged 16 to 18 years at the place of work for a term from two months to one year.
3. From the earnings of a minor sentenced to correctional labor, a deduction to the income of the state shall be made in the amount established by the court judgment, within the limits of five to ten percent.

Article 101. Arrest

Arrest consists in keeping a minor who has reached the age of sixteen at the time of pronouncement of the judgment under conditions of isolation in specially adapted institutions for a term from fifteen to forty-five days.

Article 102. Deprivation of liberty for a definite term

1. Punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for persons who have not reached the age of eighteen before committing a crime may be assigned for a term from six months to ten years, except for cases provided for in point 5 of part three of this Article. Minors sentenced to punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty shall serve it in special educational institutions.
{Part two of Article 102 excluded on the basis of Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
3. Punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty shall be assigned to a minor:
{Point 1 of part three of Article 102 excluded on the basis of Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
2) for a minor crime - for a term not exceeding four years;
3) for a grave crime - for a term not exceeding seven years;
4) for a particularly grave crime - for a term not exceeding ten years;
5) for a particularly grave crime combined with intentional deprivation of human life - for a term of up to fifteen years.
{Article 102 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 103. Assignment of punishment

1. When assigning punishment to a minor, the court, in addition to the circumstances provided for in Articles 65-67 of this Code, shall take into account the conditions of his or her life and upbringing, the influence of adults, the level of development and other features of the personality of the minor.
2. When assigning punishment to a minor for a cumulation of criminal offenses or judgments, the final punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty may not exceed fifteen years.
{Article 103 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 104. Release from serving punishment on probation

1. Release from serving punishment on probation shall be applied to minors in accordance with Articles 75-78 of this Code, taking into account the provisions provided for in this Article.
2. Release from serving punishment on probation may be applied to a minor only in case of his or her sentencing to arrest or deprivation of liberty.
3. The probationary period shall be established for a duration from one to two years.
4. In case of release of a minor from serving punishment on probation, the court may impose on a separate person, with his or her consent or at his or her request, the obligation to supervise the convicted person and conduct educational work with him or her.
{Article 104 as amended according to Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 105

Release from punishment with application of compulsory measures of educational character
1. A minor who has committed a criminal misdemeanor or a minor crime may be released by the court from punishment if it is recognized that due to sincere repentance and subsequent impeccable behavior he or she does not require application of punishment at the time of pronouncement of the judgment.
2. In this case, the court shall apply to the minor the following compulsory measures of educational character:
2) restriction of leisure and establishment of special requirements for the behavior of the minor;
3) transfer of the minor under the supervision of parents or persons replacing them, or under the supervision of a pedagogical or labor collective with its consent, as well as individual citizens at their request;
4) imposition on a minor who has reached the age of fifteen and has property, funds or earnings, of the obligation to compensate for property damage caused;
5) sending the minor to a special educational institution for children and adolescents until his or her correction, but for a term not exceeding three years. The conditions of stay in these institutions of minors and the procedure for their departure shall be determined by law.
3. Several compulsory measures of educational character provided for in part two of this Article may be applied to a minor. The duration of measures of educational character provided for in points 2 and 3 of part two of this Article shall be established by the court that assigns them.
4. The court may also recognize it necessary to appoint an educator to the minor in the procedure provided for by law.
{Article 105 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 106

Release from criminal liability and serving of punishment in connection with expiration of limitation periods
1. Release from criminal liability and serving of punishment in connection with expiration of limitation periods for persons who committed a criminal offense before reaching the age of eighteen is applied in accordance with Articles 49 and 80 of this Code, taking into account the provisions stipulated by this Article.
2. For persons specified in part one of this Article, the following limitation periods are established:
1) two years - in case of committing a criminal misdemeanor;
2) five years - in case of committing a minor crime;
3) seven years - in case of committing a serious crime;
4) ten years - in case of committing a particularly serious crime.
3. For persons specified in part one of this Article, the following periods for execution of a conviction sentence are established:
1) two years - in case of sentencing to a punishment not related to deprivation of liberty, as well as when sentenced to punishment for a criminal misdemeanor;
2) five years - in case of sentencing to punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a minor crime, as well as when sentenced to punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years for a serious crime;
3) seven years - in case of sentencing to punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a term exceeding five years for a serious crime;
4) ten years - in case of sentencing to punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a particularly serious crime.
{Article 106 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 107. Conditional early release from serving punishment

1. Conditional early release from serving punishment may be applied to persons serving punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a criminal offense committed before reaching the age of eighteen, regardless of the gravity of the criminal offense committed.
