2. Conditional early release from serving punishment may be applied if the convicted person has proven their correction through conscientious behavior and attitude to work and study.
3. Conditional early release from serving punishment may be applied to persons convicted for a criminal offense committed before reaching the age of eighteen, after actual serving of:
1) not less than one third of the assigned term of punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a minor crime and for a negligent serious crime;
2) not less than half of the term of punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty assigned by the court for an intentional serious crime or negligent particularly serious crime, as well as if the person previously served punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for an intentional criminal offense and before expungement or removal of conviction committed again before reaching the age of eighteen a new intentional criminal offense for which they were sentenced to deprivation of liberty;
3) not less than two thirds of the term of punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty assigned by the court for an intentional particularly serious crime, as well as if the person previously served punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty and was conditionally released early from serving punishment, but before the end of the unserved part of the punishment and before reaching the age of eighteen committed again an intentional criminal offense for which they were sentenced to deprivation of liberty.
4. Replacement of the unserved part of punishment with a milder punishment is not applied to minors.
5. In case a person to whom conditional early release from serving punishment was applied commits a new criminal offense during the unserved part of the punishment, the court assigns them punishment according to the rules stipulated in Articles 71 and 72 of this Code.
{Article 107 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 108. Expungement and removal of conviction

1. Expungement and removal of conviction for persons who committed a criminal offense before reaching the age of eighteen is carried out in accordance with Articles 88-91 of this Code, taking into account the provisions stipulated by this Article.
2. The following minors are recognized as having no conviction:
1) sentenced to punishment not related to deprivation of liberty, after execution of this punishment;
2) sentenced to deprivation of liberty for a minor crime, if they do not commit a new criminal offense within one year from the day of serving the punishment;
3) sentenced to deprivation of liberty for a serious crime, if they do not commit a new criminal offense within three years from the day of serving the punishment;
4) sentenced to deprivation of liberty for a particularly serious crime, if they do not commit a new criminal offense within five years from the day of serving the punishment.
3. Early removal of conviction is permitted only for a person who served punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty for a serious or particularly serious crime committed before reaching the age of eighteen, on the grounds stipulated in part one of Article 91 of this Code, after expiration of not less than half of the term of expungement of conviction specified in part two of this Article.
{Article 108 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section I

CRIMES AGAINST THE FOUNDATIONS OF NATIONAL SECURITY OF UKRAINE

Article 109

Actions aimed at violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order or at seizure of state power
1. Actions committed with the purpose of violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order or seizure of state power, as well as conspiracy to commit such actions, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. Public calls for violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order or for seizure of state power, as well as distribution of materials with calls to commit such actions, -
are punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to three years or deprivation of liberty for the same term with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Actions stipulated by part two of this Article, committed by a person who is a representative of authority, or repeatedly, or by an organized group, or using mass media, -
are punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term with confiscation of property or without such.
{Article 109 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014}

Article 110. Encroachment on territorial integrity and inviolability of Ukraine

1. Intentional actions committed with the purpose of changing the boundaries of the territory or state border of Ukraine in violation of the procedure established by the Constitution of Ukraine, as well as public calls or distribution of materials with calls to commit such actions, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to five years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same actions, if they are committed by a person who is a representative of authority, or repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or combined with incitement of national or religious enmity, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Actions stipulated by parts one or two of this Article, which resulted in death of people or other serious consequences, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment with confiscation of property or without such.
{Article 110 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1183-VII of 08.04.2014, No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014}

Стаття 110-1. {Article 110-1 excluded on the basis of Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Стаття 110-2

Financing of actions committed with the purpose of violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order or seizure of state power, change of the boundaries of the territory or state border of Ukraine
1. Financing of actions committed with the purpose of changing the boundaries of the territory or state border of Ukraine in violation of the procedure established by the Constitution of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to two years and with confiscation of property.
2. Financing of actions committed with the purpose of violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order or seizure of state power, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to two years and with confiscation of property.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed repeatedly or from mercenary motives, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or on a large scale, or if they resulted in causing significant property damage, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of six to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
4. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by an organized group or on an especially large scale, or if they resulted in other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
5. A person, except for the leader of an organized group, shall be released from criminal liability for actions provided for in this Article, if before being notified of suspicion of committing a crime, he or she voluntarily reported what happened to a body whose official is empowered by law to notify of suspicion, about the relevant illegal activity or otherwise contributed to its termination or prevention of the crime which he or she financed or contributed to the commission of, provided that his or her actions do not contain the elements of another crime.
Note. 1. Financing of actions provided for in this Article means actions committed with the purpose of their financial or material support.
2. Financing of actions committed with the purpose of violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order or seizure of state power, change of the boundaries of the territory or state border of Ukraine, shall be deemed committed on a large scale if the amount of financial or material support exceeds six thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens.
3. Financing of actions committed with the purpose of violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order or seizure of state power, change of the boundaries of the territory or state border of Ukraine, shall be deemed committed on an especially large scale if the amount of financial or material support exceeds eighteen thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 110-2 in accordance with Law No. 1533-VII of 19.06.2014}
1. High treason, that is, an act intentionally committed by a citizen of Ukraine to the detriment of the sovereignty, territorial integrity and inviolability, defense capability, state, economic or information security of Ukraine: going over to the enemy's side during an armed conflict, espionage, providing assistance to a foreign state, foreign organization or their representatives in conducting subversive activities against Ukraine, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of twelve to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same acts committed under martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of fifteen years or life imprisonment, with confiscation of property.
3. A citizen of Ukraine shall be released from criminal liability if, in execution of a criminal assignment of a foreign state, foreign organization or their representatives, he or she committed no actions and voluntarily reported to state authorities about his or her connection with them and about the assignment received.
{Article 111 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1183-VII of 08.04.2014, No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014; as worded by Law No. 2113-IX of 03.03.2022}

Article 111-1. Collaborationist Activity

1. Public denial by a citizen of Ukraine of the commission of armed aggression against Ukraine, the establishment and consolidation of temporary occupation of part of the territory of Ukraine, or public calls by a citizen of Ukraine to support decisions and/or actions of the aggressor state, armed formations and/or the occupation administration of the aggressor state, to cooperate with the aggressor state, armed formations and/or the occupation administration of the aggressor state, to non-recognition of the extension of state sovereignty of Ukraine to temporarily occupied territories of Ukraine -
shall be punished by deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years.
2. Voluntary assumption by a citizen of Ukraine of a position not related to the performance of organizational-administrative or administrative-economic functions in illegal authorities created in temporarily occupied territory, including in the occupation administration of the aggressor state, -
shall be punished by deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Carrying out by a citizen of Ukraine of propaganda in educational institutions regardless of types and forms of ownership for the purpose of facilitating the commission of armed aggression against Ukraine, the establishment and consolidation of temporary occupation of part of the territory of Ukraine, avoidance of responsibility for the commission by the aggressor state of armed aggression against Ukraine, as well as actions of citizens of Ukraine aimed at implementing educational standards of the aggressor state in educational institutions, -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years.
4. Transfer of material resources to illegal armed or paramilitary formations created in temporarily occupied territory, and/or to armed or paramilitary formations of the aggressor state, and/or conducting economic activity in cooperation with the aggressor state, illegal authorities created in temporarily occupied territory, including the occupation administration of the aggressor state, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to ten thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of three to five years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years and with confiscation of property.
5. Voluntary assumption by a citizen of Ukraine of a position related to the performance of organizational-administrative or administrative-economic functions in illegal authorities created in temporarily occupied territory, including in the occupation administration of the aggressor state, or voluntary election to such authorities, as well as participation in the organization and conduct of illegal elections and/or referendums in temporarily occupied territory or public calls to conduct such illegal elections and/or referendums in temporarily occupied territory -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years and with confiscation of property or without such.
6. Organization and conduct of events of a political nature, carrying out information activities in cooperation with the aggressor state and/or its occupation administration, aimed at supporting the aggressor state, its occupation administration or armed formations and/or at its avoidance of responsibility for armed aggression against Ukraine, in the absence of signs of state treason, active participation in such events -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years and with confiscation of property or without such.
7. Voluntary assumption by a citizen of Ukraine of a position in illegal judicial or law enforcement authorities created in temporarily occupied territory, as well as voluntary participation of a citizen of Ukraine in illegal armed or paramilitary formations created in temporarily occupied territory, and/or in armed formations of the aggressor state or provision of assistance to such formations in conducting combat operations against the Armed Forces of Ukraine and other military formations formed in accordance with the laws of Ukraine, volunteer formations that were formed or self-organized to defend the independence, sovereignty and territorial integrity of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of twelve to fifteen years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years and with confiscation of property or without such.
8. Commission by persons specified in parts five through seven of this article of actions or adoption of decisions that resulted in the death of people or the occurrence of other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of fifteen years or life imprisonment, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years and with confiscation of property or without such.
Note. 1. In part one of this article, public means dissemination of calls or expression of denial to an indefinite circle of persons, in particular on the Internet or through mass media.
2. In part six of this article, events of a political nature means congresses, assemblies, rallies, marches, demonstrations, conferences, round tables, etc.
3. In part six of this article, carrying out information activities means creation, collection, receipt, storage, use and dissemination of relevant information.
4. Grave consequences in part eight of this article means damage that exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by one thousand times or more.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 111-1 in accordance with Law No. 2108-IX of 03.03.2022}

Article 111-2. Aiding the Aggressor State

1. Intentional actions aimed at assisting the aggressor state (aiding), armed formations and/or the occupation administration of the aggressor state, committed by a citizen of Ukraine, a foreigner or a stateless person, with the exception of citizens of the aggressor state, with the purpose of causing harm to Ukraine by: implementation or support of decisions and/or actions of the aggressor state, armed formations and/or the occupation administration of the aggressor state; voluntary collection, preparation and/or transfer of material resources or other assets to representatives of the aggressor state, its armed formations and/or the occupation administration of the aggressor state -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of ten to fifteen years and with confiscation of property or without such.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 111-2 in accordance with Law No. 2198-IX of 14.04.2022}

Article 112. Attempt on the life of a state or public figure

Attempt on the life of the President of Ukraine, the Chairman of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, a People's Deputy of Ukraine, the Prime Minister of Ukraine, a member of the Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine, the Chairman or a member of the High Council of Justice, the Chairman or a member of the High Qualifications Commission of Judges of Ukraine, the Chairman or a judge of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine or the Supreme Court, or higher specialized courts, the Prosecutor General, the Director of the National Anti-Corruption Bureau of Ukraine, the Director of the Bureau of Economic Security of Ukraine, the Director of the State Bureau of Investigation, the Commissioner of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine for Human Rights, the Chairman or another member of the Accounting Chamber, the Chairman of the National Bank of Ukraine, the leader of a political party, committed in connection with their state or public activity, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment with confiscation of property or without such.
{Article 112 as amended according to Laws No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 576-VIII of 02.07.2015, No. 1798-VIII of 21.12.2016, No. 1888-IX of 17.11.2021}
1. Committing explosions, arson or other acts aimed at mass destruction of people, infliction of bodily injuries or other harm to their health, destruction or damage of objects of important national economic or defense significance, for the purpose of weakening the state, as well as committing acts aimed at radioactive contamination, mass poisoning, spread of epidemics, epizootics or epiphytotics, for the same purpose, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same acts committed under martial law or during an armed conflict, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of fifteen years or life imprisonment, with confiscation of property.
{Article 113 as amended according to Laws No. 1183-VII of 08.04.2014, No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014; as worded by Law No. 2113-IX of 03.03.2022; as amended according to Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}
1. Transfer or collection for the purpose of transfer to a foreign state, foreign organization or their representatives of information constituting a state secret, if these acts are committed by a foreigner or a stateless person, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. A person who has ceased the activity provided for in part one of this article and voluntarily informed state authorities of what was committed shall be released from criminal liability, if as a result of this and the measures taken, harm to the interests of Ukraine was averted.
{Article 114 as amended according to Laws No. 1183-VII of 08.04.2014, No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014}

Article 114-1

Obstruction of lawful activities of the Armed Forces of Ukraine and other military formations
1. Obstruction of lawful activities of the Armed Forces of Ukraine and other military formations during a special period -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
2. The same act that resulted in the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years.
{Section I of the Special Part supplemented with Article 114-1 according to Law No. 1183-VII of 08.04.2014}

Article 114-2

Unauthorized dissemination of information about the direction, movement of weapons, armaments and ammunition to Ukraine, movement, displacement or deployment of the Armed Forces of Ukraine or other military formations formed in accordance with the laws of Ukraine, committed under martial law or state of emergency
1. Dissemination of information about the direction, movement of weapons, armaments and ammunition to Ukraine, including their movement through the territory of Ukraine, if such information was not posted (disseminated) in open access by the General Staff of the Armed Forces of Ukraine, the Ministry of Defense of Ukraine, the Main Intelligence Directorate of the Ministry of Defense of Ukraine or the Security Service of Ukraine or in official sources of partner countries, committed under martial law or state of emergency, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. Dissemination of information about the movement, displacement or location of the Armed Forces of Ukraine or other military formations formed in accordance with the laws of Ukraine, with the possibility of their identification on the ground, if such information was not posted in open access by the General Staff of the Armed Forces of Ukraine, the Ministry of Defense of Ukraine or other authorized state bodies, committed under martial law or state of emergency, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons or from mercenary motives, or with the purpose of providing such information to a state committing armed aggression against Ukraine, or its representatives, or other illegal armed formations, or if they caused grave consequences, in the absence of signs of state treason or espionage -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years.
{Section I of the Special Part supplemented with Article 114-2 according to Law No. 2160-IX of 24.03.2022; as amended according to Law No. 2178-IX of 01.04.2022}

Section II

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST LIFE AND HEALTH OF A PERSON
{Title of Section II as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. Murder, that is, intentional unlawful causing of death to another person, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to fifteen years.
2) of a minor child or a woman who was knowingly to the perpetrator in a state of pregnancy;
3) of a hostage or a kidnapped person;
4) committed with particular cruelty;
5) committed in a manner dangerous to the lives of many persons;
8) of a person or their close relative in connection with the performance by that person of official or public duty;
9) with the purpose of concealing another criminal offense or facilitating its commission;
10) combined with rape or sexual violence;
12) committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons;
13) committed by a person who previously committed intentional murder, except for murder provided for in Articles 116-118 of this Code;
14) from motives of racial, national or religious intolerance, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment, with confiscation of property in the case provided for in point 6 of part two of this article.
{Article 115 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1707-VI of 05.11.2009, No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 116. Intentional murder committed in a state of strong emotional disturbance

Intentional murder committed in a state of strong emotional disturbance caused by cruel treatment, or such that humiliates the honor and dignity of a person, and also in the presence of a systematic nature of such treatment by the victim, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 116 as amended according to Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017}

Article 117. Intentional murder by a mother of her newborn child

Intentional murder by a mother of her newborn child during childbirth or immediately after childbirth -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.

Article 118

Intentional murder in exceeding the limits of necessary defense or in case of exceeding the measures necessary for apprehension of a person who committed a criminal offense
Intentional murder committed in exceeding the limits of necessary defense, as well as in case of exceeding the measures necessary for apprehension of a person who committed a criminal offense, -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term of up to two years or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
{Article 118 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 119. Homicide through negligence

1. Homicide committed through negligence -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or by imprisonment for the same term.
2. Homicide of two or more persons committed through negligence -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.

Стаття 120. Driving to suicide

1. Driving a person to suicide or to an attempt at suicide, which is a consequence of cruel treatment of such person, blackmail, systematic humiliation of his or her human dignity or systematic unlawful coercion to actions contrary to his or her will, incitement to suicide, as well as other actions facilitating the commission of suicide, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term up to three years or by imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same act committed against a person who was in material or other dependence on the guilty person, or against two or more persons, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or by imprisonment for the same term.
3. An act provided for by parts one or two of this Article, if it was committed against a minor, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.
{Стаття 120 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 2292-VIII від 08.02.2018}

Стаття 121. Intentional grievous bodily injury

1. Intentional grievous bodily injury, that is, intentional bodily injury dangerous to life at the moment of infliction, or such that caused the loss of any organ or its functions, mutilation of sexual organs, mental illness or other health disorder combined with permanent loss of working capacity by not less than one third, or termination of pregnancy or irreparable disfigurement of the face, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
2. Intentional grievous bodily injury committed by a method having the character of special torture, or committed by a group of persons, as well as for the purpose of intimidating the victim or other persons, or from motives of racial, national or religious intolerance, or committed on order, or such that caused the death of the victim, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.
{Стаття 121 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 1707-VI від 05.11.2009, № 2227-VIII від 06.12.2017}

Стаття 122. Intentional medium gravity bodily injury

1. Intentional medium gravity bodily injury, that is, intentional injury which is not dangerous to life and did not entail the consequences provided for in Article 121 of this Code, but such that caused a prolonged health disorder or significant permanent loss of working capacity by less than one third, -
shall be punishable by correctional labour for a term up to two years or by restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or by imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts committed for the purpose of intimidating the victim or his or her relatives or coercing to certain actions or from motives of racial, national or religious intolerance, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for three to five years.
{Стаття 122 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 1707-VI від 05.11.2009}

Стаття 123

Intentional grievous bodily injury inflicted in a state of strong emotional excitement
Intentional grievous bodily injury committed in a state of strong emotional excitement caused by cruel treatment, or such that humiliates the honour and dignity of a person, as well as in the presence of a systematic character of such treatment by the victim, -
shall be punishable by community service for a term of one hundred fifty to two hundred forty hours or by correctional labour for a term up to two years, or by restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or by imprisonment for a term up to two years.
{Стаття 123 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 2227-VIII від 06.12.2017}

Стаття 124

Intentional infliction of grievous bodily injuries in case of exceeding the limits of necessary defence or in case of exceeding the measures necessary for apprehension of a person who committed a criminal offence
Intentional infliction of grievous bodily injuries committed in case of exceeding the limits of necessary defence or in case of exceeding the measures necessary for apprehension of a person who committed a criminal offence, -
shall be punishable by community service for a term of one hundred fifty to two hundred forty hours or by correctional labour for a term up to two years, or by arrest for a term up to six months, or by restraint of liberty for a term up to two years, or by imprisonment for the same term.
{Стаття 124 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018}

Стаття 125. Intentional light bodily injury

1. Intentional light bodily injury -
shall be punishable by a fine up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or by community service for a term up to two hundred hours, or by correctional labour for a term up to one year.
2. Intentional light bodily injury that caused a short-term health disorder or insignificant loss of working capacity, -
shall be punishable by a fine from fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or by community service for a term of one hundred fifty to two hundred forty hours or by correctional labour for a term up to one year, or by arrest for a term up to six months, or by restraint of liberty for a term up to two years.
{Стаття 125 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 270-VI від 15.04.2008}

Стаття 126. Battery and torture

1. Intentional infliction of a blow, battery or commission of other violent actions that caused physical pain and did not cause bodily injuries, -
shall be punishable by a fine up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or by community service for a term up to two hundred hours, or by correctional labour for a term up to one year.
2. The same acts having the character of torture, committed by a group of persons, or for the purpose of intimidating the victim or his or her relatives, or from motives of racial, national or religious intolerance, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or by imprisonment for the same term.
{Стаття 126 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 1707-VI від 05.11.2009}

Article 126-1. Domestic Violence

Domestic violence, that is, intentional systematic commission of physical, psychological or economic violence against a spouse or former spouse or another person with whom the perpetrator is (was) in family or close relations, which leads to physical or psychological suffering, health disorders, loss of working capacity, emotional dependence or deterioration of the quality of life of the victim, -
is punishable by community service for a term of one hundred and fifty to two hundred and forty hours, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 126-1 in accordance with Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017}
1. Torture, that is, any intentional act aimed at inflicting severe physical pain or mental suffering on a person, committed for the purpose of forcing that person or another person to commit acts contrary to their will, including obtaining information or confession, or for the purpose of punishing that person or another person for acts committed by that person or another person or of which that person or another person is suspected, or for the purpose of intimidating that person or other persons, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to six years.
2. The same act committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or for the purpose of discrimination, including on grounds of racial, national or religious intolerance, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this Article, committed by a representative of the state, including a foreign state, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Note 1. Representatives of the state in this Article and Article 146-1 of this Code shall mean officials, as well as persons acting as official persons, or acting at their instigation, or with their knowledge, or with their tacit consent.
2. Representatives of a foreign state in this Article and Article 146-1 of this Code shall mean persons acting as civil servants of a foreign state or serving in the armed forces, police bodies, state security bodies, intelligence bodies, or persons holding positions in the said or any other state bodies or local self-government bodies of a foreign state, established in accordance with its legislation, or acting on the orders of such persons, as well as representatives of irregular illegal armed formations, armed gangs and groups of mercenaries, formed, subordinated, controlled and financed by the Russian Federation, as well as representatives of the occupation administration of the Russian Federation, which includes its state bodies and structures functionally responsible for the administration of temporarily occupied territories of Ukraine, and representatives of self-proclaimed bodies controlled by the Russian Federation that have usurped the exercise of governmental functions in the temporarily occupied territories of Ukraine.
{Article 127 as amended by Laws No. 2322-IV of 12.01.2005, No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as amended in accordance with Law No. 1707-VI of 05.11.2009; as amended by Law No. 2812-IX of 01.12.2022}

Article 128. Negligent Grievous or Medium Gravity Bodily Injury

Negligent grievous or medium gravity bodily injury -
is punishable by community service for a term of one hundred and fifty to two hundred and forty hours or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 128 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. Threat of murder, if there were real grounds to fear the implementation of this threat, -
is punishable by arrest for a term of up to six months or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. The same act committed by a member of an organized group or on grounds of racial, national or religious intolerance, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
{Article 129 as amended in accordance with Law No. 1707-VI of 05.11.2009}

Article 130

Infection with Human Immunodeficiency Virus or Other Incurable Infectious Disease
1. Knowingly placing another person in danger of infection with human immunodeficiency virus or other incurable infectious disease that is dangerous to human life, -
is punishable by arrest for a term of up to three months or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. Infection of another person with human immunodeficiency virus or other incurable infectious disease by a person who knew that he or she was a carrier of this virus, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
3. Acts provided for in part two of this Article, committed against two or more persons or a minor, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
4. Intentional infection of another person with human immunodeficiency virus or other incurable infectious disease that is dangerous to human life, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.

Article 131

Improper Performance of Professional Duties that Caused Infection of a Person with Human Immunodeficiency Virus or Other Incurable Infectious Disease
1. Improper performance by a medical, pharmaceutical or other worker of his or her professional duties as a result of negligent or unconscientious attitude towards them, which caused infection of a person with human immunodeficiency virus or other incurable infectious disease that is dangerous to human life, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act, if it caused infection of two or more persons, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.

Article 132

Disclosure of Information about Conducting Medical Examination for Detection of Infection with Human Immunodeficiency Virus or Other Incurable Infectious Disease
Disclosure by an official of a medical institution, an auxiliary worker who independently obtained information, or a medical worker of information about conducting a medical examination of a person for detection of infection with human immunodeficiency virus or other incurable infectious disease that is dangerous to human life, or disease with acquired immunodeficiency syndrome (AIDS) and its results, which became known to them in connection with the performance of official or professional duties, -
is punishable by a fine of fifty to one hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of up to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.

Article 133. Infection with Venereal Disease

1. Infection of another person with a venereal disease by a person who knew about the presence of this disease in him or her, -
is punishable by correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed by a person previously convicted of infecting another person with a venereal disease, as well as infection of two or more persons or a minor, -
are punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, if they caused serious consequences, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.

This excerpt sits under: (Vidomosti Verkhovnoi Rady Ukrainy (VVR), 2001, No. 25-26, Art. 131) > Section II

Стаття 134. Illegal Abortion or Sterilization

1. Performing an abortion by a person who does not have specialized medical education, -
is punishable by a fine of fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred to two hundred forty hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Coercion to abortion without the voluntary consent of the victim, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
3. Illegal performance of an abortion that caused prolonged health disorder, infertility or death of the victim, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
4. Coercion to sterilization without the voluntary consent of the victim, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
5. The act provided for in part four of this article, if it caused the death of the victim or other grave consequences, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Стаття 134 в редакції Закону № 2227-VIII від 06.12.2017; із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018}

Стаття 135. Abandonment in Danger

1. Deliberate abandonment without assistance of a person who is in a life-threatening condition and is deprived of the ability to take measures for self-preservation due to infancy, old age, illness or as a result of another helpless condition, if the person who abandoned without assistance was obliged to care for this person and had the opportunity to provide assistance, as well as in the case when he himself put the victim in a life-threatening condition, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed by a mother in relation to a newborn child, if the mother was not in a condition caused by childbirth, -
are punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of a person or other grave consequences, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.

Стаття 136. Failure to Provide Assistance to a Person in a Life-Threatening Condition

1. Failure to provide assistance to a person who is in a life-threatening condition, when it is possible to provide such assistance, or failure to notify the appropriate institutions or persons about such condition of the person, if this caused serious bodily injuries, -
is punishable by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred fifty to two hundred forty hours, or arrest for a term of up to six months.
2. Failure to provide assistance to a minor who is knowingly in a life-threatening condition, when it is possible to provide such assistance, or failure to notify the appropriate institutions or persons about such condition of the child, -
is punishable by a fine of five hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of the victim, -
are punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
{Стаття 136 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 270-VI від 15.04.2008, № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018}

Стаття 137. Improper Performance of Duties Regarding the Protection of Life and Health of Children

1. Failure to perform or improper performance of professional or official duties regarding the protection of life and health of minors as a result of negligent or unconscientious attitude towards them, if this caused substantial harm to the health of the victim, -
is punishable by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts, if they caused the death of a minor or other grave consequences, -
are punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Стаття 137 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 2556-VI від 23.09.2010, № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018}

Стаття 138. Illegal Medical Practice

Engaging in medical practice without special permission, carried out by a person who does not have proper medical education, if this caused grave consequences for the patient, -
is punishable by correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Стаття 139. Failure to Provide Assistance to a Patient by a Medical Worker

1. Failure to provide assistance to a patient without valid reasons by a medical worker who is obliged, in accordance with established rules, to provide such assistance, if he is knowingly aware that this may have grave consequences for the patient, -
is punishable by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or community service for a term of up to two hundred hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to two years.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of the patient or other grave consequences, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to four years or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Стаття 139 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 270-VI від 15.04.2008}

Стаття 140

Improper Performance of Professional Duties by a Medical or Pharmaceutical Worker
1. Failure to perform or improper performance by a medical or pharmaceutical worker of their professional duties as a result of negligent or unconscientious attitude towards them, if this caused grave consequences for the patient, -
is punishable by deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same act, if it caused grave consequences to a minor, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.

Стаття 141. Violation of Patient Rights

Conducting clinical trials of medicinal products without the written consent of the patient or their legal representative, or in relation to a minor or incapacitated person, if these actions caused the death of the patient or other grave consequences, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Стаття 141 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 270-VI від 15.04.2008}

Стаття 142. Illegal Conduct of Experiments on a Human Being

1. Illegal conduct of medical-biological, psychological or other experiments on a human being, if it created danger to his or her life or health, -
shall be punishable by a fine of up to two hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to four years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed against a minor, two or more persons, by coercion or deception, and also if they caused prolonged impairment of the victim's health, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Article 142 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 143

Violation of the Procedure for Transplantation of Human Anatomical Materials Established by Law
1. Intentional violation of the procedure for application of transplantation of human anatomical materials established by law, which caused substantial harm to the victim's health, -
shall be punishable by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. Removal from a human being by coercion or deception of his or her anatomical materials for the purpose of their transplantation -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of up to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in part two of this Article, committed against a person who was in a helpless state or in material or other dependence on the guilty person, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
4. Illegal trafficking in human anatomical materials -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
5. Acts provided for in parts two, three or four of this Article, committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or participation in transnational organizations engaged in such activities, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 143 as amended by Law No. 2427-VIII of 17.05.2018; as amended according to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 418-IX of 20.12.2019, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Стаття 144. Forced Donation

1. Forced or by deception removal of blood from a human being for the purpose of using him or her as a donor -
shall be punishable by deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, with a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or without such.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed against a minor or a person who was in a helpless state or in material dependence on the guilty person, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
3. Acts provided for in parts one and two of this Article, committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons or for the purpose of sale, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of up to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.

Стаття 145. Illegal Disclosure of Medical Secrecy

Intentional disclosure of medical secrecy by a person to whom it became known in connection with the performance of professional or official duties, if such act caused grave consequences, -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of up to two hundred and forty hours, or deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years, or correctional labour for a term of up to two years.
{Article 145 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Розділ III

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST LIBERTY, HONOUR AND DIGNITY OF A PERSON
{Title of Section III as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 146. Illegal Deprivation of Liberty or Abduction of a Person

1. Illegal deprivation of liberty or abduction of a person -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed against a minor or from mercenary motives, against two or more persons or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or in a manner dangerous to the life or health of the victim, or such that was accompanied by infliction of physical suffering on him or her, or with the use of a weapon, or carried out for a prolonged time, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by an organized group, or such that caused grave consequences, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.

Стаття 146-1. Enforced Disappearance

1. Arrest, detention, abduction or deprivation of liberty of a person in any other form, committed by a representative of the state, including a foreign one, with subsequent refusal to acknowledge the fact of such arrest, detention, abduction or deprivation of liberty of a person in any other form or concealment of data about the fate of such person or place of stay -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. Issuance of an order or instruction to commit acts specified in part one of this Article, or failure by a manager who became aware of the commission of acts specified in part one of this Article by his or her subordinates to take measures to stop them and failure to notify competent authorities about the crime -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 146-1 according to Law No. 2505-VIII of 12.07.2018; as amended according to Law No. 2812-IX of 01.12.2022}

Article 147. Hostage-taking

1. Seizure or holding of a person as a hostage with the purpose of inducing relatives of the detained person, a state or other institution, enterprise or organization, a natural or official person to commit or refrain from committing any action as a condition for the release of the hostage -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
2. The same acts, if they were committed against a minor or by an organized group, or were combined with a threat to destroy people, or such as caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to fifteen years.
Substitution of another person's child, committed from mercenary or other personal motives, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
1. Trafficking in human beings, as well as recruitment, transportation, harbouring, transfer or receipt of a person, committed for the purpose of exploitation, using coercion, abduction, deception, blackmail, material or other dependence of the victim, his or her vulnerable state or bribery of a third person who controls the victim, to obtain consent for his or her exploitation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed against a minor or against several persons, or repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official using his or her official position, or combined with violence that is not dangerous to the life or health of the victim or his or her relatives, or with a threat of using such violence, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed against a minor by his or her parents, adoptive parents, guardians or custodians, or committed against a young child, or by an organized group, or combined with violence dangerous to the life or health of the victim or his or her relatives, or with a threat of using such violence, or if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
Note. 1. Exploitation of a person in this article shall mean all forms of sexual exploitation, use in the pornography business, forced labour or forced provision of services, slavery or customs similar to slavery, servitude, involvement in debt bondage, removal of organs, conducting experiments on a person without his or her consent, adoption for the purpose of profit, forced pregnancy or forced termination of pregnancy, forced marriage, forced involvement in begging, involvement in criminal activity, use in armed conflicts, etc.
2. In articles 149 and 303 of this Code, the vulnerable state of a person shall mean a state of a person caused by physical or mental characteristics or external circumstances, which deprives or limits his or her ability to be aware of his or her actions (inaction) or to control them, to make independent decisions of his or her own will, to resist violent or other unlawful acts, the coincidence of grave personal, family or other circumstances.
3. Liability for recruitment, transportation, harbouring, transfer or receipt of a young child or minor under this article arises regardless of whether such acts were committed using coercion, abduction, deception, blackmail or the vulnerable state of the said persons or with the use or threat of use of violence, use of official position, or by a person on whom the victim was materially or otherwise dependent, or bribery of a third person who controls the victim, to obtain his or her consent for the exploitation of a person.
{Article 149 as amended by Laws No. 3316-IV of 12.01.2006, No. 2539-VIII of 06.09.2018}

Article 150. Exploitation of children

1. Exploitation of a child who has not reached the age from which employment is permitted by law, by using his or her labour -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, committed against several children or if they caused substantial harm to the health, physical development or educational level of the child, or combined with the use of child labour in harmful production, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 150 as amended by Law No. 3571-VI of 05.07.2011}

Article 150-1. Use of a young child for begging

1. Use by parents or persons replacing them of a young child for begging (systematic solicitation of money, things, other material valuables from strangers) -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts, committed against another person's young child or related to the use of violence or threat of its use, as well as committed repeatedly or by a person who previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in articles 150, 303, 304 of this Code, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group, as well as if as a result of such acts medium gravity or grave bodily injuries were caused to the child, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
{The Code was supplemented with article 150-1 by Law No. 894-VI of 15.01.2009; as amended by Laws No. 3571-VI of 05.07.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 151. Unlawful placement in an institution providing psychiatric care

1. Placement in an institution providing psychiatric care of a person known to be mentally healthy -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of three to six months or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act that caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.

Article 151-1. {Article 151-1 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 151-2. Coercion into marriage

1. Coercion of a person to enter into marriage or to continue a forcibly concluded marriage, or to enter into cohabitation without concluding a marriage, or to continue such cohabitation, or inducement for this purpose of a person to move to the territory of another state than the one in which he or she resides, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or against a person who has not reached marriageable age according to the law, or against two or more persons, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Section III of the Special Part was supplemented with article 151-2 by Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017}

Розділ IV

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST SEXUAL FREEDOM AND SEXUAL INTEGRITY OF A PERSON
{Title of Section IV as amended pursuant to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. Commission of acts of a sexual nature involving vaginal, anal or oral penetration into the body of another person using genitals or any other object, without the voluntary consent of the victim (rape) -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. Rape committed repeatedly or by a person who has previously committed any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 153-155 of this Code, or commission of such acts against a spouse or former spouse or another person with whom the perpetrator is (was) in family or close relations, or against a person in connection with the performance by that person of official, professional or public duty, or against a woman who was knowingly to the perpetrator in a state of pregnancy, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
3. Rape committed by a group of persons, or rape of a minor person -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years.
4. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed against a person who has not reached the age of fourteen, regardless of his or her voluntary consent, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years.
5. Acts provided for in parts one, two or three of this Article that caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years.
6. Acts provided for in part four of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a person who has previously committed any of the crimes provided for in parts four or five of Article 153, Article 155 or part two of Article 156 of this Code, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of fifteen years or life imprisonment.
Note: Consent is considered voluntary if it is the result of a person's free expression of will, taking into account the accompanying circumstances.
{Article 152 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2295-VI of 01.06.2010; as worded by Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017; as amended pursuant to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 409-IX of 19.12.2019}

Стаття 153. Sexual violence

1. Commission of any violent acts of a sexual nature not involving penetration into the body of another person, without the voluntary consent of the victim (sexual violence) -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2. Sexual violence committed repeatedly or by a person who has previously committed any of the criminal offenses provided for in Articles 152, 154, 155 of this Code, or commission of such acts against a spouse or former spouse or another person with whom the perpetrator is (was) in family or close relations, or against a person in connection with the performance by that person of official, professional or public duty, or against a woman who was knowingly to the perpetrator in a state of pregnancy, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
3. Sexual violence committed by a group of persons, or sexual violence against a minor person -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years.
4. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed against a person who has not reached the age of fourteen, regardless of his or her voluntary consent -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
5. Acts provided for in parts one, two, three or four of this Article that caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years.
6. Acts provided for in part four of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a person who has previously committed any of the criminal offenses provided for in part four of Article 152, Article 155 or part two of Article 156 of this Code, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of fifteen years or life imprisonment.
{Article 153 as amended pursuant to Laws No. 2276-IV of 21.12.2004, No. 2295-VI of 01.06.2010; as worded by Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017; as amended pursuant to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 409-IX of 19.12.2019, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Стаття 154. Coercion to engage in sexual intercourse

1. Coercion of a person without his or her voluntary consent to perform an act of a sexual nature with another person -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months.
2. Coercion of a person without his or her voluntary consent to perform an act of a sexual nature with a person on whom the victim is materially or officially dependent, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this Article, combined with a threat of destruction, damage or seizure of property of the victim or his or her close relatives, or with a threat of disclosure of information that disgraces him or her or close relatives, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 154 as amended pursuant to Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017}

Стаття 155

Commission of acts of a sexual nature with a person who has not reached the age of sixteen
1. Commission by an adult person of acts of a sexual nature involving vaginal, anal or oral penetration into the body of a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, using genitals, another organ or part of the body or any object, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed by close relatives or family members, a person entrusted with duties regarding the upbringing of the victim or care for him or her, or if they are combined with the provision of monetary or other remuneration to the victim or a third person or with a promise of such remuneration, or if they caused infertility or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
Note. In Articles 155, 156 of this Code, close relatives or family members shall mean persons defined in paragraph 1 of part one of Article 3 of the Criminal Procedure Code of Ukraine.
{Article 155 as amended pursuant to Law No. 600-VI of 25.09.2008; as worded by Laws No. 2334-VIII of 14.03.2018, No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}

Стаття 156. Debauchery of minors

1. Commission of lewd acts against a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Paragraph two of part one of Article 156 as worded by Law No. 600-VI of 25.09.2008}
2. The same acts committed against a minor person or committed by family members or close relatives, a person entrusted with duties regarding the upbringing of the victim or care for him or her, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Part two of Article 156 as worded by Laws No. 600-VI of 25.09.2008, No. 2334-VIII of 14.03.2018}

This excerpt sits under: (Відомості Верховної Ради України (ВВР), 2001, № 25-26, ст.131)

Стаття 156-1. Solicitation of a child for sexual purposes

1. A proposal for a meeting made by an adult, including through the use of information and telecommunication systems or technologies, to a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, with the purpose of committing any actions of a sexual nature or lewd acts against them, where at least one action directed at ensuring such a meeting takes place was committed following such proposal, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. A proposal for a meeting made by an adult, including through the use of information and telecommunication systems or technologies, to a minor with the purpose of involving them in the production of child pornography, where at least one action directed at ensuring such a meeting takes place was committed following such proposal, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or against a young child, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to six years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Note. 1. A meeting in this Article shall be understood to include, inter alia, a meeting the conduct of which involves the use of information and telecommunication systems or technologies.
2. Child pornography in this Article and Article 301-1 of this Code shall be understood to mean any depiction of a child or a person who appears to be a child in a real or simulated explicitly sexual image or engaged in real or simulated explicitly sexual conduct, or any depiction of a child's sexual organs for sexual purposes.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 156-1 in accordance with Law No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}

Розділ V

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST ELECTORAL, LABOUR AND OTHER PERSONAL RIGHTS AND FREEDOMS OF MAN AND CITIZEN
{The title of Section V as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 157

Obstruction of the exercise of the right to vote or the right to participate in a referendum, the work of an electoral commission or referendum commission, or the activities of an official observer
1. Obstruction of the free exercise by a voter or referendum participant of their right to vote or right to participate in a referendum, obstruction of the activities of another subject of the electoral process, referendum process, referendum initiative group, referendum commission, member of an electoral commission, member of a referendum initiative group, member of a referendum commission or official observer in the performance of their powers or exercise of their rights, combined with deception or coercion, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same act combined with the use of violence, destruction or damage to property, or threat of use of violence or destruction or damage to property, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of two to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or by a member of an electoral commission or member of a referendum commission, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
4. Unlawful interference by an official in the activities of an electoral commission, referendum commission or member of an electoral commission, referendum commission, using their official position with the purpose of influencing their actions or decisions -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
5. Evasion by a member of an electoral commission or member of a referendum commission of the performance of their duties in the work of the commission without valid reasons, which resulted in the impossibility of the work of the electoral commission, referendum commission on voting day, conducting the vote count at a polling station or referendum station, establishing the results of voting in the respective electoral district or referendum district, establishing the results of elections or referendum, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
{Article 157 as worded by Law No. 3504-IV of 23.02.2006; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1616-VI of 21.08.2009, No. 1703-VII of 14.10.2014; as worded by Law No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020}

Стаття 158

Provision of false information to the body maintaining the State Register of Voters or other unauthorised interference in the operation of the State Register of Voters
1. Intentional submission to the body maintaining the State Register of Voters of false information about voters, intentional entry of false information into the database of the State Register of Voters, unauthorised actions with information contained in the database of the State Register of Voters, or other unauthorised interference in the operation of the database of the State Register of Voters -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to four years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, by a member of an electoral commission or referendum commission, by an official using their official position, by an authorised representative of a candidate for the post of President of Ukraine, representative of a party, representative of a subject of the all-Ukrainian referendum process in the Central Electoral Commission, authorised person of a political party, representative of a party organisation in an electoral commission, authorised persons of a subject of the all-Ukrainian referendum process, member of a referendum initiative group, candidate, authorised person of a candidate, or where such acts resulted in the impossibility of establishing the results of voting at a polling station or referendum station, the results of voting in the respective electoral district or referendum district, or in the recognition of voting at a polling station or referendum station as invalid, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
{Article 158 as amended in accordance with Law No. 744-IV of 15.05.2003; as worded by Law No. 3504-IV of 23.02.2006; as amended in accordance with Law No. 698-V of 22.02.2007; as worded by Laws No. 1703-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020}

Article 158-1

Illegal use of a ballot paper, referendum ballot paper, voting by a voter, referendum participant more than once, theft, damage, concealment or destruction of a ballot paper, referendum ballot paper
1.
