Provision or receipt of a ballot paper or referendum ballot paper by a person who does not have the right to provide or receive it, or provision to a voter, referendum participant of a completed ballot paper or referendum ballot paper, or voting by a voter, referendum participant more than once, illegal insertion of a ballot paper or referendum ballot paper into a ballot box -
shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to three hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. Theft, damage, concealment or destruction of a ballot paper, referendum ballot paper -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to four hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, by a candidate, member of an election commission or member of a referendum commission, authorised representative of a candidate, authorised person of a political party, representative of a party organisation in an election commission, authorised person of a subject of the all-Ukrainian referendum process, member of a referendum initiative group, official observer, or if such actions led to the impossibility of conducting vote counting at a polling station or referendum station or to the recognition of voting at a polling station or referendum station as invalid, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
Note. The subject of the criminal offence defined by part two of this article shall not be voters, referendum participants who committed actions provided for by this part in relation to their own ballot paper or referendum ballot paper.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 158-1 in accordance with Law No. 1616-VI of 21.08.2009; as amended in accordance with Law No. 879-VII of 13.03.2014; as worded by Law No. 1703-VII of 14.10.2014; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018; as worded by Law No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020}

Article 158-2

Illegal destruction or damage of election documentation or referendum documentation
1. Illegal destruction or damage of election documentation or referendum documentation in archival institutions or in the Central Election Commission after the conduct of elections or a referendum -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for a term of two to four years.
2. The same acts committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or by a member of an election commission or member of a referendum commission, or another official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
Note. In this article and Article 158-3 of this Code, election documentation shall mean voter lists, ballot papers, all resolutions, protocols, acts of election commissions, applications and submissions of members of election commissions, subjects of the electoral process, other documents that are adopted (drawn up) by election commissions and/or submitted to election commissions during the electoral process and are subject to storage after the conduct of elections in state archival institutions or in the Central Election Commission of Ukraine.
In this article and Article 158-3 of this Code, referendum documentation shall mean lists of referendum participants, referendum ballot papers, control coupons of referendum ballot papers, all resolutions, protocols, acts, applications and submissions of members of referendum commissions, other documents that are adopted (drawn up) by referendum commissions or submitted to referendum commissions during the conduct of a referendum, and are subject to storage after the conduct of a referendum in archival institutions or in the Central Election Commission.
{The Code was supplemented with an article in accordance with Law No. 3169-IV of 01.12.2005; as amended in accordance with Law No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020}

Article 158-3

Falsification, forgery, theft, damage or destruction of election documentation, referendum documentation, theft, damage, concealment, destruction of a seal of an election commission, referendum commission, ballot box, voter list or referendum participant list
1. Signing by the chairman, deputy chairman, secretary, other member of an election commission, referendum commission of a protocol on vote counting of voters at a polling station or referendum station, a protocol on voting results within the respective electoral district, referendum district or on election or referendum results before the final completion of the protocol, as well as signing such a protocol not at a meeting of the election commission or referendum commission -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to six years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
2. Forgery or illegal production of election documentation, referendum documentation, use or storage of illegally produced or forged election documentation, referendum documentation, as well as inclusion in election documentation or referendum documentation of knowingly false information -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
3. Theft, damage, concealment, destruction of a seal of an election commission, referendum commission, ballot box with ballots, voter list or referendum participant list or protocol on vote counting of voters or referendum participants, on voting results within the respective electoral district in elections or referendum, on election or referendum results -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
4. Acts provided for in parts one, two or three of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official using their official position, or if such actions led to the impossibility of conducting vote counting at a polling station or referendum station, establishing voting results in the respective electoral district or referendum district, establishing election or referendum results or to the recognition of voting at a polling station or referendum station as invalid, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
5. A person shall be released from criminal liability for a crime provided for in part one of this article if they voluntarily, before being brought to criminal liability, reported the commission of the crime and facilitated its disclosure.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 158-3 in accordance with Law No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020}

Article 159. Violation of voting secrecy

1. Intentional violation of voting secrecy during elections or a referendum, expressed in the disclosure of the content of the will of a voter or referendum participant, -
is punishable by a fine of three hundred to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. The same act committed by a member of an election commission or a member of a referendum commission, a candidate, an authorized person of a political party, a representative of a party organization in an election commission, an authorized person of a subject of the all-Ukrainian referendum process, an authorized person of a candidate, a member of a referendum initiative group, an official observer or an official using their official position, -
is punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
{Article 159 as amended by Laws No. 3504-IV of 23.02.2006, No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020}

Article 159-1

Violation of the procedure for financing a political party, pre-election campaigning or referendum campaigning
1. Submission of knowingly false information in a political party's report on property, income, expenses and financial obligations or in a financial report on the receipt and use of funds from the electoral fund of a political party, local organization of a political party, candidate -
is punishable by a fine of one hundred to three hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
2. Intentional making of a contribution in support of a political party by a person who does not have the right to do so, or on behalf of a legal entity that does not have the right to do so, intentional making of a contribution in favor of a political party by a natural person or on behalf of a legal entity in a large amount, intentional provision of financial (material) support for pre-election campaigning or referendum campaigning by a natural person or on behalf of a legal entity in a large amount or by a person who does not have the right to do so, or on behalf of a legal entity that does not have the right to do so, as well as intentional receipt of a contribution in favor of a political party from a person who does not have the right to make such a contribution, or in a large amount, intentional receipt in a large amount of financial (material) support in conducting pre-election campaigning or referendum campaigning, intentional receipt of such financial (material) support from a person who does not have the right to provide such financial (material) support, -
are punishable by a fine of one hundred to three hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed repeatedly, -
are punishable by a fine of three hundred to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
4. Acts provided for in part two of this article, committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, an organized group or combined with extortion of a contribution or financial (material) support in conducting pre-election campaigning or referendum campaigning, -
are punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
Note. A large amount in this article means the amount of monetary funds, value of property, benefits, services, loans, advantages, intangible assets, any other benefits of a non-material or non-monetary nature that exceeds the maximum amount of contribution in support of a political party or the maximum amount of financial (material) support for pre-election campaigning or referendum campaigning established by law.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 159-1 in accordance with Law No. 3504-IV of 23.02.2006; as amended in accordance with Law No. 1703-VII of 14.10.2014; as amended by Law No. 731-VIII of 08.10.2015; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Стаття 160

Bribery of a voter, referendum participant, member of an election commission or referendum commission
1. Acceptance of a proposal, promise or receipt by a voter, referendum participant, member of an election commission or referendum commission, official observer for themselves or a third person of unlawful benefit for committing or not committing any actions related to the direct exercise of their electoral rights, rights to participate in a referendum, -
shall be punished by a fine of three hundred to four hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Proposal, promise or provision to a voter or referendum participant, candidate, member of an election commission or referendum commission of unlawful benefit for committing or not committing any actions related to the direct exercise of their electoral right, right to participate in a referendum, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for a term of two to six years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
3. Provision to voters, referendum participants, legal entities of unlawful benefit accompanied by pre-election campaigning or referendum campaigning, mentioning the name of a candidate, name of a political party that nominated a candidate in the relevant elections, or use of the image of a candidate, symbols of a political party that nominated a candidate in the relevant elections, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years or imprisonment for a term of two to six years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
4. Actions provided for in part two or three of this article, committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons or by a member of an election commission or referendum commission, member of a referendum initiative group, candidate or their authorized person, another person at the request of a candidate or on behalf of a candidate or political party that nominated them, authorized person of a political party, representative of a political party organization in an election commission, authorized person of a subject of the all-Ukrainian referendum process, as well as an official observer, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of five years.
5. A person, except for the organizer and instigator of a criminal offence, shall be released from liability for a criminal offence provided for in this article if they voluntarily, before being brought to criminal liability, reported the commission of the crime and facilitated its disclosure.
Note. In this article, unlawful benefit shall be understood as food products, alcoholic beverages and tobacco products, regardless of their value, as well as funds, goods (except campaign materials defined by law), services, works, privileges, advantages, gift certificates, securities, loans, lottery tickets, other tangible and intangible assets, the value of which exceeds 0.06 tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, which are offered, promised, provided to candidates, voters, referendum participants or members of an election commission, referendum commission on a gratuitous or other preferential basis or are received by the specified persons.
{Article 160 as amended by Law No. 1703-VII of 14.10.2014; as amended by Law No. 1791-VIII of 20.12.2016; as amended by Law No. 805-IX of 16.07.2020}

Стаття 161

Violation of equality of citizens depending on their racial, national, regional affiliation, religious beliefs, disability and other grounds
1. Intentional actions aimed at inciting national, regional, racial or religious enmity and hatred, at humiliating national honour and dignity, or insulting the feelings of citizens in connection with their religious beliefs, as well as direct or indirect restriction of rights or establishment of direct or indirect privileges of citizens on grounds of race, colour, political, religious and other beliefs, sex, disability, ethnic and social origin, property status, place of residence, linguistic or other grounds -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same actions combined with violence, fraud or threats, as well as committed by an official, -
shall be punished by a fine of five hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of two to five years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, which were committed by an organized group of persons or caused serious consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
Note. In this article, regional affiliation shall be understood as a person's affiliation by birth or residence to a region - part of the territory of Ukraine or territory of compact settlement of Ukrainians outside the territory of Ukraine - which differs from other territories by a number of historical, geographical, linguistic and other characteristics.
{Article 161 as amended by Law No. 1707-VI of 05.11.2009; as amended by Laws No. 1519-VII of 18.06.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2110-IX of 03.03.2022}

Стаття 162. Violation of inviolability of dwelling

1. Unlawful entry into a dwelling or other possession of a person, unlawful conduct of inspection or search therein, as well as unlawful eviction or other actions that violate the inviolability of citizens' dwellings, -
shall be punished by a fine of fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same actions committed by an official or with the use of violence or threat of its use, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
{Article 162 as amended by Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended by Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Стаття 163

Violation of secrecy of correspondence, telephone conversations, telegraphic or other correspondence transmitted by means of communication or through a computer
1. Violation of secrecy of correspondence, telephone conversations, telegraphic or other correspondence transmitted by means of communication or through a computer, -
shall be punished by a fine of fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for up to three years.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or against state or public figures, a journalist, or committed by an official, or with the use of special means intended for covert removal of information, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
{Article 163 as amended by Law No. 993–VIII of 04.02.2016}

Стаття 164. Evasion of payment of alimony for the maintenance of children

1. Malicious evasion of payment of funds established by a court decision for the maintenance of children (alimony), as well as malicious evasion by parents of maintaining minor or incapacitated children who are dependent on them, -
shall be punished by community service for a term of eighty to one hundred and twenty hours, or arrest for a term of up to three months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. The same act committed by a person previously convicted of a criminal offense provided for by this Article, -
shall be punished by community service for a term of one hundred and twenty to two hundred and forty hours, or arrest for a term of three to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of two to three years.
Note. In Articles 164 and 165 of this Code, malicious evasion of payment of funds for the maintenance of children (alimony) or for the maintenance of incapacitated parents shall be understood as any actions of the debtor aimed at non-execution of a court decision (concealment of income, change of place of residence or place of work without notifying the state executor, private executor, etc.), which have led to the occurrence of arrears in payment of such funds in an amount that cumulatively constitutes the sum of payments for three months of the respective payments.
{Article 164 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2456-IV of 03.03.2005, No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2677-VI of 04.11.2010, No. 1404-VIII of 02.06.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 165. Evasion of payment of funds for the maintenance of incapacitated parents

1. Malicious evasion of payment of funds established by a court decision for the maintenance of incapacitated parents -
shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to two hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, or community service for a term of eighty to one hundred and twenty hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to one year, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. The same act committed by a person previously convicted of a criminal offense provided for by this Article, -
shall be punished by community service for a term of one hundred and twenty to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of two to three years.
{Article 165 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2456-IV of 03.03.2005, No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 166

Malicious failure to fulfill duties of care for a child or for a person in respect of whom guardianship or trusteeship has been established
Malicious failure by parents, guardians or trustees to fulfill duties established by law of care for a child or for a person in respect of whom guardianship or trusteeship has been established, which has caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.

Стаття 167. Abuse of guardianship rights

Use of guardianship or trusteeship for mercenary purposes to the detriment of the ward (occupation of living space, use of property, etc.) -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Paragraph two of Article 167 as worded by Law No. 2556-VI of 23.09.2010; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 168. Disclosure of the secret of adoption

1. Disclosure of the secret of adoption contrary to the will of the adopter -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, or community service for a term of up to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to two years.
2. The same act committed by an official or an employee of a medical institution to whom information about the adoption became known in the course of service or work, or if it caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years, or without such.
{Article 168 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 169. Illegal actions regarding adoption

1. Illegal intermediary activity or other illegal actions regarding adoption of a child, transfer of the child under guardianship (trusteeship) or for upbringing in a family of citizens -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Paragraph two of part one of Article 169 as worded by Law No. 1452-VI of 04.06.2009}
2. The same acts committed in respect of several children, repeatedly, by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, with use of official position, or if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
{Paragraph two of part two of Article 169 as worded by Law No. 1452-VI of 04.06.2009}
{Article 169 as amended in accordance with Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Стаття 170

Obstruction of lawful activity of trade unions, political parties, public organizations
Intentional obstruction of lawful activity of trade unions, political parties, public organizations or their bodies -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.

Стаття 171. Obstruction of lawful professional activity of journalists

1. Illegal seizure of materials collected, processed, prepared by a journalist and technical means which he or she uses in connection with his or her professional activity, illegal refusal to grant a journalist access to information, illegal prohibition of coverage of certain topics, showing of certain persons, criticism of a subject of power, as well as any other intentional obstruction of a journalist's lawful professional activity -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. Influence in any form on a journalist with the purpose of obstructing the performance of his or her professional duties or persecution of a journalist in connection with his or her lawful professional activity -
shall be punished by a fine of up to two hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to four years.
3. Acts provided for by part two of this Article, if they were committed by an official with use of his or her official position or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years, or without such.
{Article 171 as worded by Law No. 993–VIII of 04.02.2016}

Article 172. Gross violation of labour legislation

1. Unlawful dismissal of an employee from work for personal motives or in connection with their reporting as a whistleblower of the commission by another person of a corruption or corruption-related offence, other violations of the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption", as well as other gross violation of labour legislation -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years, or correctional labour for a term of up to two years.
2. The same acts, if they were committed repeatedly, or against a minor, a pregnant woman, a single father, mother or person replacing them and raising a child under the age of 14 or a child with a disability, -
shall be punished by a fine of three thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years, or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months.
{Article 172 as amended by Law No. 77-VIII of 28.12.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2581-VIII of 02.10.2018, No. 140-IX of 02.10.2019, No. 198-IX of 17.10.2019}

Article 173. Gross violation of a labour agreement

1. Gross violation of a labour agreement by an official of an enterprise, institution, organisation regardless of the form of ownership, as well as by an individual citizen or a person authorised by them through deception or abuse of trust or coercion to perform work not stipulated by the agreement, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. The same acts committed against a citizen with whom an agreement has been concluded regarding their work outside Ukraine, -
shall be punished by a fine of fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.

Article 174. Coercion to participate in a strike or obstruction of participation in a strike

Coercion to participate in a strike or obstruction of participation in a strike by violence or threat of violence or by other unlawful acts -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred and twenty to two hundred and forty hours, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{Article 174 as amended in accordance with Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 175

Non-payment of wages, scholarships, pensions or other payments established by law
1. Unjustified non-payment of wages, scholarships, pensions or other payments established by law to citizens for more than one month, committed intentionally by the head of an enterprise, institution or organisation regardless of the form of ownership or by a citizen who is a business entity, -
shall be punished by a fine of five hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act, if it was committed as a result of misuse of funds intended for payment of wages, scholarships, pensions and other payments established by law, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to one and a half thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. A person shall be released from criminal liability if, before being brought to criminal liability, they have made payment of wages, scholarships, pensions or other payments established by law to citizens.
{Article 175 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1027-VI of 19.02.2009, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 176. Violation of copyright and related rights

1. Unlawful reproduction, use and distribution of works of science, literature and art, computer programs and databases, other works, as well as unlawful reproduction, use and distribution of performances, phonograms, videograms and broadcasting programs, their unlawful duplication and distribution on audio and video cassettes, diskettes, other information carriers, camcording, cardsharing or other intentional violation of copyright and related rights, as well as financing of such acts, if this caused material damage on a significant scale, -
shall be punished by a fine of three hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. The same acts, if they were committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or caused material damage on a large scale, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to two thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or deprivation of liberty for a term of two to five years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by an official using their official position or by an organised group, or if they caused material damage on a particularly large scale, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of liberty for a term of three to six years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
Note. In Articles 176 and 177 of this Code, material damage is considered to be caused on a significant scale if its amount exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by twenty or more times, on a large scale - if its amount exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by two hundred or more times, and caused on a particularly large scale - if its amount exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
{Article 176 as amended by Law No. 850-IV of 22.05.2003; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 3423-IV of 09.02.2006, No. 1111-V of 31.05.2007, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 1977-VIII of 23.03.2017, No. 2803-IX of 01.12.2022}

Article 177

Violation of rights to an invention, utility model, industrial design, topology of an integrated circuit, plant variety, rationalisation proposal
1. Unlawful use of an invention, utility model, industrial design, topology of an integrated circuit, plant variety, rationalisation proposal, appropriation of authorship of them, or other intentional violation of the right to these objects, if this caused material damage on a significant scale, -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. The same acts, if they were committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or caused material damage on a large scale, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to two thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or deprivation of liberty for a term of two to five years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by an official using their official position or by an organised group, or if they caused material damage on a particularly large scale, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of liberty for a term of three to six years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Article 177 as amended by Law No. 850-IV of 22.05.2003; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 3423-IV of 09.02.2006, No. 1111-V of 31.05.2007, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016}

Article 178. Damage to Religious Structures or Houses of Worship

Damage to or destruction of a religious structure or house of worship -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of sixty to two hundred forty hours, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 178 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 179. Unlawful Retention, Desecration or Destruction of Religious Shrines

Unlawful retention, desecration or destruction of religious shrines -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of sixty to two hundred forty hours, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 179 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 180. Obstruction of the Performance of a Religious Rite

1. Unlawful obstruction of the performance of a religious rite that disrupted or threatened to disrupt a religious rite, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred twenty to two hundred forty hours, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Coercion of a clergyman by means of physical or psychological violence to conduct a religious rite -
shall be punished by a fine of up to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 180 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 181

Encroachment on the Health of Persons under the Pretext of Preaching Religious Doctrines or Performing Religious Rites
1. Organization of or leadership of a group whose activity is carried out under the pretext of preaching religious doctrines or performing religious rites and is associated with causing harm to the health of persons or sexual depravity, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts associated with involving minors in the activity of the group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.

Article 182. Violation of the Inviolability of Private Life

1. Unlawful collection, storage, use, destruction, dissemination of confidential information about a person or unlawful alteration of such information, except for cases provided for by other articles of this Code, -
shall be punished by a fine of five hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly, or if they caused substantial harm to the rights, freedoms and interests of a person protected by law, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of three to six months or restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
Note. Substantial harm in this article, if it consists in causing material losses, is considered to be such harm that exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by one hundred or more times.
Public, including through mass media, journalists, public associations, trade unions, notification by a person of information about the commission of a criminal or other offense, carried out in compliance with the requirements of the law, is not an act provided for by this article and does not entail criminal liability.
{Article 182 as amended by Law No. 3454-VI of 02.06.2011; as amended in accordance with Law No. 198-IX of 17.10.2019}

Article 183. Violation of the Right to Receive Education

1. Unlawful refusal to admit to an educational institution of any form of ownership -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. Unlawful demand for payment for education in state or municipal educational institutions -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 183 as amended in accordance with Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 184. Violation of the Right to Free Medical Care

1. Unlawful demand for payment for the provision of medical care in state or municipal health care institutions -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months.
2. Unlawful reduction of the network of state and municipal health care institutions -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years.

Розділ VI

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST PROPERTY
{Title of Section VI as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. Secret misappropriation of another's property (theft) -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of eighty to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years.
2. Theft committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of three to six months or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Theft combined with penetration into a dwelling, other premises or repository or that caused significant damage to the victim, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to six years.
4. Theft committed in large amounts or under conditions of martial law or state of emergency, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
5. Theft committed in especially large amounts or by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property.
Note. 1. In Articles 185, 186 and 189-191, a criminal offense is recognized as repeated if committed by a person who previously committed any of the criminal offenses provided for by these articles or Articles 187, 262 of this Code.
2. In Articles 185, 186, 189 and 190 of this Code, significant damage is recognized taking into account the financial situation of the victim and if losses in the amount of one hundred to two hundred and fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens were caused to him or her.
3. In Articles 185-191, 194 of this Code, a criminal offense is recognized as committed in large amounts if committed by one person or a group of persons in an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by two hundred and fifty or more times at the time of committing the criminal offense.
4. In Articles 185-187 and 189-191, 194 of this Code, a criminal offense is recognized as committed in especially large amounts if committed by one person or a group of persons in an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by six hundred or more times at the time of committing the criminal offense.
{Article 185 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1449-VI of 04.06.2009, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2117-IX of 03.03.2022}
1. Open misappropriation of another's property (robbery) -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred and twenty to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. Robbery combined with violence that is not dangerous to the life or health of the victim, or with a threat of application of such violence, or committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to six years.
3. Robbery combined with penetration into a dwelling, other premises or repository or that caused significant damage to the victim, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.
4. Robbery committed in large amounts or under conditions of martial law or state of emergency, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.
5. Robbery committed in especially large amounts or by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to thirteen years with confiscation of property.
{Article 186 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2117-IX of 03.03.2022}
1. Attack for the purpose of seizing another's property, combined with violence dangerous to the life or health of the person who suffered the attack, or with a threat of application of such violence (brigandage), -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
2. Brigandage committed by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or by a person who previously committed brigandage or banditry, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years with confiscation of property.
3. Brigandage combined with penetration into a dwelling, other premises or repository, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property.
4. Brigandage aimed at seizing property in large or especially large amounts or committed by an organized group or under conditions of martial law or state of emergency, or combined with infliction of grievous bodily injuries, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years with confiscation of property.
{Article 187 as amended according to Law No. 2117-IX of 03.03.2022}

Article 188. {Article 188 excluded on the basis of Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 188-1

Theft of water, electrical or thermal energy by means of its unauthorized use
1. Theft of hot or drinking water, electrical or thermal energy by means of its unauthorized use without metering devices, the measurement results of which are used for commercial settlements (if the use of metering devices is mandatory), or as a result of intentional damage to metering devices or in any other way, if such actions caused significant damage, -
is punishable by a fine from one hundred to two hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if they caused damage on a large scale, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Note. Damage provided for by this article is recognized as significant if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one hundred or more times, and on a large scale - if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by two hundred and fifty or more times.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 188-1 in accordance with Law No. 2598-IV of 31.05.2005; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2119-VIII of 22.06.2017}
1. Demand for transfer of another's property or right to property or commission of any actions of a property nature with a threat of violence against the victim or his close relatives, restriction of rights, freedoms or lawful interests of these persons, damage or destruction of their property or property under their management or protection, or disclosure of information that the victim or his close relatives wish to keep secret (extortion), -
is punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Extortion committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official using his official position, or with a threat of murder or infliction of grievous bodily harm, or with damage or destruction of property, or such that caused significant damage to the victim, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
3. Extortion combined with violence dangerous to life or health of a person, or such that caused property damage on a large scale, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property.
4. Extortion that caused property damage on an especially large scale, or committed by an organized group or under conditions of martial law or state of emergency, or combined with infliction of grievous bodily harm, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property.
{Article 189 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2117-IX of 03.03.2022}
1. Taking possession of another's property or acquisition of a right to property by means of deception or abuse of trust (fraud) -
is punishable by a fine from two thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of two hundred to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. Fraud committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or such that caused significant damage to the victim, -
is punishable by a fine from three thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of one to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. Fraud committed under conditions of martial law or state of emergency that caused significant damage to the victim, -
is punishable by a fine from four thousand to eight thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
4. Fraud committed on a large scale, or by means of illegal operations using electronic computing equipment, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
5. Fraud committed on an especially large scale or by an organized group, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with confiscation of property.
{Article 190 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018; as worded by Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Article 191

Misappropriation, embezzlement of property or taking possession of it by abuse of official position
1. Misappropriation or embezzlement of another's property that was entrusted to a person or was under his management, -
is punishable by a fine from two thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to four years, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. Misappropriation, embezzlement or taking possession of another's property by an official by abuse of his official position -
is punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
are punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for a term of three to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
4. Actions provided for by parts one, two or three of this article, if they were committed on a large scale or under conditions of martial law or state of emergency, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
5. Actions provided for by parts one, two, three or four of this article, if they were committed on an especially large scale or by an organized group, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
{Article 191 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2117-IX of 03.03.2022}

Article 192. Causing property damage by means of deception or abuse of trust

1. Causing significant property damage by means of deception or abuse of trust in the absence of signs of fraud -
is punishable by a fine of up to fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of up to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months.
2. The same acts committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or such that caused property damage on a large scale, -
are punishable by a fine from fifty to one hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
Note. In accordance with this article, property damage is recognized as significant if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by fifty or more times, and on a large scale - such that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one hundred or more times.
{Article 192 as amended in accordance with Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 193

Unlawful appropriation by a person of found property or another's property that accidentally came into his possession
1. Unlawful appropriation by a person of found or accidentally come into his possession another's property or treasure that has special historical, scientific, artistic or cultural value, -
is punishable by a fine from one hundred to one hundred and fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of up to two hundred and forty hours, or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months.
{Article 193 as worded by Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2518-VI of 09.09.2010}

Article 194. Intentional destruction or damage to property

1. Intentional destruction or damage to another person's property that caused damage in large amounts, -
is punishable by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term from one hundred twenty to two hundred forty hours, or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same act committed by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, or caused property damage in especially large amounts, or caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term from three to ten years.
{Article 194 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 194-1. Intentional damage to electric power facilities

1. Intentional damage or destruction of electric power facilities, if these actions led or could lead to disruption of normal operation of these facilities, or caused danger to human life, -
is punishable by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by a generally dangerous method, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term from three to ten years.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term from eight to fifteen years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 194-1 according to Law No. 2598-IV of 31.05.2005; as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 195. Threat of destruction of property

Threat of destruction of another person's property by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, if there were real grounds to fear the implementation of this threat, -
is punishable by a fine up to fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term from sixty to one hundred twenty hours, or correctional labor for a term up to one year, or arrest for a term up to six months.
{Article 195 as amended according to Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 196. Negligent destruction or damage to property

Negligent destruction or damage to another person's property that caused serious bodily injuries or death of people, -
is punishable by correctional labor for a term up to two years or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 196 as amended according to Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 197. Violation of obligations regarding protection of property

Failure to perform or improper performance by a person entrusted with storage or protection of another person's property of their obligations, if this caused grave consequences for the owner of the property, -
is punishable by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term from one hundred twenty to two hundred forty hours or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or restraint of liberty for the same term.
{Article 197 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 197-1. Unauthorized occupation of land plot and unauthorized construction

1. Unauthorized occupation of a land plot that caused significant damage to its lawful possessor or owner, -
is punishable by a fine from two hundred to three hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months.
2. Unauthorized occupation of a land plot committed by a person previously convicted of a criminal offense provided for by this article, or by a group of persons, or regarding land plots of especially valuable lands, lands in protection zones, sanitary protection zones, sanitary-protective zones or zones of special regime of land use, -
is punishable by restraint of liberty for a term from two to four years or imprisonment for a term up to two years.
3. Unauthorized construction of buildings or structures on an unlawfully occupied land plot specified in part one of this article, -
is punishable by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years.
4. Unauthorized construction of buildings or structures on an unlawfully occupied land plot specified in part two of this article, or committed by a person previously convicted of the same criminal offense or a criminal offense provided for by part three of this article, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term from one to three years.
Note. According to this article, damage provided for by part one of this article is recognized as significant if it exceeds one hundred or more times the non-taxable minimum income of citizens.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 197-1 according to Law No. 578-V of 11.01.2007; as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 198

Acquisition, receipt, storage or sale of property obtained by criminally unlawful means
Acquisition or receipt, storage or sale of property not promised in advance, knowingly obtained by criminally unlawful means in the absence of signs of legalization (laundering) of property obtained by criminally unlawful means, -
is punishable by arrest for a term up to six months or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 198 as amended according to Laws No. 430-IV of 16.01.2003, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Section VII

CRIMINAL OFFENSES IN THE SPHERE OF ECONOMIC ACTIVITY
{Title of Section VII as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 199

Manufacture, storage, acquisition, transportation, forwarding, importation into Ukraine for the purpose of use in sale of goods, sale or sale of counterfeit money, state securities existing in paper form, state lottery tickets, excise tax stamps or holographic protective elements
1. Manufacture, storage, acquisition, transportation, forwarding, importation into Ukraine for the purpose of use in sale of goods, sale, as well as sale of illegally manufactured, obtained or counterfeit excise tax stamps, holographic protective elements, counterfeit national currency of Ukraine in the form of banknotes or metal coins, foreign currency, state securities existing in paper form or state lottery tickets -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term from three to seven years.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or in a large amount, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term from five to ten years with confiscation of property.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group or in an especially large amount, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term from eight to twelve years with confiscation of property.
Note. Actions provided for by this article are considered committed in a large amount if the amount of counterfeiting exceeds two hundred or more times the non-taxable minimum income of citizens; in an especially large amount - if the amount of counterfeiting exceeds four hundred or more times the non-taxable minimum income of citizens.
{Article 199 as amended according to Laws No. 5283-VI of 18.09.2012, No. 71-VIII of 28.12.2014, No. 738-IX of 19.06.2020}

This excerpt sits under: (Відомості Верховної Ради України (ВВР), 2001, № 25-26, ст.131) > Розділ VI

Стаття 200

Illegal actions with documents for transfer, payment cards and other means of access to bank accounts, electronic money, equipment for their production
1. Forgery of documents for transfer, payment cards or other means of access to bank accounts, electronic money, as well as acquisition, storage, transportation, sending for the purpose of sale of forged documents for transfer, payment cards or their use or sale, as well as unlawful issuance or use of electronic money -
shall be punished by a fine from three to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by a fine from five to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
Note. Documents for transfer shall mean a document in paper or electronic form used by banks or their clients to transmit instructions or information for the transfer of funds between subjects of money transfer (settlement documents, documents for transfer of cash, as well as those used in conducting interbank transfer and payment notification, and others).
{Article 200 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 5284-VI of 18.09.2012}
1. Smuggling, that is, movement across the customs border of Ukraine outside customs control or with concealment from customs control of cultural valuables, poisonous, potent, explosive substances, radioactive materials, weapons or ammunition (except smooth-bore hunting weapons or ammunition therefor), parts of rifled firearms, as well as special technical means for covert obtaining of information, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
2. The same action committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or by a person previously convicted of smuggling, or by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
Note. 1. In Articles 201, 201-1, 201-3, 201-4, 305 of this Code, movement across the customs border of Ukraine of objects of smuggling outside customs control shall mean intentional movement of objects of smuggling outside the location of the customs authority or outside the working hours established for it, and without performance of customs formalities or movement of objects of smuggling with unlawful exemption from customs control.
2. In Articles 201, 201-1, 201-3, 201-4, 305 of this Code, movement across the customs border of Ukraine of objects of smuggling with concealment from customs control shall mean intentional movement of objects of smuggling using specially made hiding places (caches) and other means or methods that make their detection difficult, or by giving some goods the appearance of others, or by submitting to the customs authority as a basis for movement of goods forged documents or documents obtained by unlawful means, or those containing knowingly false information subject to mandatory declaration in accordance with legislation on customs matters, and if the said documents and information are necessary for determining the amount of customs payments due for payment and/or for confirming compliance with prohibitions and restrictions established by law regarding movement of goods across the customs border of Ukraine.
3. For the purposes of Articles 201, 201-1, 201-3, 201-4 of this Code, a person previously convicted of smuggling shall mean a person having a conviction for committing a crime provided for by Articles 201, 201-1, 201-3, 201-4, 305 of this Code.
{Article 201 as amended in accordance with Law No. 1071-V of 24.05.2007; as revised by Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2052-VIII of 18.05.2017, No. 3513-IX of 09.12.2023}
1. Smuggling, that is, movement across the customs border of Ukraine outside customs control or with concealment from customs control of timber or lumber of valuable and rare tree species, unprocessed timber, as well as other timber prohibited for export beyond the customs territory of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. The same action committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or by a person previously convicted of smuggling, or by an official using their official position, or on a large scale, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this Article, committed by an organized group or on an especially large scale, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
Note. 1. Valuable and rare tree species in this Article shall mean genera of trees provided for by Article 1 of the Law of Ukraine "On peculiarities of state regulation of activities of business entities related to sale and export of timber".
2. Movement on a large scale in this Article shall mean movement of timber or lumber of valuable and rare tree species, as well as unprocessed timber, the value of which exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by eighteen or more times, movement on an especially large scale - the value of which exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by thirty-six or more times.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 201-1 in accordance with Law No. 2531-VIII of 06.09.2018; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2708-VIII of 25.04.2019, No. 3513-IX of 09.12.2023}

Стаття 201-2

Illegal use of humanitarian aid, charitable donations or gratuitous assistance for the purpose of obtaining profit
1. Sale of goods (items) of humanitarian aid or use of charitable donations, gratuitous assistance or conclusion of other transactions regarding the disposal of such property, for the purpose of obtaining profit, committed in a significant amount, -
shall be punished by a fine of two to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to four years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official using their official position, or in a large amount, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed by an organized group or in a particularly large amount, or during a state of emergency or martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
1. Humanitarian aid, charitable donations and gratuitous assistance shall be understood as the concepts defined by the Law of Ukraine "On Humanitarian Aid" and the Law of Ukraine "On Charitable Activities and Charitable Organizations".
2. Acts provided for in this article shall be considered committed in a significant amount if the total value of such humanitarian aid, charitable donations or gratuitous assistance exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by three hundred and fifty times or more; in a large amount - if the total value of goods, gratuitous assistance or monetary aid exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand times or more; in a particularly large amount - if the total value of goods, gratuitous assistance or monetary aid exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by three thousand times or more.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 201-2 in accordance with Law No. 2155-IX of 24.03.2022; as amended in accordance with Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}
{Article 201-3 enters into force on July 1, 2024}
1. Smuggling, that is, movement across the customs border of Ukraine outside customs control or with concealment from customs control of goods (except excisable goods and electric energy), committed in a significant amount, -
shall be punished by a fine of ten thousand to twenty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The same act committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or by a person previously convicted of smuggling, or by an official using their official position, or in a large amount, -
shall be punished by a fine of fifty thousand to seventy-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of five to ten years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to eleven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
Note. Acts provided for in this article shall be considered committed in a significant amount if the total value of the objects of smuggling exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five thousand times or more, in a large amount - if the total value of the objects of smuggling exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by ten thousand times or more.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 201-3 in accordance with Law No. 3513-IX of 09.12.2023}
1. Smuggling, that is, movement across the customs border of Ukraine outside customs control or with concealment from customs control of excisable goods (except electric energy), committed in a significant amount, -
shall be punished by a fine of twenty thousand to forty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of three to six years.
2. The same act committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or by a person previously convicted of smuggling, or by an official using their official position, or in a large amount, -
shall be punished by a fine of seventy-five thousand to one hundred and twenty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of six to eleven years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
Note. Acts provided for in this article shall be considered committed in a significant amount if the total value of the objects of smuggling (except electric energy) exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by seven hundred and fifty times or more, in a large amount - if the total value of the objects of smuggling (except electric energy) exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand five hundred times or more.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 201-4 in accordance with Law No. 3513-IX of 09.12.2023}

Стаття 202. {Article 202 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}

Стаття 203. {Article 203 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}

Стаття 203-1

Illegal circulation of discs for laser reading systems, matrices, equipment and raw materials for their production
1. Illegal production, export, import, storage, sale and movement of discs for laser reading systems, matrices, equipment and raw materials for their production, if these acts are committed in significant amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine of three thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The same acts, if they are committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or committed in large amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine of five thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
Note. A significant amount shall be understood as the value of discs for laser reading systems, matrices, equipment or raw materials for their production that exceeds the level of the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by twenty times or more; a large amount shall be understood as the value of discs for laser reading systems, matrices, equipment or raw materials for their production that exceeds the level of the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one hundred times or more.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 203-1 in accordance with Law No. 2953-III of 17.01.2002; as amended by Law No. 2734-IV of 06.07.2005; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016}

Стаття 203-2. Illegal activity in the organization or conduct of gambling, lotteries

1. Organization or conduct of gambling without a license for carrying out the relevant type of activity in the organization and conduct of gambling, issued in accordance with the law, or the issue or conduct of lotteries by a person who does not have the status of lottery operator, or the organization or operation of establishments for the purpose of providing access to gambling or lotteries conducted on the Internet, -
shall be punished by a fine from ten thousand to forty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of one year.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed by a person previously convicted of illegal activity in the organization or conduct of gambling or lotteries, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by a fine from forty thousand to fifty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 203-2 in accordance with Law No. 2852-VI of 22.12.2010; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016; as worded by Law No. 768-IX of 14.07.2020}

Стаття 204

Illegal manufacture, storage, sale or transportation for the purpose of sale of excisable goods
1. Illegal acquisition for the purpose of sale or storage for this purpose, as well as sale or transportation for the purpose of sale of illegally manufactured ethyl alcohol, alcohol distillates, alcoholic beverages, beer, tobacco products, tobacco, industrial tobacco substitutes, fuel or other excisable goods -
shall be punished by a fine from five thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with confiscation and destruction of illegally manufactured goods.
2. Illegal manufacture of ethyl alcohol, alcohol distillates, alcoholic beverages, beer, tobacco products, tobacco, industrial tobacco substitutes, fuel or other excisable goods, or committed by a person who was previously convicted under this article, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with confiscation and destruction of illegally manufactured or acquired goods, means of production, raw materials for their manufacture.
3. Illegal manufacture of goods specified in parts one or two of this article that pose a threat to human life and health, as well as sale of such goods that resulted in poisoning or caused the death of a person, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to ten years with confiscation of property, with seizure and destruction of illegally manufactured or acquired products, equipment for their manufacture.
{Article 204 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016. Amendments to Article 204 additionally see in Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018. As worded by Law No. 2628-VIII of 23.11.2018}

Стаття 205. {Article 205 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 101-IX of 18.09.2019}

Стаття 205-1

Falsification of documents submitted for state registration of legal entities and natural persons - entrepreneurs
1. Entry into documents that are submitted in accordance with the law for state registration of a legal entity or natural person - entrepreneur, of knowingly false information, as well as intentional submission for such registration of documents containing knowingly false information, -
shall be punished by a fine from five thousand to eight thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official using their official position, -
