shall be punished by a fine from eight thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of three to five years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 205-1 in accordance with Law No. 642-VII of 10.10.2013; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 835-VIII of 26.11.2015, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019}

Стаття 206. Counteraction to lawful economic activity

1. Counteraction to lawful economic activity, that is, unlawful demand to cease engaging in economic activity or to limit it, to conclude an agreement or not to perform a concluded agreement, the performance (non-performance) of which may cause material damage or limit the lawful rights or interests of the person engaged in economic activity, combined with a threat of violence against the victim or persons close to them, damage or destruction of their property or seizure of an integral property complex, its part, buildings, structures, land plot, construction objects, other objects and illegal termination or restriction of activities at these objects and restriction of access to them in the absence of signs of extortion, -
shall be punished by a fine from ten thousand to fifteen thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or with a threat of murder or infliction of grievous bodily harm, or combined with violence that is not dangerous to life and health, or with damage or destruction of property, -
shall be punished by a fine from fifteen thousand to twenty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years, or imprisonment for a term of three to six years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
3. Counteraction to lawful economic activity committed by an organized group, or by an official using their official position, or combined with violence dangerous to life or health, or that caused great damage or resulted in other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of six to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such and with confiscation of property.
Note. Material damage is considered great if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five hundred or more times.
{Article 206 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1666-VIII of 06.10.2016; as worded by Law No. 402-IX of 19.12.2019; as amended in accordance with Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Стаття 206-2. Unlawful Seizure of Property of an Enterprise, Institution, Organization

1. Unlawful seizure of property of an enterprise, institution, organization, including shares, stocks, contributions of their founders, participants, shareholders, members, by means of committing transactions using forged or stolen documents, seals, stamps of the enterprise, institution, organization, -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term up to two years or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to two years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or with threat of murder or infliction of grievous bodily harm, or combined with violence that is not dangerous to life and health, or with damage or destruction of property, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to two years.
3. The same acts committed by an official using his or her official position or if they caused major damage or resulted in other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years and with confiscation of property.
Note. Material damage shall be considered major if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens five hundred times or more.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 206-2 in accordance with Law No. 642-VII of 10.10.2013}

Стаття 207. {Article 207 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}

Стаття 208. {Article 208 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}

Стаття 209. Legalization (Laundering) of Property Obtained by Criminal Means

1. Acquisition, possession, use, disposal of property in respect of which factual circumstances indicate its obtaining by criminal means, including carrying out a financial operation, committing a transaction with such property, or movement, change of form (transformation) of such property, or commission of acts aimed at concealing, disguising the origin of such property or possession thereof, rights to such property, sources of its origin, location, if these acts are committed by a person who knew or should have known that such property was directly or indirectly, wholly or partially obtained by criminal means, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to six years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to two years and with confiscation of property.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or on a large scale, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years and with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this Article, committed by an organized group or on an especially large scale, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years and with confiscation of property.
1. Legalization (laundering) of property obtained by criminal means shall be deemed committed on a large scale if the subject of the crime was property in an amount exceeding six thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. Legalization (laundering) of property obtained by criminal means shall be deemed committed on an especially large scale if the subject of the crime was property in an amount exceeding eighteen thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
{Article 209 as amended by Law No. 430-IV of 16.01.2003; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2258-VI of 18.05.2010, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1702-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015; as amended by Law No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019}

Стаття 209-1

Intentional Violation of Requirements of Legislation on Prevention and Counteraction to Legalization (Laundering) of Proceeds from Crime, Financing of Terrorism and Financing of Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction
1. Intentional failure to submit, untimely submission or submission of unreliable information about financial operations that are subject to financial monitoring in accordance with the law, to the central executive body implementing state policy in the sphere of prevention and counteraction to legalization (laundering) of proceeds from crime, financing of terrorism and financing of proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, if such acts caused substantial harm to legally protected rights, freedoms or interests of individual citizens, state or public interests or interests of individual legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. Disclosure in any form of the secret of financial monitoring or the fact of exchange of information about a financial operation and its participants between subjects of primary financial monitoring, subjects of state financial monitoring, other state bodies, as well as the fact of providing (receiving) a request, decision or instruction of the central executive body implementing state policy in the sphere of prevention and counteraction to legalization (laundering) of proceeds from crime, financing of terrorism and financing of proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, or providing (receiving) a response to such request, decision or instruction by a person to whom this information became known in connection with his or her professional or official activity, if such acts caused substantial harm to legally protected rights, freedoms or interests of individual citizens, state or public interests or interests of individual legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 209-1 in accordance with Law No. 430-IV of 16.01.2003 - enters into force on 11.06.2003; as amended by Law No. 2258-VI of 18.05.2010; as amended in accordance with Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011. Amendments to Article 209-1 additionally see in Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018. As amended in accordance with Law No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019}

Стаття 210

Misuse of budget funds, execution of budget expenditures or provision of loans from the budget without established budget appropriations or in excess thereof
1. Misuse of budget funds by an official, as well as execution of budget expenditures or provision of loans from the budget without established budget appropriations or in excess thereof contrary to the Budget Code of Ukraine or the law on the State Budget of Ukraine for the relevant year, if the subject of such actions were budget funds in large amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts, the subject of which were budget funds in especially large amounts or committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term from two to five years or imprisonment for a term from two to six years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
Примітка. 1. Budget funds include funds that are included in the state budget and local budgets regardless of the source of their formation.
2. A large amount of budget funds in accordance with Articles 210, 211 of this Code shall mean an amount that exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
3. An especially large amount of budget funds in accordance with Articles 210, 211 of this Code shall mean an amount that exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by three thousand or more times.
{Article 210 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2457-VI of 08.07.2010, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 211

Issuance of regulatory legal acts that reduce budget revenues or increase budget expenditures contrary to law
1. Issuance by an official of regulatory legal acts that reduce budget revenues or increase budget expenditures contrary to law, if the subject of such actions were budget funds in large amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term up to five years, or imprisonment for a term up to four years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, the subject of which were budget funds in especially large amounts or committed repeatedly, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to six years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 211 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2457-VI of 08.07.2010, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 212. Evasion of payment of taxes, levies (mandatory payments)

1. Intentional evasion of payment of taxes, levies (mandatory payments) that are part of the taxation system, introduced in the manner established by law, committed by an official of an enterprise, institution, organization, regardless of the form of ownership, or by a person engaged in entrepreneurial activity without establishing a legal entity, or by any other person obliged to pay them, if these acts resulted in actual non-receipt of funds in significant amounts to budgets or state trust funds, -
shall be punished by a fine from five thousand to ten thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if they resulted in actual non-receipt of funds in large amounts to budgets or state trust funds, -
shall be punished by a fine from ten thousand to fifteen thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by a person previously convicted of evasion of payment of taxes, levies (mandatory payments), or if they resulted in actual non-receipt of funds in especially large amounts to budgets or state trust funds, -
shall be punished by a fine from fifteen thousand to twenty-five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years with confiscation of property.
4. A person who has committed acts provided for in parts one, two of this Article, or acts provided for in part three (if they resulted in actual non-receipt of funds in especially large amounts to budgets or state trust funds) of this Article, shall be released from criminal liability if, before being brought to criminal liability, taxes, levies (mandatory payments) have been paid, as well as damage caused to the state by their untimely payment (financial sanctions, penalty) has been compensated.
5. Acts provided for in parts one through three of this Article shall not be considered intentional evasion of payment of taxes, levies (mandatory payments) if the taxpayer has achieved a tax compromise in accordance with subsection 9-2 of Section XX "Transitional Provisions" of the Tax Code of Ukraine.
6. Acts provided for in parts one through three of this Article shall not be considered intentional evasion of payment of taxes, levies (mandatory payments) that are part of the taxation system, if such acts are related to the acquisition (formation of sources of acquisition), creation, receipt, use of objects in respect of which a person has submitted a one-time (special) voluntary declaration and paid the agreed amount of levy in accordance with subsection 9-4 "Peculiarities of application of one-time (special) voluntary declaration of assets of individuals" of Section XX of the Tax Code of Ukraine within the composition and value of declaration objects specified in the one-time (special) voluntary declaration as the base for calculation of the levy on one-time (special) voluntary declaration, if such acts were committed before January 1, 2021 and are related to the acquisition (formation of sources of acquisition), creation, receipt, use of objects of one-time (special) voluntary declaration or disposal thereof.
Примітка. A significant amount of funds shall mean amounts of taxes, levies and other mandatory payments that exceed the tax-free minimum income of citizens established by law by three thousand or more times, a large amount of funds shall mean amounts of taxes, levies and other mandatory payments that exceed the tax-free minimum income of citizens established by law by five thousand or more times, an especially large amount of funds shall mean amounts of taxes, levies, other mandatory payments that exceed the tax-free minimum income of citizens established by law by seven thousand or more times.
{Article 212 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2756-VI of 02.12.2010, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 63-VIII of 25.12.2014. Amendments to Article 212 see in Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018. As amended in accordance with Laws No. 101-IX of 18.09.2019, No. 1542-IX of 15.06.2021, No. 1888-IX of 17.11.2021}

This excerpt sits under: (Відомості Верховної Ради України (ВВР), 2001, № 25-26, ст.131) > Розділ VII

Стаття 212-1

Evasion of payment of the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance and insurance contributions for mandatory state pension insurance
1. Intentional evasion of payment of the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance or insurance contributions for mandatory state pension insurance, committed by an official of an enterprise, institution, organization regardless of the form of ownership or by a person engaged in entrepreneurial activity without establishing a legal entity, or by any other person obliged to pay it, if such act resulted in the actual non-receipt by the funds of mandatory state social insurance of funds in significant amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or if they resulted in the actual non-receipt by the funds of mandatory state social insurance of funds in large amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this Article, committed by a person previously convicted of evasion of payment of the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance or insurance contributions for mandatory state pension insurance, or if they resulted in the actual non-receipt by the funds of mandatory state social insurance of funds in especially large amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine from fifteen thousand to twenty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years with confiscation of property.
4. A person who has committed acts provided for by parts one, two or three of this Article shall be released from criminal liability if, before being brought to criminal liability, the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance or insurance contributions for mandatory state pension insurance has been paid, and the damage caused to the funds of mandatory state social insurance by their untimely payment (penalty sanctions, penalty) has been compensated.
5. Acts provided for by parts one through three of this Article shall not be considered intentional evasion of payment of the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance or insurance contributions for mandatory state pension insurance if such acts are related to the acquisition (formation of sources of acquisition), creation, receipt, use of objects in respect of which a person has submitted a one-time (special) voluntary declaration and paid the agreed amount of tax liabilities in accordance with subsection 9-4 "Peculiarities of application of one-time (special) voluntary declaration of assets of natural persons" of Section XX of the Tax Code of Ukraine within the composition and value of the objects of declaration specified in the one-time (special) voluntary declaration as the basis for calculating the levy on one-time (special) voluntary declaration, if such acts were committed before January 1, 2021 and are related to the acquisition (formation of sources of acquisition), creation, receipt, use of objects of one-time (special) voluntary declaration or disposal thereof.
Примітка. A significant amount of funds shall mean amounts of the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance or insurance contributions for mandatory state pension insurance that exceed the non-taxable minimum income of citizens established by law by one thousand times or more, a large amount of funds shall mean amounts of the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance that exceed the non-taxable minimum income of citizens established by law by three thousand times or more, an especially large amount of funds shall mean amounts of the single contribution for mandatory state social insurance or insurance contributions for mandatory state pension insurance that exceed the non-taxable minimum income of citizens established by law by five thousand times or more.
{Кодекс доповнено статтею 212-1 згідно із Законом № 3108-IV від 17.11.2005; в редакції Закону № 2464-VI від 08.07.2010; із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011, № 77-VIII від 28.12.2014, № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018, № 1542-IX від 15.06.2021, № 1888-IX від 17.11.2021}

Стаття 213

Violation of the procedure for carrying out procurement of scrap metal and operations with scrap metal
1. Carrying out procurement of scrap metal, processing of scrap of non-ferrous and ferrous metals by natural persons without compliance with the requirements of legislation regarding state registration as a business entity, organization of illegal points of acceptance, storage and sale of scrap metal -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand five hundred to two thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. Acts provided for by part one of this Article, if they were committed by a person previously convicted of a criminal offense provided for by this Article, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
{Стаття 213 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011; в редакції Закону № 191-VIII від 12.02.2015; із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018, № 776-IX від 14.07.2020, № 3233-IX від 13.07.2023}
{Зміни до абзацу першого частини першої статті 213 див. в Законі № 222-VIII від 02.03.2015}

Стаття 214. {Статтю 214 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 215. {Статтю 215 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 216. {Статтю 216 виключено на підставі Закону № 1965-IX від 15.12.2021}

Стаття 217. {Статтю 217 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 218. {Статтю 218 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 218-1. Bringing a bank to insolvency

1. Bringing a bank to insolvency, that is, intentional, with mercenary motives, other personal interest or in the interests of third parties, commission by a person related to the bank of any actions that resulted in the bank being classified as insolvent, if this caused large material damage to the state or a creditor, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term from one to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with imposition of a fine from five thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens and deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Примітка. 1. In this Article, material damage shall be considered large if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by ten thousand times or more.
2. The term "person related to the bank" is used in the meaning defined by the Law of Ukraine "On Banks and Banking Activity".
{Кодекс доповнено статтею 218-1 згідно із Законом № 218-VIII від 02.03.2015}

Стаття 219. Bringing to Bankruptcy

Bringing to bankruptcy, that is, intentional commission, from mercenary motives, other personal interest or in the interests of third parties, by a citizen - founder (participant) or an official of a business entity of actions that led to persistent financial insolvency of the business entity, if this caused major material damage to the state or a creditor, -
is punished by a fine from two thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Note. In Articles 219, 222 of this Code, material damage is considered major if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five hundred or more times.
{Article 219 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 220. {Article 220 excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}

Стаття 220-1

Violation of the Procedure for Maintaining the Database on Depositors or the Procedure for Forming Reports
1. Entry by the head or other official of a bank into the database on depositors of knowingly false information -
is punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The same act committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
is punished by a fine from four thousand to six thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Entry by the head or other official of a bank into the reports submitted to the Deposit Guarantee Fund for Individuals of knowingly false information -
is punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
4. Intentional damage or destruction by the head or other official of a bank of the database on depositors or commission of actions that make it impossible to identify the depositor based on information available in the database on depositors, or commission of actions resulting in illegal increase of the amount of expenses of the Deposit Guarantee Fund for Individuals related to the withdrawal of the bank from the market, or make it impossible to commence payments to depositors of an insolvent bank in accordance with the Law of Ukraine "On the System of Guaranteeing Deposits of Individuals", -
are punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{The Code supplemented with Article 220-1 according to Law No. 629-VIII of 16.07.2015; as amended according to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Стаття 220-2

Falsification of Financial Documents and Reports of a Financial Organization, Concealment of Insolvency of a Financial Institution or Grounds for Revocation (Annulment) of a License of a Financial Institution
1. Making changes to documents or accounting registers or entry into the reports of a financial institution of knowingly incomplete or inaccurate information about transactions, obligations, property of the institution, including property under trust management, or about the financial condition of the institution, or confirmation of such information, provision of such information to the National Bank of Ukraine, publication or disclosure of such information in the manner determined by the legislation of Ukraine, if these actions were committed with the purpose of concealing signs of bankruptcy or persistent financial insolvency or grounds for mandatory revocation (annulment) of a license from a financial institution or recognition of it as insolvent, -
are punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to four years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{The Code supplemented with Article 220-2 according to Law No. 629-VIII of 16.07.2015; as amended according to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Стаття 221. {Article 221 excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}

Стаття 222. Fraud with Financial Resources

1. Provision of knowingly false information to state authorities, authorities of the Autonomous Republic of Crimea or local self-government bodies, banks or other creditors with the purpose of obtaining subsidies, subventions, grants, credits or tax benefits in the absence of signs of a criminal offense against property -
is punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they were committed repeatedly or caused major material damage, -
are punished by a fine from three thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 222 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 3795-VI of 22.09.2011, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 222-1. Manipulation on Organized Markets

1. Intentional actions of a natural person that have signs of manipulation on organized markets, defined by law, that led to obtaining by such person or third parties profit in significant amounts, or avoidance by such persons of losses in significant amounts, or if this caused significant damage to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens or state or public interests, or interests of legal entities, -
are punished by a fine from three thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if they caused grave consequences, -
are punished by a fine from five thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Note. 1. Significant amount in this Article is considered an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five hundred or more times.
2. Significant damage in this Article is considered damage that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five hundred or more times.
3. Grave consequences in this Article are considered damage that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
{The Code supplemented with Article 222-1 according to Law No. 3267-VI of 21.04.2011; as amended according to Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 738-IX of 19.06.2020}

Стаття 222-2. Market manipulation in the energy market

1. Intentional actions of persons in the energy market (electricity market and natural gas market) that have the characteristics of manipulation in wholesale energy markets, as defined by legislation in the field of energy, that resulted in such person or third parties obtaining profit in significant amounts, or such persons avoiding losses in significant amounts, or if this caused significant damage to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens or state or public interests, or interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from twenty thousand to sixty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine from sixty thousand to eighty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Примітка. 1. A significant amount in Articles 222-2 and 232-2 is considered to be an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by thirty thousand or more times.
2. Significant damage in Articles 222-2 and 232-2 is considered to be damage that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by thirty thousand or more times.
3. Grave consequences in Articles 222-2 and 232-2 are considered to be damage that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one hundred eighty thousand or more times.
4. The subject of the crime specified in Articles 222-2 and 232-2 is a natural person who committed the actions provided for by these articles, or in the case of such actions being committed by a legal entity - a natural person who independently or as a member of a collegial body of the legal entity is authorized to perform functions of representing the legal entity and/or making decisions on behalf of the legal entity and/or exercising control over the activities of the legal entity.
{Кодекс доповнено статтею 222-2 згідно із Законом № 738-IX від 19.06.2020}

Стаття 223. {Статтю 223 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 223-1

Falsification of documents submitted for registration of securities issue
1. Inclusion by an authorized person in documents submitted for registration of securities issue of knowingly false information, if this caused significant material damage to an investor in financial instruments who acquired ownership rights to securities, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Примітка. Damage provided for by this article is considered significant if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by twenty or more times.
{Кодекс доповнено статтею 223-1 згідно із Законом № 801-VI від 25.12.2008; із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011, № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018, № 738-IX від 19.06.2020}

Стаття 223-2. {Статтю 223-2 виключено на підставі Закону № 738-IX від 19.06.2020}

Стаття 224

Production, sale and use of counterfeit securities (except state securities)
1. Production for the purpose of sale, sale or other use of counterfeit securities (except state securities) that exist in paper form -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. Actions provided for by part one of this article, if they were committed repeatedly or if they caused large material damage, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they were committed by an organized group or if they caused especially large material damage, -
shall be punished by a fine from ten thousand to fifteen thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Примітка. 1. Repeated in Article 224 is recognized as a crime committed by a person who previously committed a crime provided for by this article or Article 199 of this Code.
2. In accordance with this article, material damage is considered large if it exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by three hundred or more times, and especially large - such that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
{Стаття 224 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011, № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018, № 738-IX від 19.06.2020}

Стаття 225. {Статтю 225 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 226. {Статтю 226 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 227

Intentional placing on the market of Ukraine (release on the market of Ukraine) of dangerous products
Intentional placing on the market (release on the market of Ukraine) of dangerous products, that is, such products that do not meet the requirements for product safety established by regulatory legal acts, if such actions were committed on a large scale, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to eight thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Примітка. Placing on the market (release on the market of Ukraine) of dangerous products committed on a large scale shall be understood as placing on the market of products whose total value exceeds five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
{Стаття 227 в редакції Закону № 2735-VI від 02.12.2010; із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011, № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018}

Стаття 228. {Статтю 228 виключено на підставі Закону № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011}

Стаття 229

Illegal use of a mark for goods and services, trade name, qualified indication of origin of goods
1. Illegal use of a mark for goods and services, trade name, qualified indication of origin of goods, or other intentional violation of the right to these objects, if this caused material damage in a significant amount, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The same actions, if they were committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or caused material damage in a large amount, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed by an official using their official position or by an organized group, or if they caused material damage in an especially large amount, -
shall be punished by a fine from ten thousand to fifteen thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
Примітка. Material damage is considered caused in a significant amount if its amount exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by twenty or more times, in a large amount - if its amount exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by two hundred or more times, and caused in an especially large amount - if its amount exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
{Стаття 229 в редакції Закону № 850-IV від 22.05.2003; із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 3423-IV від 09.02.2006, № 1111-V від 31.05.2007, № 4025-VI від 15.11.2011, № 1019-VIII від 18.02.2016, № 2617-VIII від 22.11.2018}

Article 230. {Article 230 excluded on the basis of Law No. 669-IV of 03.04.2003}

Article 231

Illegal collection for the purpose of use or use of information constituting commercial or banking secrets
Intentional actions aimed at obtaining information constituting commercial or banking secrets, for the purpose of disclosure or other use of such information, as well as illegal use of such information, if this caused substantial harm to a business entity, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to eight thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
Note. Public, including through mass media, journalists, public associations, trade unions, reporting by a person of information about the commission of a criminal or other offense, carried out in compliance with the requirements of the law, is not an action provided for by this article and does not entail criminal liability.
{Article 231 as amended according to Laws No. 2252-IV of 16.12.2004, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 198-IX of 17.10.2019}

Article 232

Disclosure of commercial, banking secrets or professional secrets in capital markets and organized commodity markets
Intentional disclosure of commercial, banking secrets or professional secrets in capital markets and organized commodity markets without the consent of its owner by a person to whom this secret is known in connection with professional or official activity, if it is committed from mercenary or other personal motives and caused substantial harm to a business entity, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 232 as amended according to Laws No. 2252-IV of 16.12.2004, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 738-IX of 19.06.2020}

Article 232-1. Illegal use of insider information

1. Violation of the prohibition on the use of insider information, if this led to obtaining by the person who committed the specified actions, or by third parties, unjustified profit in a significant amount, or avoidance by the person who committed the specified actions, or by third parties, of significant losses, or if this caused significant harm to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens or state or public interests, or interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. Commission using insider information for one's own benefit or for the benefit of other persons of transactions aimed at acquisition or alienation (replacement of parties) of financial instruments to which the insider information relates, if this led to obtaining by the person who committed the specified actions, or by third parties, unjustified profit in a significant amount, or avoidance by a capital market participant or third parties of significant losses, or if this caused significant harm to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens or state or public interests, or interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if such actions caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine from five thousand to eight thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
4. Actions provided for by parts one - three of this article, if they are committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by a fine from eight thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Note: 1. A significant amount (significant loss, significant harm) in this article is considered an amount (loss, harm) that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five hundred or more times.
2. Grave consequences in this article, if they consist in causing material losses, are considered such that exceed the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
3. Persons who committed actions provided for by this article are understood to be: officials of the issuer, including those who were officials of the issuer at the time of familiarization with insider information; persons who have access to insider information in connection with their performance of labor (official) duties or contractual obligations regardless of relations with the issuer, including employees of professional participants in capital markets; civil servants to whom insider information is known as a result of their performance of official (service) duties; persons who became familiar with insider information by unlawful means; auditors, notaries, experts, appraisers, arbitration managers or other persons who perform public powers granted by law.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 232-1 according to Law No. 3480-IV of 23.02.2006; as amended by Law No. 801-VI of 25.12.2008; as amended according to Laws No. 3306-VI of 22.04.2011, No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 738-IX of 19.06.2020}

Article 232-2. Concealment of information about the activities of an issuer

1. Failure by an official of an issuer to provide an investor in securities (including a shareholder) upon their written request with information about the activities of the issuer within the limits provided by law, or providing them with unreliable information, if this caused the investor in securities (including the shareholder) material harm in a significant amount, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to six thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Note. In this article, material harm is considered caused in a significant amount if its amount exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five hundred or more times.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 232-2 according to Law No. 801-VI of 25.12.2008; as amended according to Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 232-3

Illegal use of insider information regarding wholesale energy products
1. Intentional illegal disclosure, transmission or provision of access to insider information, as well as provision of recommendations to other persons regarding the purchase or sale of wholesale energy products, the list of which is determined by current legislation in the field of energy, based on insider information possessed by the given person, if this resulted in obtaining by the person who committed the specified actions, or by third parties, of unjustified profit in a significant amount, or avoidance by a participant of the wholesale energy market or by third parties of significant losses, or if this caused significant harm to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens or to state or public interests, or to interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from forty thousand to fifty thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. Commission for one's own benefit or for the benefit of other persons, directly or indirectly, of transactions using insider information, aimed at the purchase or sale of wholesale energy products, the list of which is determined by current legislation in the field of energy, if this resulted in obtaining by the person who committed the specified actions, or by third parties, of unjustified profit in a significant amount, or avoidance by a participant of the wholesale energy market or by third parties of significant losses, or if this caused significant harm to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens or to state or public interests, or to interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from fifty-five thousand to sixty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
3. Actions provided for by part one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if such actions caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine from sixty-five thousand to seventy-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
4. Actions provided for by parts one through three of this article, if they were committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by a fine from sixty thousand to seventy thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 232-3 in accordance with Law No. 738-IX of 19.06.2020}

Article 233. Illegal privatization of state, communal property

1. Privatization of state, communal property by undervaluing its worth through determining it in a manner not provided for by law, or using forged privatization documents, as well as privatization itself of property that is not subject to privatization according to law, or privatization by an unauthorized person -
shall be punished by a fine from five thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The act provided for by part one of this article, if it resulted in illegal privatization of property of state or communal ownership in large amounts or was committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by a fine from twenty-five thousand to thirty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens with confiscation of property or without such.
Note. Large amount, as provided for in this article, means illegal privatization of property in an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand times or more.
{Article 233 as amended in accordance with Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}
{Article 234 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}
{Article 235 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011}
{Section VIIА became invalid on the basis of Law No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010}

Section VIII

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT
{Title of Section VIII as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 236. Violation of environmental safety rules

Violation of the procedure for carrying out environmental impact assessment, environmental safety rules during the design, placement, construction, reconstruction, commissioning, operation and liquidation of enterprises, structures, mobile facilities and other objects, if this caused the death of people, environmental pollution of significant territories or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 236 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2059-VIII of 23.05.2017}

Article 237. Failure to take measures to eliminate the consequences of environmental pollution

Evasion of conducting or improper conducting in a territory that has suffered pollution by hazardous substances or radiation, of decontamination or other restorative measures to eliminate or remove the consequences of environmental pollution by a person on whom such duty is imposed, if this caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.

Article 238

Concealment or distortion of information about environmental conditions or morbidity of the population
1. Concealment or intentional distortion by an official of information about environmental, including radiation, conditions, which is related to pollution of lands, water resources, atmospheric air, food products and food raw materials and such that negatively affects human health, plant and animal life, as well as about the morbidity of the population in areas with increased environmental danger -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts, committed repeatedly or in a locality declared a zone of extraordinary environmental situation, or such that caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Article 238 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 239. Pollution or spoilage of lands

1. Pollution or spoilage of lands by substances, waste or other materials harmful to life, health of people or the environment, as a result of violation of special rules, if this created danger to life, health of people or the environment, -
shall be punished by a fine from one to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts that caused the death of people, their mass disease or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Article 239 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 239-1. Illegal seizure of soil cover (surface layer) of lands

1. Illegal seizure of soil cover (surface layer) of lands, if it created danger to human life, health or to the environment, -
is punishable by a fine from one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The same act, if it was committed repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or caused material damage on a large scale, -
is punishable by restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they were committed by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method or caused death of people, mass destruction of objects of fauna or flora or other grave consequences, -
are punishable by restriction of liberty for a term from three to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
Note. In this article, material damage is considered to be caused on a large scale if its amount exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one hundred or more times.
{The Law was supplemented with Article 239-1 in accordance with Law No. 1708-VI of 05.11.2009; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 239-2. Illegal seizure of water fund lands in especially large amounts

1. Illegal seizure of the surface (soil) layer of water fund lands in especially large amounts -
is punishable by a fine from one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The same act, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
is punishable by restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
are punishable by restriction of liberty for a term from three to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
Note. Especially large amount in this article shall mean the volume of the surface (soil) layer of lands that exceeds ten cubic meters.
{The Law was supplemented with Article 239-2 in accordance with Law No. 1708-VI of 05.11.2009; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 240

Violation of rules for protection or use of subsoil, illegal extraction of minerals
1. Violation of established rules for protection of subsoil, if it created danger to human life, health or the environment, as well as illegal extraction of minerals of local significance in a significant amount -
are punishable by a fine from five hundred to eight hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. Violation of established rules for use of subsoil, if it created danger to human life, health or the environment, as well as illegal extraction of minerals of local significance on a large scale or illegal extraction of minerals of national significance -
are punishable by a fine from three thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed on territories or objects of the nature reserve fund or committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or committed by a person previously convicted of a crime provided for by this article, or by an official using their official position, -
are punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to six years.
4. Acts provided for by parts one, two or three of this article, if they were committed by an organized group or by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, or caused death of people, their mass disease or other grave consequences, -
are punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to eight years.
Note. Illegal extraction of minerals of local significance is considered to be committed in a significant amount if their value exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by thirty or more times. Illegal extraction of minerals of local significance is considered to be committed on a large scale if their value exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one hundred or more times.
{Article 240 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2308-IV of 11.01.2005, No. 2984-IV of 18.10.2005; as worded by Law No. 1708-VI of 05.11.2009; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018; as worded by Law No. 1685-IX of 15.07.2021}

Стаття 240-1

Illegal extraction, sale, acquisition, transfer, sending, transportation, processing of amber
1. Illegal extraction of amber, as well as sale, acquisition, storage, transfer, sending, transportation, processing of amber, the legality of origin of which is not confirmed by relevant documents, -
are punishable by a fine from three thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term from two to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. The same acts, if they were committed repeatedly or in significant amounts, or on territories or objects of the nature reserve fund, -
are punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from four to seven years with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for by part one of this article, committed by an official by using their official position, -
are punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to eight years with confiscation of property.
Note. Significant amount in this article shall mean the value of amber that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one hundred or more times.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 240-1 in accordance with Law No. 402-IX of 19.12.2019}

Стаття 241. Pollution of atmospheric air

1. Pollution or other change of natural properties of atmospheric air by substances, waste or other materials of industrial or other production harmful to human life, health or to the environment as a result of violation of special rules, if it created danger to human life, health or to the environment, -
are punishable by a fine from one thousand eight hundred to three thousand six hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for the same term or without such.
2. The same acts, if they caused death of people or other grave consequences, -
are punishable by restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
{Amendments to Article 241 see in Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018; Article 241 as amended in accordance with Law No. 556-IX of 13.04.2020}

Article 242. Violation of the rules for the protection of waters

1. Violation of the rules for the protection of waters (water bodies), if it caused pollution of surface or underground waters and aquifers, sources of drinking, medicinal waters or a change in their natural properties, or depletion of water sources and created a danger to the life, health of people or to the environment, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years, or restriction of liberty for the same term.
2. The same acts, if they caused the death or illness of people, mass death of objects of the animal and plant world or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
{Article 242 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
1. Pollution of the sea within the internal marine or territorial waters of Ukraine or within the waters of the exclusive (marine) economic zone of Ukraine with materials or substances harmful to the life or health of people, or with waste as a result of violation of special rules, if it created a danger to the life or health of people or living resources of the sea or could hinder lawful uses of the sea, as well as illegal dumping or burial within the internal marine or territorial waters of Ukraine or in the open sea of the specified materials, substances and waste -
shall be punished by a fine from three hundred to eight hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts, if they caused the death or illness of people, mass death of objects of the animal and plant world or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
3. Failure by specially responsible persons of marine and air vessels or other means and structures located at sea to notify the administration of the nearest port of Ukraine, another authorized body or person, and in case of dumping for the purpose of burial - also the organization that issues permits for dumping, of information about the prepared or carried out as a result of extreme necessity dumping or inevitable losses by them within the internal marine and territorial waters of Ukraine or in the open sea of harmful substances or mixtures containing such substances above established norms, other waste, if it created a danger to the life, health of people or living resources of the sea or could cause harm to treatment and recreation zones or hinder other lawful uses of the sea, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
{Article 243 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 244. Violation of legislation on the continental shelf of Ukraine

1. Violation of legislation on the continental shelf of Ukraine that caused substantial harm, as well as failure by a person responsible for the operation of technological installations or other sources of danger in the safety zone to take measures to protect living organisms of the sea from the effects of harmful waste or dangerous radiation and energy, if it created a danger of their death or threatened the life or health of people, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. Research, exploration, development of natural resources and other works on the continental shelf of Ukraine carried out by foreigners, if it is not provided for by a treaty between Ukraine and the interested foreign state, consent to the binding nature of which was given by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine or by a special permit issued in the manner established by law, -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months.
{Article 244 as amended according to Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 245. Destruction or damage to objects of the plant world

1. Destruction or damage to forest areas, green plantations around settlements, along railways, as well as stubble, dry wild-growing grasses, vegetation or its remains on agricultural lands by fire or other generally dangerous method -
shall be punished by a fine from five thousand four hundred to nine thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. The same acts, if they caused the death of people, mass death of animals or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to ten years.
{Article 245 as amended by Law No. 1708-VI of 05.11.2009; as amended according to Law No. 556-IX of 13.04.2020}

Article 246. Illegal felling or illegal transportation, storage, sale of forest

1. Illegal felling of trees or shrubs in forests, protective and other forest plantations, transportation, storage, sale of illegally felled trees or shrubs that caused substantial harm, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to one thousand five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term from three to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed in reserves or on territories or objects of the nature reserve fund, or in other specially protected forests, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand five hundred to two thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term from three to five years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
4. Acts provided for in parts one, two or three of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to seven years.
Note. 1. In this article, substantial harm is considered to be such harm that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by twenty or more times, or other substantial harm caused to the natural environment in terms of ensuring effective protection, proper defense, rational use and reproduction of forests.
2. In this article, grave consequences are considered to be such consequences that exceed the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by sixty or more times.
{Article 246 as amended according to Law No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016; as amended by Laws No. 2063-VIII of 23.05.2017, No. 2531-VIII of 06.09.2018; as amended according to Law No. 2708-VIII of 25.04.2019}

Article 247. Violation of legislation on plant protection

Violation of the rules established for combating pests and plant diseases, and other requirements of legislation on plant protection, which caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term from one hundred twenty to two hundred hours, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
{Article 247 as amended according to Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Article 248. Illegal Hunting

1. Violation of hunting rules, if it caused substantial harm, as well as illegal hunting in reserves or on other territories and objects of the nature reserve fund, or hunting of animals, birds or other species of fauna listed in the Red Book of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred sixty to two hundred forty hours, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they were committed by an official using their official position, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by a method of mass destruction of animals, birds or other species of fauna, or with the use of vehicles, or by a person previously convicted of a criminal offense provided for by this article, -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to four hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
Note. Substantial harm in this article, if it consists in causing material damage, is considered to be such harm that exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by two hundred fifty or more times.
{Article 248 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1827-VI of 21.01.2010, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 249. Illegal Engagement in Fish, Animal or Other Aquatic Extractive Industry

1. Illegal engagement in fish, animal or other aquatic extractive industry, if it caused substantial harm, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they were committed with the use of explosive, poisonous substances, electric current or other method of mass destruction of fish, animals or other species of fauna or by a person previously convicted of a criminal offense provided for by this article, -
shall be punished by a fine of three thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 249 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1827-VI of 21.01.2010, No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 250. Conducting Explosive Works in Violation of Rules for Protection of Fish Stocks

Conducting explosive works in violation of rules for protection of fish stocks or wild aquatic animals -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
{Article 250 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 251. Violation of Veterinary Rules

Violation of veterinary rules that caused the spread of epizootic or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 251 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 252

Intentional Destruction or Damage of Territories Taken Under State Protection and Objects of the Nature Reserve Fund
1. Intentional destruction or damage of territories taken under state protection and objects of the nature reserve fund -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed by arson or other generally dangerous method, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
{Article 252 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 253. Design or Operation of Structures Without Environmental Protection Systems

1. Development and submission of projects, other similar documentation to the customer by an official or specially authorized person without mandatory engineering systems for environmental protection or commissioning (acceptance) into operation of structures without such protection, if they created a danger of severe technological accidents or environmental catastrophes, death or mass disease of the population or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused the consequences provided for in part one of this article, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
{Article 253 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 254. Mismanagement of Land

1. Mismanagement of land, if it caused prolonged reduction or loss of their fertility, withdrawal of land from agricultural circulation, washing away of the humus layer, disruption of soil structure, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. Intentional evasion of mandatory recultivation of land disturbed as a result of pilot-industrial development of amber deposits or extraction of amber on the basis of a special permit for the use of subsoil, which caused substantial harm, -
shall be punished by a fine of seven thousand to ten thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in part two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
4. Acts provided for in part two or three of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
Note. In this article, substantial harm is considered to be such harm that exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens established by law by two hundred fifty or more times.
In this article, grave consequences are considered to be such consequences that exceed the tax-free minimum income of citizens established by law by one thousand or more times.
{Article 254 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as revised by Law No. 402-IX of 19.12.2019; amendments adopted by Law of Ukraine No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018 cannot be made due to the new version of the article}

Section IX

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST PUBLIC SAFETY
{Title of Section IX as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 255

Establishment of, leadership of a criminal community or criminal organization, and participation therein
1. Establishment of a criminal organization, leadership of such organization or its structural parts -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property.
2. Participation in a criminal organization -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by an official using his or her official position, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of eight to thirteen years with confiscation of property.
4. Establishment of a criminal community, that is, an association of two or more criminal organizations, leadership of such community -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years with confiscation of property.
