5. Acts provided for in parts one, two or four of this Article, committed by a person who exercises criminal influence or is a person having the status of a subject of enhanced criminal influence, including the status of "thief in law", -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of twelve to fifteen years with confiscation of property.
6. A person, except for an organizer or leader of a criminal organization, shall be released from criminal liability for committing a crime provided for in part two or three of this Article, if he or she, before being notified of suspicion of committing this crime, voluntarily reported the establishment of a criminal organization or participation therein and actively contributed to its detection.
Note. 1. Criminal influence in this Article and Articles 255-1, 255-3 of this Code shall mean any actions of a person who, by virtue of authority, other personal qualities or capabilities, facilitates, incites, coordinates or otherwise influences criminal activity, organizes or directly carries out the distribution of funds, property or other assets (income therefrom) directed at ensuring such activity.
2. A person having the status of a subject of enhanced criminal influence, including the status of "thief in law", in this Article and Articles 255-1, 255-3 of this Code shall mean a person who, by virtue of authority, other personal qualities or capabilities, exercises criminal influence and coordinates the criminal activity of other persons who exercise criminal influence.
{Article 255 as amended by Law No. 671-IX of 04.06.2020}

Article 255-1. Establishment or extension of criminal influence

1. Intentional establishment or extension in society of criminal influence in the absence of the elements specified in part five of Article 255 of this Code, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years with confiscation of property.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or in temporary detention facilities, pre-trial detention centers or penal institutions, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of nine to fifteen years with or without confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this Article, committed by a person having the status of a subject of enhanced criminal influence, including the status of "thief in law", -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of twelve to fifteen years with confiscation of property.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 255-1 pursuant to Law No. 671-IX of 04.06.2020}

Article 255-2

Organization of, facilitation of or participation in a criminal gathering (meeting)
1. Organization of or facilitation of a criminal gathering (meeting) of representatives of criminal organizations or organized groups, or persons who exercise criminal influence, for the purpose of planning the commission of one or more crimes, material support or coordination of criminal activity, including the distribution of proceeds obtained by criminal means, or spheres of criminal influence, as well as participation in such gathering (meeting), including by means of communication, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property.
2. A person, except for an organizer of a criminal gathering (meeting), shall be released from criminal liability for committing a crime provided for in part one of this Article, if he or she, before being notified of suspicion of committing this crime, voluntarily reported this crime and also actively contributed to its detection.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 255-2 pursuant to Law No. 671-IX of 04.06.2020}

Article 255-3. Request for application of criminal influence

1. Request to a person who, as is known to the perpetrator, can exercise criminal influence, in particular to a person having the status of a subject of enhanced criminal influence, including the status of "thief in law", for the purpose of his or her application of such influence -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years with or without confiscation of property.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 255-3 pursuant to Law No. 671-IX of 04.06.2020}

Article 256

Assistance to participants of criminal organizations and concealment of their criminal activity
1. Assistance not promised in advance to participants of criminal organizations and concealment of their criminal activity by providing premises, storage facilities, vehicles, information, documents, technical devices, money, securities, as well as performance not promised in advance of other actions to create conditions that facilitate their criminal activity, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 256 as amended according to Law No. 671-IX of 04.06.2020}
Organization of an armed gang for the purpose of attacking enterprises, institutions, organizations or individual persons, as well as participation in such a gang or in an attack committed by it -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to fifteen years with confiscation of property.
1. Terrorist act, that is, use of weapons, committing an explosion, arson or other actions that created danger to human life or health or causing significant property damage or occurrence of other grave consequences, if such actions were committed for the purpose of violating public safety, intimidating the population, provoking a military conflict, international complication, or for the purpose of influencing the adoption of decisions or commission or non-commission of actions by state authorities or local self-government bodies, officials of these bodies, associations of citizens, legal entities, international organizations, or attracting public attention to certain political, religious or other views of the perpetrator (terrorist), as well as threat of committing the specified actions with the same purpose -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if they led to causing significant property damage or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article that led to the death of a person, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment with confiscation of property or without such.
{Part four of Article 258 excluded on the basis of Law No. 170-V of 21.09.2006}
{Part five of Article 258 excluded on the basis of Law No. 170-V of 21.09.2006}
6. A person shall be released from criminal liability for the act provided for by part one of this article in the part of threat of committing a terrorist act, if they, before being notified of suspicion of committing a crime, voluntarily informed a law enforcement body about this crime, facilitated its termination or disclosure, in case that as a result of this and the measures taken, danger to human life or health or causing significant property damage or occurrence of other grave consequences was averted, if their actions do not contain elements of another crime.
{Article 258 as amended according to Laws No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014, No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019}

Article 258-1. Involvement in committing a terrorist act

1. Involvement of a person in committing a terrorist act or coercion to commit a terrorist act using deception, blackmail, vulnerable condition of a person, or with application or threat of application of violence -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. Acts provided for by part one of this article, committed against several persons or repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to seven years with confiscation of property or without such.
{The Code supplemented with Article 258-1 according to Law No. 170-V of 21.09.2006; as amended according to Law No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014}

Article 258-2. Public calls to commit a terrorist act

1. Public calls to commit a terrorist act, as well as distribution, production or storage for the purpose of distribution of materials with such calls -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same acts committed using mass media, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to four years or imprisonment for a term of up to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property or without such.
{The Code supplemented with Article 258-2 according to Law No. 170-V of 21.09.2006; as amended according to Law No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014}

Article 258-3. Creation of a terrorist group or terrorist organization

1. Creation of a terrorist group or terrorist organization, leadership of such a group or organization or participation in it, as well as organizational or other assistance to the creation or activity of a terrorist group or terrorist organization -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. A person, except the organizer and leader of a terrorist group or terrorist organization, who voluntarily informed a law enforcement body about the relevant terrorist activity, facilitated its termination or disclosure of crimes committed in connection with the creation or activity of such a group or organization, shall be released from criminal liability for the act provided for by part one of this article, if their actions do not contain elements of another crime.
{The Code supplemented with Article 258-3 according to Law No. 170-V of 21.09.2006; as amended according to Laws No. 2258-VI of 18.05.2010, No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014}

Article 258-4. Assistance in committing a terrorist act, undergoing terrorism training

1. Recruitment, arming, training of a person for the purpose of committing a terrorist act, as well as use of a person for this purpose, undergoing terrorism training -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same acts committed against several persons (except undergoing terrorism training) or repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. A person shall be released from criminal liability for the act provided for by part one of this article in the part of undergoing terrorism training, if they voluntarily renounced further criminal activity, informed a law enforcement body about this crime, facilitated prevention or termination of terrorist activity related to the crime committed by them.
4. Acts provided for by part one or two of this article, committed by a person who exercises criminal influence, or is a person who has the status of a subject of increased criminal influence, including the status of "thief in law", -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of twelve to fifteen years with confiscation of property.
Note. Undergoing terrorism training in this article shall be understood as receiving by a person instructions, including acquiring knowledge or practical skills, from another person regarding the production or use of explosives, firearms or other weapons or harmful or dangerous substances, or regarding other specific methods or means for the purpose of carrying out activity that according to law is terrorist.
{The Code supplemented with Article 258-4 according to Law No. 170-V of 21.09.2006; as amended according to Laws No. 2258-VI of 18.05.2010, No. 1689-VII of 07.10.2014; as revised by Law No. 2997-IX of 21.03.2023}

Стаття 258-5. Financing of terrorism

1. Provision, collection or storage of any assets directly or indirectly with the purpose of their use or with awareness of the possibility that they will be used in whole or in part for any purposes by an individual terrorist or terrorist group (organization), or for the organization, preparation or commission of a terrorist act, involvement in the commission of a terrorist act, public calls to commit a terrorist act, creation of a terrorist group (organization), facilitation of the commission of a terrorist act, training in terrorism, crossing of the state border of Ukraine with a terrorist purpose, conduct of any other terrorist activity, as well as attempts to commit such actions, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to two years and with confiscation of property.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or from mercenary motives, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or on a large scale, or if they caused significant property damage, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group or on an especially large scale, or if they caused other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
4. A person, except for the organizer or leader of a terrorist group (organization), shall be released from criminal liability for actions provided for in this article if he or she voluntarily, before being brought to criminal liability, reported the relevant terrorist activity or otherwise facilitated its termination or prevention of the crime which he or she financed or facilitated the commission of, provided that his or her actions do not contain the elements of another crime.
Примітка. 1. Financing of terrorism is deemed to have been committed on a large scale if the amount of financial or material support exceeds six thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. Financing of terrorism is deemed to have been committed on an especially large scale if the amount of financial or material support exceeds eighteen thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
{Кодекс доповнено статтею 258-5 згідно із Законом № 2258-VI від 18.05.2010; із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 361-IX від 06.12.2019, № 2997-IX від 21.03.2023}

Стаття 258-6. Crossing of the state border of Ukraine with a terrorist purpose

1. Crossing of the state border of Ukraine for the purpose of carrying out activities which, in accordance with the law, are terrorist, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to six years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same action committed repeatedly or by an official using his or her official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed during a state of emergency or martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years with confiscation of property.
4. A person shall be released from criminal liability for an act provided for in part one of this article if he or she voluntarily abandoned further criminal activity, notified a law enforcement body of this crime, facilitated the prevention or termination of terrorist activity related to the crime committed by him or her.
{Кодекс доповнено статтею 258-6 згідно із Законом № 2997-IX від 21.03.2023}

Стаття 259

Knowingly false report about a threat to the safety of citizens, destruction or damage to property objects
1. Knowingly false report about the preparation of an explosion, arson or other actions that threaten the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to six years.
2. The same act, if the objects of the knowingly false report were critically important infrastructure objects or buildings or structures ensuring the activities of state authorities, or health care institutions, or educational institutions, or if it caused grave consequences or was committed repeatedly, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.
Примітка. The term "critically important infrastructure objects" is used in the meaning defined in the Law of Ukraine "On the Basic Principles of Ensuring Cybersecurity of Ukraine".
{Стаття 259 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законами № 3075-III від 07.03.2002, № 4955-VI від 07.06.2012, № 1292-IX від 02.03.2021}

Стаття 260. Creation of paramilitary or armed formations not provided for by law

1. Creation of paramilitary formations not provided for by the laws of Ukraine or participation in their activities -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years with confiscation of property or without such.
2. Creation of armed formations not provided for by law or participation in their activities -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Leadership of the formations specified in parts one or two of this article, their financing, supply of weapons, ammunition, explosives or military equipment to them -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
4. Participation as part of the formations provided for in parts one or two of this article in an attack on enterprises, institutions, organizations or on citizens -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property or without such.
5. An act provided for in part four of this article that resulted in the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
6. A person who was part of the formations specified in this article shall be released from criminal liability under this article for actions provided for in parts one or two of this article if he or she voluntarily left such formation and notified state authorities or local self-government bodies of its existence.
Примітка. 1. Paramilitary should be understood as formations that have an organizational structure of a military type, namely: unity of command, subordination and discipline, and in which military or drill or physical training is conducted.
2. Armed formations should be understood as paramilitary groups that illegally have at their disposal suitable for use firearms, explosive or other weapons.
{Стаття 260 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 1689-VII від 07.10.2014}

Стаття 261

Attack on objects where there are items that pose an increased danger to the environment
Attack on objects where radioactive, chemical, biological or explosive materials, substances, items are manufactured, stored, used or by which they are transported, with the purpose of seizing, damaging or destroying these objects -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with confiscation of property or without such.
{Стаття 261 із змінами, внесеними згідно із Законом № 1689-VII від 07.10.2014}

Article 262

Theft, misappropriation, extortion of firearms, ammunition, explosives or radioactive materials or taking possession of them by fraud or abuse of official position
1. Theft, misappropriation, extortion of firearms (except smooth-bore hunting firearms), ammunition, explosives, explosive devices or radioactive materials or taking possession of them by fraud -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, as well as taking possession of the items listed in part one of this article by an official's abuse of his or her official position -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they are committed by an organized group, robbery for the purpose of stealing firearms (except smooth-bore hunting firearms), ammunition, explosives or radioactive materials, as well as extortion of these items combined with violence dangerous to life and health, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years with confiscation of property.

Article 263

Illegal handling of weapons, ammunition or explosives
1. Carrying, storing, acquiring, transferring or selling firearms (except smooth-bore hunting firearms), ammunition, explosives or explosive devices without a permit provided by law -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
2. Carrying, manufacturing, repairing or selling daggers, Finnish knives, brass knuckles or other cold weapons without a permit provided by law -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred twenty to two hundred forty hours, or arrest for a term of three to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. A person who voluntarily surrendered weapons, ammunition, explosives or explosive devices to the authorities shall not be held criminally liable for committing acts provided for in part one or two of this article.
{Article 263 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 5064-VI of 05.07.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2150-IX of 24.03.2022}

Article 263-1

Illegal manufacture, conversion or repair of firearms or falsification, illegal removal or alteration of its marking, or illegal manufacture of ammunition, explosives or explosive devices
1. Illegal manufacture, conversion or repair of firearms or falsification, illegal removal or alteration of its marking, or illegal manufacture of ammunition, explosives or explosive devices -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 263-1 in accordance with Law No. 5064-VI of 05.07.2012; as amended in accordance with Law No. 228-VII of 14.05.2013}

Article 264. Negligent storage of firearms or ammunition

Negligent storage of firearms or ammunition, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.

Article 265. Illegal handling of radioactive materials

1. Acquisition, carrying, storage, use, transfer, modification, destruction, dispersion or demolition of radioactive materials (sources of ionizing radiation, radioactive substances or nuclear materials in any physical state in a facility or product or in any other form) without a permit provided by law -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
2. The same acts committed with the purpose of causing the death of people, harm to human health, property damage on a large scale or significant environmental pollution, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or if they caused the death of people, property damage on a large scale, significant environmental pollution or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years.
4. A person who committed a crime provided for in part one of this article shall be released from criminal liability if he or she voluntarily surrendered radioactive materials (sources of ionizing radiation, radioactive substances or nuclear materials in any physical state in a facility or product or in any other form) to the authorities.
Note. In Articles 265, 265-1 of this Code, property damage is considered to be inflicted on a large scale if direct losses constitute an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by three hundred times or more.
{Article 265 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1071-V of 24.05.2007, No. 1638-VIII of 04.10.2016}

Article 265-1

Illegal manufacture of a nuclear explosive device or a device that disperses radioactive material or emits radiation
1. Illegal manufacture of any nuclear explosive device or a device that disperses radioactive material or emits radiation and can, through its properties, cause the death of people, harm to human health, property damage on a large scale or significant environmental pollution, -
shall be punished by a fine of three hundred to seven hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same act committed with the purpose of causing the death of people, harm to human health, property damage on a large scale or significant environmental pollution, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or if they caused the death of people, property damage on a large scale, significant environmental pollution or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 265-1 in accordance with Law No. 1071-V of 24.05.2007}

Article 266. Threat to commit theft or use radioactive materials

1. Threat to commit theft of radioactive materials with the purpose of compelling a natural or legal person, international organization or state to perform any action or refrain from it, if there were grounds to fear the implementation of this threat, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. Threat to use radioactive materials with the purpose of causing the death of people or other grave consequences, if there were grounds to fear the implementation of this threat, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

Stattia 267

Violation of the rules for handling explosive, flammable and corrosive substances or radioactive materials
1. Violation of the rules for storage, use, accounting, transportation of explosive substances or radioactive materials or other rules for handling them, as well as illegal sending of these substances or materials by mail or cargo, if this violation created a danger of death of people or the occurrence of other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, as well as illegal sending by mail or baggage of flammable or corrosive substances, if they caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to twelve years.
{Article 267 as amended according to Laws No. 4837-VI of 24.05.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Stattia 267-1. Violation of the requirements of the radiation safety regime

1. Movement by any means beyond the exclusion zone or the zone of unconditional (mandatory) resettlement without providing the permit provided for by law or conducting dosimetric control of food products of plant and animal origin, industrial or other products, animals, fish, plants or any other objects -
shall be punished by a fine from fifty to eighty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term from one to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Acquisition for the purpose of use or sale of objects defined in part one of this article, if the fact of their origin from the exclusion zone or the zone of unconditional (mandatory) resettlement is known in advance to the guilty person, -
shall be punished by a fine from seventy to eighty-five non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term from two to four years, or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed for the purpose of sale, or sale of objects defined in part one of this article, -
shall be punished by a fine from seventy-five to one hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
4. Acts provided for in parts one, two or three of this article, committed by an official or repeatedly, as well as if they caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to seven years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 267-1 according to Law No. 966-V of 19.04.2007}

Stattia 268. Illegal importation into the territory of Ukraine of waste and secondary raw materials

1. Importation into the territory of Ukraine or transit through its territory of waste or secondary raw materials without proper permission -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. Importation into the territory of Ukraine or transit through its territory of substances or materials belonging to the category of hazardous waste, which are prohibited from importation, -
shall be punished by a fine from fifty to two hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Stattia 269

Illegal transportation on an aircraft of explosive or flammable substances
1. Illegal transportation on an aircraft of explosive or flammable substances -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts that caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to ten years.
{Article 269 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Stattia 270

Violation of the requirements of fire or technogenic safety established by legislation
1. Violation of the requirements of fire or technogenic safety established by legislation, if it caused the occurrence of a fire or accident, which caused harm to the health of people or property damage in a large amount, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand twenty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of people, property damage in a particularly large amount or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years.
Note. Property damage is considered to be caused in large amounts if direct losses constitute an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by three hundred or more times, and in particularly large amounts - if direct losses constitute an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
{Article 270 as amended according to Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1366-IX of 30.03.2021}

Stattia 270-1

Intentional destruction or damage to objects of housing and communal services
1. Intentional destruction or damage to objects of housing and communal services, if this led or could lead to the impossibility of operation, disruption of the normal functioning of such objects, which caused danger to the life or health of people or property damage in a large amount, -
shall be punished by a fine from one hundred to two hundred fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or in a generally dangerous manner, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused property damage in a particularly large amount or the death of a person or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to twelve years.
Note. 1. Objects of housing and communal services in this article should be understood as housing stock, objects of improvement, heat supply, water supply and sewerage, as well as their networks or components (manhole covers, gratings on them, etc.).
2. Property damage is considered to be caused in large amounts if direct losses constitute an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by three hundred or more times, and in particularly large amounts - if direct losses constitute an amount that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
{Section IX was supplemented with Article 270-1 according to Law No. 2924-VI of 13.01.2011}

Rozdil X

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST PRODUCTION SAFETY
{Title of Section X as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Stattia 271. Violation of the requirements of legislation on labour protection

1. Violation of the requirements of legislative and other regulatory legal acts on labour protection by an official of an enterprise, institution, organization or a citizen - a subject of entrepreneurial activity, if this violation caused harm to the health of the victim, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for the same term.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to two years or without such.
{Article 271 as amended according to Laws No. 4837-VI of 24.05.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Stattia 272. Violation of safety rules during the performance of works with increased danger

1. Violation of safety rules during the performance of works with increased danger at production or any enterprise by a person who is obliged to observe them, if this violation created a threat of death of people or occurrence of other grave consequences or caused harm to the health of the victim, -
is punished by a fine from one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
is punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or deprivation of liberty for a term up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 272 as amended according to Laws No. 4837-VI of 24.05.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Stattia 273

Violation of safety rules at explosion-hazardous enterprises or in explosion-hazardous workshops
1. Violation of safety rules at explosion-hazardous enterprises or in explosion-hazardous workshops by a person who is obliged to observe them, if it created a threat of death of people or occurrence of other grave consequences or caused harm to the health of the victim, -
is punished by correctional labor for a term up to two years or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
is punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.

Stattia 274. Violation of nuclear or radiation safety rules

1. Violation at production of nuclear or radiation safety rules by a person who is obliged to observe them, if it created a threat of death of people or occurrence of other grave consequences or caused harm to the health of the victim, -
is punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to four years or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
is punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.

Stattia 275

Violation of rules concerning the safe use of industrial products or safe operation of buildings and structures
1. Violation during the development, design, manufacture or storage of industrial products of rules concerning its safe use, as well as violation during the design or construction of rules concerning the safe operation of buildings and structures, by a person who is obliged to observe such rules, if this created a threat of death of people or occurrence of other grave consequences or caused harm to the health of the victim, -
are punished by a fine from one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to two years or without such.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
is punished by correctional labor for a term up to two years or restriction of liberty for a term up to five years, or deprivation of liberty for a term from two to five years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 275 as amended according to Laws No. 4837-VI of 24.05.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Rozdil XI

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST THE SAFETY OF TRAFFIC AND OPERATION OF TRANSPORT
{Title of Section XI as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Stattia 276

Violation of traffic safety or operation rules of railway, water or air transport
1. Violation by an employee of railway, water or air transport of traffic safety or operation rules of transport, as well as poor-quality repair of vehicles, tracks, signaling and communication means, if this created danger to human life or occurrence of other grave consequences, -
are punished by correctional labor for a term up to two years or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused the victim medium gravity or grave bodily injuries or caused great material damage, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to seven years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of people, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to ten years.
{Article 276 as amended according to Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Stattia 276-1

Performance of professional activity by a crew member or air traffic service by an air traffic control dispatcher (traffic service dispatcher) in a state of alcoholic intoxication or under the influence of narcotic or psychotropic substances
Performance of professional activity by a crew member or air traffic service by an air traffic control dispatcher (traffic service dispatcher) in a state of alcoholic intoxication or under the influence of narcotic or psychotropic substances -
is punished by correctional labor for a term up to two years or deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 276-1 according to Code No. 3393-VI of 19.05.2011}

Stattia 277. Damage to communication routes and vehicles

1. Intentional destruction or damage to communication routes, structures on them, rolling stock or vessels, communication or signaling means, as well as other actions aimed at bringing the specified objects into a state unsuitable for operation, if this caused or could cause an accident of a train, vessel or disrupted the normal operation of transport, or created danger to human life or occurrence of other grave consequences, -
are punished by a fine from fifty to two hundred fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or restriction of liberty for the same term, or deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused the victim medium gravity or grave bodily injuries or caused great material damage, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to eight years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of people, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from seven to fifteen years.
{Article 277 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2742-VI of 02.12.2010}

Stattia 278

Hijacking or seizure of railway rolling stock, air, sea or river vessel
1. Hijacking or seizure of railway rolling stock, air, sea or river vessel -
is punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to six years.
2. The same actions, committed by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy or combined with violence that is not dangerous to the life or health of the victim, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to eight years.
3. Actions provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group or combined with violence dangerous to the life or health of the victim, or such that caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
are punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to fifteen years.

Article 279

Blocking of Transport Communications, and also Seizure of a Transport Enterprise
1. Blocking of transport communications by arranging obstacles, disconnecting power supply or by other means, which disrupted the normal operation of transport or created danger to human life, or the occurrence of other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine from fifty to one hundred and fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. Seizure of a railway station, aerodrome, port, station or other transport enterprise, institution or organisation -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to eight years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from eight to fifteen years.
{Article 279 as amended according to Laws No. 2742-VI of 02.12.2010, No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 1183-VII of 08.04.2014}

Article 280

Coercion of a Transport Worker to Non-Performance of Their Official Duties
1. Coercion of a worker of railway, air, water, road, urban electric or main pipeline transport to non-performance of their official duties by threat of murder, infliction of grievous bodily harm or destruction of property of this worker or persons close to them, -
shall be punished by a fine up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed by an organised group or combined with violence dangerous to the life or health of the victim, or such that caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to twelve years.

Article 281. Violation of Rules of Air Flights

1. Violation of flight safety rules of aircraft by persons who are not workers of air transport, if this created danger to human life or the occurrence of other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused the victim medium gravity bodily harm or inflicted large material damage, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to five years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of people, or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to twelve years.
{Article 281 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 282. Violation of Rules of Use of Airspace

1. Violation of rules of launching rockets, conducting all types of shooting, explosive works or committing other actions in airspace, if this created a threat to the safety of air flights, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term up to five years.
2. The same acts, if they caused the victim medium gravity or grievous bodily harm or inflicted large material damage, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to five years.
3. Acts provided for by part one or two of this article, if they caused the death of people, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to twelve years.
{Article 282 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 283. Unauthorised Stopping of a Train Without Urgent Need

1. Unauthorised stopping of a train without urgent need by means of a stop-valve or by disconnecting the air brake line or by other means, if this created a threat of death of people or the occurrence of other grave consequences or caused harm to the health of the victim, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to two thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to ten years.
{Article 283 as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 284. Failure to Render Assistance to a Vessel and Persons in Distress

Failure to render assistance by the captain of a vessel in case of collision with another vessel to the crew and passengers of the latter, and also to persons encountered at sea or on another waterway who are in distress, if he had the possibility to render such assistance without serious danger to his own vessel, its crew and passengers, -
shall be punished by a fine up to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to two years.

Article 285. Failure of a Captain to Report the Name of Their Vessel Upon Collision of Vessels

Failure of the captain of a vessel to report to another vessel that collided with it at sea, the name and port of registry of their vessel, and also the place of their departure and destination, despite the availability of the possibility to provide this information, -
shall be punished by a fine up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months.

Article 286

Violation of Road Traffic Safety Rules or Operation of Transport by Persons Driving Vehicles
1. Violation of road traffic safety rules or operation of transport by a person driving a vehicle, which caused the victim medium gravity bodily harm, -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused the death of the victim or inflicted grievous bodily harm, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term up to three years or without such.
3. Acts provided for by part one of this article, if they caused the death of several persons, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to ten years with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term up to three years.
Note. Vehicles in this article and articles 286-1, 287, 289 and 290 shall be understood to mean all types of automobiles, tractors and other self-propelled machines, trams and trolleybuses, and also motorcycles and other mechanical vehicles.
{Article 286 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 586-VI of 24.09.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 286-1

Violation of road traffic safety rules or transport operation by persons driving vehicles while intoxicated
1. Violation of road traffic safety rules or transport operation by a person driving a vehicle while under the influence of alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, which caused medium severity bodily injury to the victim, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term of three to five years.
2. The same acts committed while under the influence of alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, if they caused severe bodily injury to the victim, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term of five to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed while under the influence of alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, if they caused the death of the victim, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term of five to ten years.
4. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed while under the influence of alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or under the influence of medicines that reduce attention and reaction speed, if they caused the death of several persons, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term of seven to ten years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 286-1 in accordance with Law No. 1231-IX of 16.02.2021; additionally see Clarification No. 04-27/3-2021/97637 of 18.03.2021}

Article 287

Putting into operation technically defective vehicles or other violation of their operation
Putting into operation knowingly technically defective vehicles, allowing a person who is under the influence of alcohol, drugs or other intoxication or under the influence of medicines that reduce their attention and reaction speed, or does not have the right to drive a vehicle, to drive a vehicle, or other gross violation of transport operation rules that ensure road traffic, committed by a person responsible for the technical condition or operation of vehicles, if this caused medium severity bodily injury, severe bodily injury or death to the victim, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years, with deprivation of the right to hold positions related to responsibility for the technical condition or operation of vehicles for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Article 287 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 586-VI of 24.09.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 288

Violation of regulatory legal acts, norms and rules relating to road traffic safety
Violation of regulatory legal acts, norms and rules relating to road traffic safety, committed by a person responsible for the construction, reconstruction, repair or maintenance of highways, streets, railway crossings, other road structures, or by a person performing such work, if this violation caused medium severity bodily injury, severe bodily injury or death to the victim, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
{Article 288 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 586-VI of 24.09.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 124-IX of 20.09.2019}

Article 289. Unlawful seizure of a vehicle

1. Unlawful seizure of a vehicle -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term;
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or combined with violence that is not dangerous to the life or health of the victim, or with a threat of using such violence, or committed with penetration into premises or other storage, or with the use of electronic devices to interfere with the operation of technical security means, or if the object of unlawful seizure is a vehicle whose value amounts to one hundred to two hundred and fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by an organized group or combined with violence dangerous to the life or health of the victim, or with a threat of using such violence, or if the object of unlawful seizure is a vehicle whose value exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by two hundred and fifty or more times, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years with confiscation of property or without such;
4. A person who committed for the first time acts provided for in part one of this article and voluntarily reported this to law enforcement authorities, returned the vehicle to the owner and fully compensated for the damage caused shall be released from criminal liability by the court.
1. Unlawful seizure of a vehicle in this article shall be understood as intentional, for any purpose, unlawful removal by any means of a vehicle from the owner or user against their will.
2. In accordance with part two of this article, repetition shall be understood as the commission of such acts by a person who previously committed unlawful seizure of a vehicle or a criminal offense provided for in Articles 185, 186, 187, 189-191, 262, 290, 410 of this Code.
{Paragraph 3 of the note to Article 289 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 875-IX of 03.09.2020}
{Article 289 as amended by Law No. 2903-IV of 22.09.2005; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 875-IX of 03.09.2020}

Article 290

Destruction, forgery or replacement of numbers of vehicle units and assemblies
Destruction, forgery or replacement of the identification number, engine, chassis or body numbers, or replacement without permission of the relevant authorities of the number plate with the identification number of the vehicle -
shall be punished by a fine of one hundred and fifty to two hundred and fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{Article 290 as amended in accordance with Law No. 586-VI of 24.09.2008}

Article 291. Violation of rules in force in transport

Violation of rules in force in transport that ensure traffic safety, as well as regulatory legal acts, norms and rules for the manufacture, conversion, repair of vehicles, if this caused the death of people or other serious consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
{Article 291 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 586-VI of 24.09.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 124-IX of 20.09.2019}

Article 292

Damage to main or industrial oil, gas, condensate and petroleum product pipelines
1. Damage or destruction of main or industrial oil, gas, condensate or petroleum product pipelines, branches from them, technologically related facilities, structures, metering devices, automation, telemechanics, communication, signaling, as well as illegal interference with the operation of technological equipment, if these actions led to disruption of the normal operation of the specified pipelines or created a danger to human life, -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, as well as in a manner dangerous to the public, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of people, other accidents with people or led to an accident, fire, significant environmental pollution or other grave consequences, or were committed by an organized group, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
{Article 292 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4838-VI of 24.05.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section XII

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER AND MORALITY
{Title of Section XII as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 293. Group violation of public order

Organization of group actions that led to a gross violation of public order or substantial disruption of the operation of transport, an enterprise, institution or organization, as well as active participation in such actions -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months.
{Article 293 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 294. Mass riots

1. Organization of mass riots accompanied by violence against a person, pogroms, arson, destruction of property, seizure of buildings or structures, forced eviction of citizens, resistance to representatives of authority with the use of weapons or other objects used as weapons, as well as active participation in mass riots -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
2. The same actions, if they led to the death of people or to other grave consequences, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years.
{Article 294 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 295. Calls to commit actions threatening public order

Public calls for pogroms, arson, destruction of property, seizure of buildings or structures, forced eviction of citizens, threatening public order, as well as distribution, production or storage for the purpose of distribution of materials of such content -
shall be punishable by a fine of up to fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{Article 295 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}
1. Hooliganism, that is, a gross violation of public order motivated by obvious disrespect for society, accompanied by particular audacity or exceptional cynicism, -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to two thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years.
2. The same actions committed by a group of persons, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they were committed by a person previously convicted of hooliganism, or involved resistance to a representative of authority or a representative of the public performing duties to protect public order, or other citizens who were stopping hooligan actions, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
4. Actions provided for in parts one, two or three of this article, if they were committed with the use of firearms or cold weapons or another object specially adapted or prepared in advance for inflicting bodily injuries, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
{Article 296 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 3075-III of 07.03.2002, No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 297. Desecration of a grave, other burial place or the body of a deceased person

1. Desecration of a grave, other burial place, the body (remains, ashes) of a deceased person or an urn with the ashes of a deceased person, as well as illegal seizure of the body (remains, ashes) of a deceased person, an urn with the ashes of a deceased person, objects located on (in) a grave, in another burial place, on the body (remains, ashes) of a deceased person, -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Desecration or destruction of a mass grave or the grave of the Unknown Soldier, a monument erected in memory of those who fought against Nazism during the Second World War - Soviet liberator soldiers, participants in the partisan movement, underground fighters, victims of Nazi persecution, internationalist soldiers and peacekeepers, as well as persons who defended the independence, sovereignty and territorial integrity of Ukraine and took direct part in the anti-terrorist operation, in the implementation of measures to ensure national security and defense, repel and contain armed aggression of the Russian Federation or another state recognized by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine as an aggressor, participants of the Revolution of Dignity, fighters for the independence of Ukraine in the XX century, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or from mercenary or hooligan motives, or with respect to a monument erected in memory of those who fought against Nazism during the Second World War, victims of Nazi persecution, as well as internationalist soldiers and peacekeepers, or combined with the use of violence or threat of its use, -
shall be punishable by restraint of liberty for a term of four to five years or imprisonment for four to seven years.
4. Actions provided for in parts one, two or three of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years.
{Article 297 as worded by Laws No. 1166-VI of 19.03.2009, No. 728-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as worded by Law No. 734-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2747-VIII of 06.06.2019}

Article 298

Illegal conduct of search works on an archaeological heritage site, destruction, demolition or damage to cultural heritage sites
1. Illegal conduct of archaeological surveys, excavations, other earthworks or underwater works on an archaeological heritage site -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
2. Intentional illegal destruction, demolition or damage to cultural heritage sites or their parts -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
3. Acts provided for by part two of this Article, committed with regard to monuments of national significance, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
4. Acts provided for by parts two or three of this Article, committed with the purpose of searching for movable objects originating from archaeological heritage sites, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
5. Acts provided for by parts two or three of this Article, committed by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
{Article 298 as amended by Law No. 1626-IV of 18.03.2004; as amended by Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as amended by Law No. 2518-VI of 09.09.2010; as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 298-1

Destruction, damage or concealment of documents or unique documents of the National Archival Fund
1. Intentional destruction, damage or concealment of documents of the National Archival Fund -
shall be punished by a fine of fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months.
2. The same acts committed with regard to unique documents of the National Archival Fund, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this Article, committed by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to five years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 298-1 by Law No. 534-V of 22.12.2006}

Article 299. Cruelty to animals

1. Cruelty to animals belonging to vertebrates, including stray animals, violation of rules for transporting animals, if such acts resulted in bodily injuries, mutilation or death of an animal, inciting animals against each other, committed with hooligan or mercenary motives, as well as propaganda, public calls to commit acts having signs of cruelty to animals, dissemination of materials with calls to commit such acts -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of one to three years or deprivation of liberty for a term of two to three years with confiscation of the animal.
2. The same acts committed in the presence of a minor or juvenile, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to five years with confiscation of the animal.
3. Acts provided for by part one or two of this Article, committed with particular cruelty or with regard to two or more animals, or repeatedly, or by a group of persons, or committed in an active manner, or violent acts against an animal aimed at satisfying sexual passion, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to eight years with confiscation of the animal.
4. Acts provided for by parts one, two or three of this Article, committed with the purpose of creating images, works, film, video, audio products, or sale or dissemination of images, works, film, video, audio products that promote cruelty to animals, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to eight years with confiscation of the animal.
{Article 299 as amended by Law No. 2120-VIII of 22.06.2017; as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018; text of Article 299 as amended by Law No. 1684-IX of 15.07.2021 - enters into force on 08.11.2021}

Article 300

Import, production or distribution of works that promote the cult of violence and cruelty, racial, national or religious intolerance and discrimination
1. Import into Ukraine of works that promote the cult of violence and cruelty, racial, national or religious intolerance and discrimination, with the purpose of sale or distribution or their production, storage, transportation or other movement with the same purpose or their sale or distribution, as well as coercion to participate in their creation -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts with regard to film and video products that promote the cult of violence and cruelty, racial, national or religious intolerance and discrimination, as well as sale to minors or distribution among them of works that promote the cult of violence and cruelty, racial, national or religious intolerance and discrimination, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to five years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this Article, if they were committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, as well as coercion of minors to participate in the creation of works that promote the cult of violence and cruelty, racial, national or religious intolerance and discrimination, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 300 as amended by Law No. 1707-VI of 05.11.2009; as amended by Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 301. Importation, production, sale and distribution of pornographic items

1. Importation into Ukraine of works, images or other items of a pornographic nature for the purpose of sale or distribution, or their production, storage, transportation or other movement with the same purpose, or their sale or distribution, as well as coercion to participate in their creation -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts committed with respect to film and video products, computer programs of a pornographic nature, as well as sale to minors or distribution among them of works, images or other items of a pornographic nature, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to five years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or with obtaining income in a large amount, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to seven years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Part four of Article 301 excluded on the basis of Law No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}
{Part five of Article 301 excluded on the basis of Law No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}
Note. For the purposes of Articles 301 and 301-1 of this Code, obtaining income in a large amount occurs when its amount exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens two hundred times or more.
{Article 301 as amended according to Laws No. 1520-VI of 11.06.2009, No. 1819-VI of 20.01.2010, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}

Стаття 301-1

Obtaining access to child pornography, its acquisition, storage, importation, transportation or other movement, production, sale and distribution
1. Intentional obtaining of access to child pornography using information and telecommunication systems or technologies, or intentional acquisition thereof, or intentional storage, importation into Ukraine, transportation or other movement of child pornography without the purpose of sale or distribution -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of three to six months or restriction of liberty for a term up to five years, or deprivation of liberty for a term of two to six years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. Importation into Ukraine of child pornography for the purpose of sale or distribution, or its storage, transportation or other movement with the same purpose -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of seven to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Production, distribution, sale of child pornography or coercion of a minor to participate in the creation of child pornography -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to twelve years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
4. Acts provided for by parts two or three of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or with obtaining income in a large amount, or coercion of a child to participate in the creation of child pornography -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of nine to fifteen years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
5. A minor shall not be subject to criminal liability for production, storage, transportation or other movement of child pornography, if such acts were committed without the purpose of sale or distribution.
6. A person who committed acts provided for by part one of this Article for the purpose of performing the powers assigned to them on the grounds and in the manner provided for by law shall not be subject to criminal liability.
Note. In this Article, obtaining access to child pornography using information and telecommunication systems or technologies shall be considered intentional if it is proven that the person was aware that in such a way they would obtain access to child pornography (for example, it is proven that the person obtained such access repeatedly or by making a payment, etc.).
{The Code supplemented with Article 301-1 according to Law No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}

Стаття 301-2

Conducting a spectacular event of a sexual nature with the participation of a minor
1. Conducting a spectacular event of a sexual nature, including using information and telecommunication systems or technologies, in which a minor is involved, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. Attending a spectacular event of a sexual nature for the purpose of viewing it, including using information and telecommunication systems or technologies, in which a child or minor is knowingly to the attendee involved, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to six years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Involving a minor in participation in a spectacular event of a sexual nature that takes place, including using information and telecommunication systems or technologies, or coercion of a minor to participate in such an event using deception, blackmail, vulnerable state of the person or with the use or threat of use of violence -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of seven to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
4. Acts provided for by part three of this Article, committed with respect to a child, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to fifteen years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
