Note. A spectacular event of a sexual nature in this Article shall be understood as a public display in any form of products of a sexual nature or stage actions, the purpose of which is the embodiment of sexual acts.
{The Code supplemented with Article 301-2 according to Law No. 1256-IX of 18.02.2021}

Стаття 302. Creation or maintenance of places of debauchery and procuring

1. Creation or maintenance of places of debauchery, as well as procuring for debauchery -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to two thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years.
2. The same acts committed for the purpose of profit or by a person previously convicted of this criminal offense, or committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this Article, committed with the involvement of a minor, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to seven years.
4. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this Article, committed with the involvement of a child, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 302 as amended according to Laws No. 2334-VIII of 14.03.2018, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 303. Pimping or involving a person in prostitution

1. Involving a person in prostitution or forcing them to engage in prostitution using deception, blackmail or the vulnerable state of that person, or with the use or threat of use of violence, or pimping -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed against several persons or repeatedly, or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or by an official using their official position, or by a person on whom the victim was materially or otherwise dependent, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to seven years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed against a minor or by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
4. Acts provided for in parts one, two or three of this article, committed against a young child, or if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years with confiscation of property or without such.
Note. 1. Pimping in this article shall be understood as actions of a person to facilitate the engagement in prostitution by another person.
2. Liability for involving a young child or minor in prostitution or forcing them to engage in prostitution under this article shall arise regardless of whether such acts were committed using deception, blackmail, the vulnerable state of the said persons or with the use or threat of use of violence, using official position, or by a person on whom the victim was materially or otherwise dependent.
{Article 303 as amended by Law No. 3316-IV of 12.01.2006}

Article 304. Involving minors in unlawful activity

1. Involving minors in unlawful activity, in drunkenness, in begging, gambling -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
2. The same acts committed against a young child or by a father, mother, stepfather, stepmother, guardian or custodian, or by a person on whom duties regarding the upbringing of the victim or care for them are imposed, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to ten years.
{Article 304 as amended by Laws No. 616-VI of 01.10.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section XIII

CRIMINAL OFFENCES IN THE SPHERE OF CIRCULATION OF NARCOTIC DRUGS, PSYCHOTROPIC SUBSTANCES, THEIR ANALOGUES OR PRECURSORS AND OTHER CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST PUBLIC HEALTH
{Title of Section XIII as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 305

Smuggling of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors or falsified medicinal products
1. Smuggling of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors or falsified medicinal products, that is their movement across the customs border of Ukraine outside customs control or with concealment from customs control, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, and also if the subject of these acts were particularly dangerous narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances or narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors or falsified medicinal products in large amounts, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to ten years with confiscation of property.
3. Smuggling of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors or falsified medicinal products, committed by an organized group, and also if the subject of smuggling were narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors or falsified medicinal products in especially large amounts, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twelve years with confiscation of property.
Note. The concept of large and especially large amount of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors, as well as poisonous or potent substances or poisonous or potent medicinal products, or falsified medicinal products, applied in this section, is determined by the central executive body that ensures the formation of state policy in the sphere of health care, jointly with the central executive body that ensures the formation of state policy in the sphere of circulation of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues and precursors, counteraction to their illegal circulation.
{Article 305 as amended by Law No. 875-V of 05.04.2007; as amended by Law No. 3718-VI of 08.09.2011; as amended by Laws No. 5065-VI of 05.07.2012, No. 5460-VI of 16.10.2012, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015}

Article 306

Use of funds obtained from illegal circulation of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues, precursors, poisonous or potent substances or poisonous or potent medicinal products
1. Use of funds obtained from illegal circulation of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues, precursors, poisonous or potent substances or poisonous or potent medicinal products for the purpose of continuing illegal circulation of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues, precursors, poisonous or potent substances or poisonous or potent medicinal products -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or in large amounts, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
Note. Large amount shall be understood as funds the sum of which constitutes two hundred or more non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
{Article 306 as amended by Laws No. 430-IV of 16.01.2003, No. 485-IV of 06.02.2003, No. 3826-VI of 06.10.2011, No. 4016-VI of 04.11.2011, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015; as amended by Law No. 361-IX of 06.12.2019}

Стаття 307

Illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation, forwarding or sale of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues
1. Illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding for the purpose of sale, as well as illegal sale of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by a person who has previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in Articles 308-310, 312, 314, 315, 317 of this Code, or with the involvement of a minor, as well as sale of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues in places intended for educational, sports and cultural events, and in other places of mass presence of citizens, or sale or transfer of these substances to places of deprivation of liberty, or if the subject of such acts were narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues in large amounts or particularly dangerous narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of six to ten years with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by an organised group, as well as if the subject of such acts were narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues in particularly large amounts, or committed with the involvement of a minor or in relation to a minor, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of nine to twelve years with confiscation of property.
4. A person who has voluntarily surrendered narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues and indicated the source of their acquisition or assisted in the detection of criminal offences related to their illegal circulation, shall be released from criminal liability for their illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation, forwarding (part one of this Article, part one of Article 309 of this Code).
{Article 307 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 3826-VI of 06.10.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 308

Theft, misappropriation, extortion of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues or taking possession of them by fraud or abuse of official position
1. Theft, misappropriation, extortion of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues or taking possession of them by fraud -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to six years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or with the use of violence that is not dangerous to the life or health of the victim, or with the threat of use of such violence, or by a person who has previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in Articles 306, 307, 310, 311, 312, 314, 317 of this Code, or in large amounts, as well as taking possession of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues by abuse by an official of his or her official position -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, if they were committed in particularly large amounts, or by an organised group, robbery for the purpose of theft of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, as well as extortion of these drugs or substances combined with violence dangerous to life and health, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property.
{Article 308 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 309

Illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues without the purpose of sale
1. Illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues without the purpose of sale -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years.
2. The same acts committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or within a year after conviction under this Article or if the subject of such acts were narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues in large amounts, -
shall be punishable by a fine of two thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed with the involvement of a minor, as well as if the subject of such acts were narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues in particularly large amounts, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
4. A person who has voluntarily applied to a medical institution and started treatment for drug addiction shall be released from criminal liability for acts provided for in part one of this Article.
{Article 309 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 310. Sowing or cultivation of opium poppy or cannabis

1. Illegal sowing or illegal cultivation of opium poppy in the amount of one hundred to five hundred plants or cannabis in the amount of ten to fifty plants -
shall be punishable by a fine of one hundred to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. Illegal sowing or illegal cultivation of opium poppy or cannabis by a person who has been convicted under this Article or who has previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in Articles 307, 309, 311, 317 of this Code, or committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy for the purpose of sale, as well as illegal sowing or illegal cultivation of opium poppy in the amount of five hundred or more plants or cannabis in the amount of fifty or more plants -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
{Article 310 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1165-VI of 19.03.2009, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 311

Illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of precursors
1. Illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of precursors for the purpose of their use for the production or manufacture of narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances -
shall be punishable by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or in large amounts, or for the purpose of sale, as well as illegal sale of precursors -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
3. Acts provided for in part one, committed by an organised group or in particularly large amounts, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with confiscation of property.
4. A person who has voluntarily surrendered precursors intended for the production or manufacture of narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances, and indicated the source of their acquisition or assisted in the detection of criminal offences related to the illegal circulation of precursors, narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, shall be released from criminal liability for their illegal production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation, forwarding (part one of this Article).
{Article 311 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 312

Theft, misappropriation, extortion of precursors or taking possession of them by fraud or abuse of official position
1. Theft, misappropriation, extortion of precursors or taking possession of them by fraud with the purpose of further sale, as well as their sale for the production or manufacture of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues -
shall be punishable by a fine of up to seventy tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or with the use of violence that is not dangerous to life or health, or with the threat of use of such violence, or on a large scale, as well as taking possession of precursors by abuse by an official of his or her official position, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed by an organized group or on an especially large scale, robbery with the purpose of theft of precursors, as well as their extortion combined with violence dangerous to life and health, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
{Article 312 as amended in accordance with Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Стаття 313

Theft, misappropriation, extortion of equipment intended for the manufacture of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, or taking possession of it by fraud or abuse of official position and other illegal acts with such equipment
1. Theft, misappropriation, extortion of equipment intended for the manufacture of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, or taking possession of it by fraud, as well as illegal manufacture, acquisition, storage, transfer or sale to other persons of such equipment -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or by a person who previously committed one of the crimes provided for in Articles 306, 312, 314, 315, 317, 318 of this Code, as well as taking possession of equipment intended for the manufacture of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues by abuse by an official of his or her official position -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to six years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, if they were committed by an organized group or with the purpose of manufacturing especially dangerous narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, robbery with the purpose of theft of equipment intended for the manufacture of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, as well as its extortion combined with violence dangerous to life and health, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with confiscation of property.
{Article 313 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 314

Illegal introduction into the body of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues
1. Illegal introduction by any means of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues into the body of another person against his or her will -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
2. The same acts, if they led to narcotic dependence of the victim or were committed repeatedly or by a person who previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in Articles 306-312 and 314-318 of this Code, or were committed against two or more persons, or if they caused medium gravity or grievous bodily injury to the victim, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed against a minor or a person who is in a helpless state, or a pregnant woman, or if they were connected with the introduction into the body of another person of especially dangerous narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, as well as if as a result of such acts the death of the victim occurred, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
{Article 314 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 315

Inducement to use narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues
1. Inducement of a certain person to use narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues -
shall be punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
2. The same act committed repeatedly or against two or more persons, or against a minor, as well as by a person who previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in Articles 307, 308, 310, 314, 317 of this Code, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
{Article 315 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 316. Незаконне публічне вживання наркотичних засобів

1. Public or committed by a group of persons illegal use of narcotic drugs in places intended for conducting educational, sports and cultural events, and in other places of mass presence of citizens -
shall be punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to four years or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a person who previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in Articles 307, 310, 314, 315, 317, 318 of this Code, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
{Article 316 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 317

Organization or maintenance of places for illegal use, production or manufacture of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues
1. Organization or maintenance of places for illegal use, production or manufacture of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or their analogues, as well as provision of premises for this purpose -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or from mercenary motives, or by a group of persons, or with the involvement of a minor, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years with confiscation of property.
{Article 317 as amended in accordance with Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008}

Стаття 318

Illegal manufacture, forgery, use or sale of forged documents for obtaining narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances or precursors
1. Illegal manufacture, forgery, use or sale of forged or illegally obtained documents that give the right to obtain narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances or precursors intended for the production or manufacture of these drugs or substances, -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by a person who previously committed one of the criminal offences provided for in Articles 306-317 of this Code, -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
{Article 318 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 319

Unlawful issuance of a prescription for the right to acquire narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances
1. Unlawful issuance of a prescription for the right to acquire narcotic drugs or psychotropic substances from mercenary motives or other personal interests -
shall be punished by a fine of up to seventy tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred sixty to two hundred forty hours, or arrest for a term of three to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act committed repeatedly -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 319 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 3826-VI of 06.10.2011}

Стаття 320

Violation of established rules for the circulation of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors
1. Violation of established rules for sowing or growing opium poppy or cannabis, as well as violation of the rules for production, manufacture, storage, accounting, dispensing, distribution, trade, transportation, forwarding or use of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors intended for the production or manufacture of these drugs or substances -
shall be punished by a fine of up to seventy tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to four years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly, or if they caused a shortage of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors in large amounts, or led to theft, misappropriation, extortion of narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances, their analogues or precursors or taking possession of them by fraud or abuse by an official of his or her official position -
shall be punished by a fine of seventy to one hundred twenty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or imprisonment for a term of three to five years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 320 as amended in accordance with Law No. 3826-VI of 06.10.2011}

Стаття 321

Unlawful production, manufacture, acquisition, transportation, forwarding, storage for the purpose of sale or sale of poisonous or potent substances or poisonous or potent medicinal products
1. Unlawful production, manufacture, acquisition, transportation, forwarding, storage for the purpose of sale, sale of poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic or their analogues, or poisonous or potent medicinal products, as well as commission of such acts with respect to equipment intended for the production or manufacture of poisonous or potent substances, or poisonous or potent medicinal products, committed without special permission therefor -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. Violation of established rules for production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, dispensing, accounting, transportation, forwarding of poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic or their analogues, or poisonous or potent medicinal products -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed repeatedly, by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or if the subject of such acts were poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic or their analogues, or poisonous or potent medicinal products in large amounts -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
4. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed by an organized group, or if the subject of such acts were poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic or their analogues, or poisonous or potent medicinal products in especially large amounts -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
5. A person who voluntarily surrendered poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic or their analogues, or poisonous or potent medicinal products and indicated the source of their acquisition or assisted in the disclosure of crimes related to their unlawful circulation, shall be released from criminal liability for unlawful production, manufacture, acquisition, transportation, forwarding, storage of poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic or their analogues, or poisonous or potent medicinal products, as well as for committing such acts without special permission therefor (part one of this article) with respect to equipment intended for the production or manufacture of poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic or their analogues, or poisonous or potent medicinal products.
{Article 321 as worded by Law No. 875-V of 05.04.2007; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 3826-VI of 06.10.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 321-1

Falsification of medicinal products or circulation of falsified medicinal products
1. Manufacture, acquisition, transportation, forwarding, storage for the purpose of sale or sale of knowingly falsified medicinal products -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or by an official by abuse of official position, by a medical or pharmaceutical worker, or with the use of information systems, including the Internet, or in large amounts, or if they caused prolonged health disorder of a person, as well as production of falsified medicinal products -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years and with confiscation of property.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused the death of a person or other grave consequences, or were committed in especially large amounts -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment, with confiscation of property.
4. A person who voluntarily surrendered falsified medicinal products and indicated the source of their acquisition or assisted in the disclosure of crimes related to their circulation, shall be released from criminal liability for acquisition, transportation, forwarding or storage for the purpose of sale, sale of knowingly falsified medicinal products, their importation into the territory of Ukraine, exportation from the territory of Ukraine, transit through its territory (part one of this article, if such acts did not create a threat to human life or health).
{The Code was supplemented with Article 321-1 in accordance with Law No. 3718-VI of 08.09.2011; as worded by Law No. 5065-VI of 05.07.2012; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 284-IX of 12.11.2019}

Article 321-2

Violation of the established procedure for preclinical study, clinical trials and state registration of medicinal products
1. Intentional violation of the established procedure for preclinical study, clinical trials of medicinal products, falsification of their results, as well as violation of the established procedure for state registration of medicinal products -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of one to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of two to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, if they caused the death of the victim or other grave consequences -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of eight to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of two to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 321-2 in accordance with Law No. 5065-VI of 05.07.2012}

Article 322

Illegal organization or maintenance of places for the use of intoxicating substances
Illegal organization or maintenance of places for the use for the purpose of intoxication of medicinal and other substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic substances or their analogues, as well as provision of premises for such purpose -
shall be punishable by a fine of seventy to one hundred and twenty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 322 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 3826-VI of 06.10.2011}

Article 323. Inducement of minors to use doping

1. Inducement of minors to use doping -
shall be punishable by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act committed by a father, mother, stepfather, stepmother, guardian or custodian or a person entrusted with the duties of upbringing the victim or caring for him or her, his or her coach, or repeatedly, or by a person who previously committed one of the crimes provided for in Articles 314, 315, 317, 324 of this Code -
shall be punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed against two or more persons, or if they caused harm to the health of the victim -
shall be punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
4. Acts provided for in parts one, two or three of this Article, if they caused substantial harm to the health of the victim or other grave consequences -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
Note. Doping means substances and methods that are included in the list prohibited by the Anti-Doping Code of the Olympic Movement.
{Article 323 as amended in accordance with Law No. 616-VI of 01.10.2008}

Article 324. Inducement of minors to use intoxicating substances

Inducement of minors to use intoxicating substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic substances or their analogues -
shall be punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.

Article 325

Violation of sanitary rules and norms for the prevention of infectious diseases and mass poisonings
{Article 325 as amended by Law No. 530-IX of 17.03.2020 - effective for three months from the date of publication of the Law}
1. Violation of rules and norms established for the purpose of preventing epidemic and other infectious diseases, as well as mass non-infectious diseases (poisonings) and combating them, if such acts caused or knowingly could cause the spread of these diseases -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts, if they caused the death of people or other grave consequences -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
{Article 325 as amended by Laws No. 1125-VI of 17.03.2009. Amendments to Article 325 additionally see in the Law. No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018. As amended by Law No. 530-IX of 17.03.2020 - effective for three months from the date of publication of the Law, taking into account amendments introduced by Law No. 540-IX of 30.03.2020}

Article 326

Violation of rules for handling microbiological or other biological agents or toxins
1. Violation of rules for storage, use, accounting, transportation of microbiological or other biological agents or toxins, other rules for handling them, if it created a threat of death of people or occurrence of other grave consequences or caused harm to the health of the victim -
shall be punishable by a fine of two thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act, if it caused the death of people or other grave consequences -
shall be punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 326 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 327

Procurement, processing or sale of radioactively contaminated food products or other products
1. Procurement, processing for the purpose of sale or sale of food products or other products radioactively contaminated above permissible levels, if they created a threat of death of people or occurrence of other grave consequences or caused harm to the health of the victim -
shall be punishable by a fine of two thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. Procurement, processing for the purpose of sale or sale of food products or other products radioactively contaminated above permissible levels, if they caused the death of people or other grave consequences -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
{Article 327 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section XIV

CRIMINAL OFFENSES IN THE SPHERE OF PROTECTION OF STATE SECRETS, INVIOLABILITY OF STATE BORDERS, ENSURING CONSCRIPTION AND MOBILIZATION
{Title of Section XIV as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 328. Disclosure of state secrets

1. Disclosure of information constituting a state secret by a person to whom this information was entrusted or became known in connection with the performance of official duties, in the absence of signs of state treason or espionage -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same act, if it caused grave consequences -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.

Article 329. Loss of documents containing state secrets

1. Loss of documents or other material carriers of classified information containing state secrets, as well as objects, information about which constitutes a state secret, by a person to whom they were entrusted, if the loss resulted from violation of the procedure established by law for handling the specified documents and other material carriers of classified information or objects -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of up to three years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same act, if it caused grave consequences -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.

Стаття 330

Transfer or collection of information constituting official information gathered in the process of operational-investigative, counterintelligence activity, in the sphere of national defense
1. Transfer or collection for the purpose of transfer to foreign enterprises, institutions, organizations or their representatives of information constituting official information gathered in the process of operational-investigative, counterintelligence activity, in the sphere of national defense, by a person to whom such information was entrusted or became known in connection with the performance of official duties, in the absence of signs of high treason or espionage, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to three years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts committed from mercenary motives, or such that caused grave consequences for the interests of the state, or committed repeatedly, or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 330 as amended according to Laws No. 2939-VI of 13.01.2011, No. 1170-VII of 27.03.2014}
{Article 331 excluded on the basis of Law No. 1723-IV of 18.05.2004}

Стаття 332. Illegal transportation of persons across the state border of Ukraine

1. Illegal transportation of persons across the state border of Ukraine, organization of illegal transportation of persons across the state border of Ukraine, management of such actions or facilitation of their commission by advice, instructions, provision of means or removal of obstacles -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
2. The same acts committed in a manner dangerous to the life or health of the person who was illegally transported across the state border of Ukraine, or committed with respect to several persons, or repeatedly, or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Acts provided for by part one or two of this article, committed by an organized group or committed from mercenary motives, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to nine years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years with confiscation of property.
{Article 332 as worded by Law No. 3186-VI of 05.04.2011; as amended according to Law No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016}

Стаття 332-1

Violation of the procedure for entry to and exit from the temporarily occupied territory of Ukraine
1. Violation of the procedure for entry to the temporarily occupied territory of Ukraine and exit from it with the purpose of causing harm to the interests of the state -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Acts provided for by part one or two of this article, committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{The Code supplemented with Article 332-1 according to Law No. 1207-VII of 15.04.2014; as amended according to Law No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016}

Стаття 332-2. Illegal crossing of the state border of Ukraine

1. Crossing of the state border of Ukraine with the purpose of causing harm to the interests of the state or by a person who is prohibited from entering the territory of Ukraine, or by representatives of units of the armed forces or other security agencies of an aggressor state in any manner outside checkpoints across the state border of Ukraine or at checkpoints across the state border of Ukraine without appropriate documents, or with documents containing false information, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, combined with violence or with the use of weapons, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
{The Code supplemented with Article 332-2 according to Law No. 2599-VIII of 18.10.2018}

Стаття 333

Violation of the procedure for carrying out international transfers of goods subject to state export control
1. Violation of the established procedure for carrying out international transfers of goods subject to state export control, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
2. The same act committed repeatedly or by an organized group, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 333 as worded by Law No. 668-IV of 03.04.2003; as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 334. Violation of the rules of international flights

Flight into Ukraine or flight from Ukraine without appropriate permission, as well as failure to comply with the routes, landing places, airways, corridors or flight levels specified in the permission -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term up to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 334 as amended according to Law No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016}

Стаття 335

Evasion of conscription for fixed-term military service, military service by conscription of persons of officer rank
Evasion of conscription for fixed-term military service, military service by conscription of persons of officer rank -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to three years.
{Article 335 as worded by Law No. 116-VIII of 15.01.2015}

Стаття 336

Evasion of conscription for military service during mobilization, for a special period, for military service by conscription of persons from among reservists in a special period
Evasion of conscription for military service during mobilization, for a special period, for military service by conscription of persons from among reservists in a special period -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
{Article 336 as worded by Law No. 1357-IX of 30.03.2021}

Стаття 336-1

Evasion of civil defense service in a special period or in case of targeted mobilization
Evasion of civil defense service in a special period (except for the recovery period) or in case of targeted mobilization -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
{The Code supplemented with Article 336-1 according to Law No. 186-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 337. Evasion of military registration or training (special) assemblies

1. Evasion by a conscript, person liable for military service, reservist of military registration after a warning made by the relevant head of the territorial center for recruitment and social support, heads of relevant bodies of the Security Service of Ukraine, relevant units of the Foreign Intelligence Service of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by a fine of three hundred to five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to one year.
2. Evasion by a person liable for military service, reservist of training (special) assemblies -
shall be punished by a fine of five hundred to seven hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years.
{Article 337 as worded by Law No. 1357-IX of 30.03.2021}

Section XV

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST THE AUTHORITY OF STATE AUTHORITIES, LOCAL SELF-GOVERNMENT BODIES, CITIZENS' ASSOCIATIONS AND CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST JOURNALISTS
{Title of Section XV as amended according to Laws No. 421-VIII of 14.05.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 338. Desecration of state symbols

1. Public desecration of the State Flag of Ukraine, the State Coat of Arms of Ukraine or the State Anthem of Ukraine -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. Public desecration of an officially established or raised flag or coat of arms of a foreign state -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months or imprisonment for a term up to two years.
{Article 338 as amended according to Laws No. 1441-VI of 04.06.2009, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 339

Unlawful raising of the State Flag of Ukraine on a river or sea vessel
Raising the State Flag of Ukraine on a river or sea vessel without the right to this Flag -
shall be punished by a fine up to fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months.

Article 340

Unlawful obstruction of the organization or holding of assemblies, rallies, marches and demonstrations
Unlawful obstruction of the organization or holding of assemblies, rallies, street marches and demonstrations, if this act was committed by an official or with the use of physical violence, -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term up to two years or arrest for a term up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.

Article 341. Seizure of state or public buildings or structures

Seizure of buildings or structures that ensure the activities of state authorities, local self-government bodies, citizens' associations, for the purpose of unlawful use thereof or obstruction of the normal operation of enterprises, institutions, organizations -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Part two of Article 341 excluded on the basis of Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}
{Article 341 as amended according to Law No. 642-VII of 10.10.2013, as amended according to Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 342

Resistance to a representative of authority, a law enforcement officer, a state bailiff, a private bailiff, a member of a public formation for the protection of public order and the state border or a serviceman, an authorized person of the Deposit Guarantee Fund for individuals
1. Resistance to a representative of authority, except for a state bailiff, a private bailiff, during the performance of his official duties -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to two thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. Resistance to a law enforcement officer during the performance of his official duties, a state bailiff or a private bailiff during the forced execution of decisions, a member of a public formation for the protection of public order and the state border or a serviceman during the performance by these persons of the duties assigned to them regarding the protection of public order or an authorized person of the Deposit Guarantee Fund for individuals -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to four years, or imprisonment for a term up to two years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, combined with coercion of these persons by violence or threat of use of such violence to perform clearly unlawful actions, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
{Article 342 as worded by Law No. 2677-VI of 04.11.2010; as amended according to Laws No. 4452-VI of 23.02.2012, No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 1403-VIII of 02.06.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 343

Interference in the activities of a law enforcement officer, a forensic expert, an employee of the state enforcement service, a private bailiff
1. Influence in any form on a law enforcement officer, a forensic expert, an employee of the state enforcement service or a private bailiff, as well as a close relative of a state bailiff or a private bailiff with the purpose of obstructing the performance of his official duties, the implementation of forensic expert activities or to obtain an unlawful decision -
shall be punished by a fine up to one hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to one year, or arrest for a term up to three months.
2. The same acts, if they obstructed the prevention of a criminal offense or the detention of a person who committed it, or were committed by an official using his official position, -
shall be punished by deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to five years or arrest for a term up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to four years.
{Article 343 as amended according to Laws No. 2677-VI of 04.11.2010, No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 1403-VIII of 02.06.2016, No. 2447-VIII of 07.06.2018, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 344. Interference in the activities of a state official

1. Unlawful influence in any form on the President of Ukraine, the Chairman of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, a people's deputy of Ukraine, the Prime Minister of Ukraine, a member of the Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine, the Chairman of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine, a judge of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine, the Chairman or a member of the High Council of Justice, the Chairman or a member of the High Qualification Commission of Judges of Ukraine, the Commissioner of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine for Human Rights or his representative, the Director of the National Anti-Corruption Bureau of Ukraine, the Director of the State Bureau of Investigation, the Director of the Bureau of Economic Security of Ukraine, the Prosecutor General, the Chairman of the Accounting Chamber or another member of the Accounting Chamber, the Chairman or a member of the Central Election Commission, the Chairman of the National Bank of Ukraine, a member of the National Council of Ukraine on Television and Radio Broadcasting, the Chairman of the Antimonopoly Committee of Ukraine, the Chairman of the State Property Fund of Ukraine, the Chairman of the State Committee for Television and Radio Broadcasting of Ukraine, a member of the National Commission carrying out state regulation in the spheres of energy and utilities, with the purpose of obstructing the performance by them of official duties or to obtain unlawful decisions -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to three hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts, if they were committed by a person using his official position, -
shall be punished by a fine of three hundred to five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
{Article 344 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 576-VIII of 02.07.2015, No. 1540-VIII of 22.09.2016, No. 1798-VIII of 21.12.2016, No. 2136-VIII of 13.07.2017, No. 1888-IX of 17.11.2021}

Article 345. Threat or violence against a law enforcement officer

1. Threat of murder, violence or destruction or damage of property against a law enforcement officer, as well as against his or her close relatives in connection with the performance by such officer of official duties -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term up to two years or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Intentional infliction upon a law enforcement officer or his or her close relatives of battery, light or moderate bodily injuries in connection with the performance by such officer of official duties -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Intentional infliction upon a law enforcement officer or his or her close relatives of grievous bodily injury in connection with the performance by such officer of official duties -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to twelve years.
4. Acts provided for by parts one, two or three of this Article, committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from seven to fourteen years.
{Article 345 as amended according to Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 345-1. Threat or violence against a journalist

1. Threat of murder, violence or destruction or damage of property against a journalist, his or her close relatives or family members in connection with the performance by such journalist of lawful professional activity -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term up to two years or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. Intentional infliction upon a journalist, his or her close relatives or family members of battery, light or moderate bodily injuries in connection with the performance by such journalist of lawful professional activity -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Intentional infliction upon a journalist, his or her close relatives or family members of grievous bodily injury in connection with the performance by such journalist of lawful professional activity -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to twelve years.
4. Acts provided for by parts one, two or three of this Article, committed by an organized group, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from seven to fourteen years.
Note. Professional activity of a journalist in this Article and Articles 171, 347-1, 348-1 of this Code shall mean systematic activity of a person related to the collection, receipt, creation, dissemination, storage or other use of information for the purpose of its dissemination to an indefinite circle of persons through printed mass media, television and radio organizations, information agencies, the Internet. The status of a journalist or his or her affiliation with a mass media outlet shall be confirmed by an editorial or official certificate or other document issued by a mass media outlet, its editorial office or a professional or creative union of journalists.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 345-1 according to Law No. 421-VIII of 14.05.2015}

Article 346. Threat or violence against a state or public figure

1. Threat of murder, infliction of harm to health, destruction or damage of property, as well as abduction or deprivation of liberty against the President of Ukraine, the Chairman of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, a People's Deputy of Ukraine, the Prime Minister of Ukraine, a member of the Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine, the Chairman or a member of the High Council of Justice, the Chairman or a member of the High Qualifications Commission of Judges of Ukraine, the Chairman or a judge of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine or the Supreme Court or higher specialized courts, the Prosecutor General, the Director of the National Anti-Corruption Bureau of Ukraine, the Director of the Bureau of Economic Security of Ukraine, the Director of the State Bureau of Investigation, the Commissioner of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine for Human Rights, the Chairman or another member of the Accounting Chamber, the Chairman of the National Bank of Ukraine, the leader of a political party of Ukraine, as well as against their close relatives, committed in connection with their state or public activity, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Intentional infliction upon persons specified in part one of this Article of moderate bodily injuries or light bodily injuries, battery or commission of other violent acts in connection with their state or public activity -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from four to seven years.
3. Intentional infliction upon persons specified in part one of this Article of grievous bodily injuries in connection with their state or public activity -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from seven to twelve years.
{Article 346 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 576-VIII of 02.07.2015, No. 1798-VIII of 21.12.2016, No. 1888-IX of 17.11.2021}

Article 347

Intentional destruction or damage of property of a law enforcement officer, an officer of the state executive service or a private bailiff
1. Intentional destruction or damage of property belonging to a law enforcement officer, an officer of the state executive service, a private bailiff (including after dismissal from office) or their close relatives, in connection with the performance of official duties by a law enforcement officer or enforcement of decisions by a state bailiff or a private bailiff (including in the past) -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to five years.
2. The same acts committed by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, or such that caused death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from six to fifteen years.
{Article 347 as amended according to Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 1403-VIII of 02.06.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 347-1. Intentional destruction or damage of property of a journalist

1. Intentional destruction or damage of property belonging to a journalist, his or her close relatives or family members, in connection with the performance by such journalist of lawful professional activity -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term up to five years.
2. The same acts committed by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, or such that caused death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from six to fifteen years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 347-1 according to Law No. 421-VIII of 14.05.2015; as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 348

Encroachment on the life of a law enforcement officer, a member of a public formation for the protection of public order and the state border or a servicemember
Murder or attempted murder of a law enforcement officer or his or her close relatives in connection with the performance by such officer of official duties, as well as a member of a public formation for the protection of public order and the state border or a servicemember in connection with their activity regarding the protection of public order -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from nine to fifteen years or life imprisonment.
{Article 348 as amended according to Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 348-1. Encroachment on the life of a journalist

Murder or attempted murder of a journalist, his or her close relatives or family members in connection with the journalist's lawful professional activity -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of nine to fifteen years or life imprisonment.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 348-1 in accordance with Law No. 421-VIII of 14.05.2015}

Article 349

Hostage-taking of a representative of authority or a law enforcement officer
Hostage-taking or holding as a hostage a representative of authority, a law enforcement officer or their close relatives with the purpose of compelling a state or other institution, enterprise, organization or official to commit or refrain from committing any action as a condition for the release of the hostage -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years.
{Article 349 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 349-1. Hostage-taking of a journalist

Hostage-taking or holding as a hostage a journalist, his or her close relatives or family members with the purpose of compelling the journalist to commit or refrain from committing any action as a condition for the release of the hostage -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 349-1 in accordance with Law No. 421-VIII of 14.05.2015}

Article 350

Threat or violence against an official or a citizen performing a public duty
1. Threat of murder, infliction of grievous bodily harm or destruction or damage to property by a generally dangerous method against an official or his or her close relatives or against a citizen performing a public duty, applied with the purpose of terminating the activity of the official or the citizen performing a public duty, or changing its nature in the interests of the person making the threat, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. Intentional infliction of battery or causing light or moderate bodily injury to an official or a citizen performing a public duty in connection with their official or public activity, as well as commission of such acts against their close relatives -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Intentional infliction of grievous bodily injury to an official or a citizen performing a public duty in connection with their official or public activity, as well as commission of such an act against their close relatives -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
{Article 350 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 351

Obstruction of the activity of a People's Deputy of Ukraine and a deputy of a local council
1. Failure by an official to comply with lawful demands of a People's Deputy of Ukraine, a deputy of a local council, creation of artificial obstacles to their work, provision of knowingly false information to them -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to two thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. Failure by an official to comply with lawful demands of committees of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, temporary investigative commissions of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine or a special temporary investigative commission of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, creation of artificial obstacles to their work, provision of unreliable information -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years, or deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 351 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 39-IX of 10.09.2019, No. 685-IX of 05.06.2020; amendments adopted by Law of Ukraine No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018 cannot be introduced (words replaced by Law No. 685-IX of 05.06.2020)}

Article 351-1

Obstruction of the activity of a state financial control body, the Accounting Chamber, a member of the Accounting Chamber
{Title of Article 351-1 as amended in accordance with Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}
1. Failure by an official to comply with lawful demands of a state financial control body, the Accounting Chamber, a member of the Accounting Chamber, creation of artificial obstacles to their work, provision of knowingly false information to them -
shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 351-1 in accordance with Law No. 576-VIII of 02.07.2015; as amended in accordance with Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Article 351-2

Obstruction of the activity of the High Council of Justice, the High Qualifications Commission of Judges of Ukraine
1. Failure to comply with lawful demands of the High Council of Justice, its body or a member of the High Council of Justice, the High Qualifications Commission of Judges of Ukraine or a member of the High Qualifications Commission of Judges of Ukraine, creation of artificial obstacles to their work -
shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 351-2 in accordance with Law No. 1798-VIII of 21.12.2016}

Article 352

Intentional destruction or damage to property of an official or a citizen performing a public duty
1. Intentional destruction or damage to property belonging to an official or a citizen performing a public duty in connection with their official or public activity, as well as commission of such acts against their close relatives -
shall be punished by a fine of fifty to two hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this Article, committed by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, or such that caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.

Article 353. Unlawful assumption of authority or title of an official

1. Unlawful assumption of authority or title of an official, combined with the commission of any socially dangerous acts, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act connected with the use of uniform or official identification of a law enforcement officer, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to four years or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
{Article 353 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009, No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011}

Article 354. Bribery of an employee of an enterprise, institution or organization

1. Offering or promising to an employee of an enterprise, institution or organization who is not an official, or to a person working for the benefit of an enterprise, institution or organization, to provide him (her) or a third person with an unlawful benefit, as well as providing such a benefit for the commission or non-commission by the employee of any actions using the position he or she holds, or by a person working for the benefit of an enterprise, institution or organization, in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides such a benefit, or in the interests of a third person -
shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to two hundred and fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of up to one hundred hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to one year, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred to two hundred hours, or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acceptance of an offer, promise or receipt by an employee of an enterprise, institution or organization who is not an official, or by a person working for the benefit of an enterprise, institution or organization, of an unlawful benefit, as well as a request to provide such a benefit for oneself or a third person for the commission or non-commission of any actions using the position held by the employee at the enterprise, institution or organization, or in connection with the activities of the person for the benefit of the enterprise, institution or organization, in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides such a benefit, or in the interests of a third person -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred to two hundred hours, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
4. Acts provided for in part three of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy or combined with extortion of an unlawful benefit -
shall be punished by a fine of five hundred to seven hundred and fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred and sixty to two hundred and forty hours, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
5. A person who offered, promised or provided an unlawful benefit shall be released from criminal liability for crimes provided for in Articles 354, 368-3, 368-4, 369, 369-2 of this Code, if after the offer, promise or provision of the unlawful benefit he or she - before receiving information about this crime from other sources by a body whose official is empowered by law to notify of suspicion - voluntarily reported what happened to such body and actively assisted in the disclosure of the crime committed by the person who received the unlawful benefit or accepted its offer or promise. The specified release shall not apply in the event that the offer, promise or provision of an unlawful benefit was committed in relation to persons defined in part four of Article 18 of this Code.
Note. 1. A person working for the benefit of an enterprise, institution, organization shall mean a person who performs work or provides services in accordance with an agreement with such enterprise, institution, organization.
2. In this article, unlawful benefit shall mean money or other property, advantages, privileges, services, intangible assets, any other benefits of an intangible or non-monetary nature that are offered, promised, provided or received without lawful grounds.
3. An offer in Articles 354, 368, 368-3-370 shall mean the expression to an employee of an enterprise, institution or organization, a person providing public services, or an official of an intention to provide an unlawful benefit, and a promise shall mean the expression of such an intention with notification of the time, place, method of providing the unlawful benefit.
4. Repeated in Articles 354, 368, 368-3, 368-4 and 369 of this Code shall mean a crime committed by a person who previously committed any of the crimes provided for in the specified articles.
5. In Articles 354, 368, 368-3 and 368-4 of this Code, extortion of an unlawful benefit shall mean a demand for the provision of an unlawful benefit with a threat to commit actions or inaction using one's position, granted powers, authority, official position in relation to the person providing the unlawful benefit, or the intentional creation of conditions under which a person is forced to provide an unlawful benefit in order to prevent harmful consequences to his or her rights and legitimate interests.
{Article 354 as amended by Laws No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013, No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015}

Article 355. Coercion to perform or not perform civil law obligations

1. Coercion to perform or not perform civil law obligations, that is, a demand to perform or not perform a contract, agreement or other civil law obligation with a threat of violence against the victim or his or her close relatives, damage or destruction of their property in the absence of signs of extortion -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. Coercion to perform or not perform civil law obligations, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or with a threat of murder or infliction of serious bodily injury, or combined with violence that is not dangerous to life and health, or with damage or destruction of property -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
3. Coercion to perform or not perform civil law obligations, committed by an organized group or combined with violence dangerous to life or health, or that caused great damage or caused other serious consequences -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.
Arbitrary conduct, that is, unauthorized, contrary to the procedure established by law, commission of any actions, the legality of which is disputed by an individual citizen or enterprise, institution or organization, if such actions caused significant damage to the interests of a citizen, state or public interests or the interests of the owner -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to three months.

Article 357

Theft, misappropriation, extortion of documents, stamps, seals, taking possession of them by fraud or abuse of official position or their damage
1.
