Theft, misappropriation, extortion of official documents, stamps or seals or taking possession of them by fraud or abuse by a person of his or her official position, as well as their intentional destruction, damage or concealment, and also commission of the same acts in relation to private documents located at enterprises, institutions or organizations regardless of the form of ownership, committed from mercenary motives or in other personal interests -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused disruption of the work of an enterprise, institution or organization or were committed in relation to particularly important documents, stamps, seals -
shall be punished by a fine of up to seventy tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Unlawful taking possession by any means of a passport or other important personal document -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to three months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years.

Article 358

Forgery of documents, seals, stamps and forms, sale or use of forged documents, seals, stamps
1. Forgery of an identity document or other official document issued or certified by an enterprise, institution, organization, citizen-entrepreneur, notary, state registrar, subject of state registration of rights, person authorized to perform state functions regarding registration of legal entities, natural persons - entrepreneurs and public formations, state executor, private executor, auditor or other person who has the right to issue or certify such documents, and which grants rights or releases from obligations, for the purpose of its use by the forger or another person or sale of such document, as well as production of forged seals, stamps or forms of enterprises, institutions or organizations regardless of form of ownership, other official seals, stamps or forms for the same purpose or their sale -
shall be punished by a fine of up to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. Preparation or issuance by an employee of a legal entity regardless of form of ownership who is not an official, preparation or issuance by a private entrepreneur, auditor, expert, appraiser, lawyer, notary, state registrar, subject of state registration of rights, person authorized to perform state functions regarding registration of legal entities, natural persons - entrepreneurs and public formations, state executor, private executor or other person engaged in professional activity related to provision of public or administrative services, of knowingly forged official documents that certify certain facts having legal significance or grant certain rights or release from obligations, forgery for the purpose of use or sale of identity documents, other official documents prepared in the form prescribed by law and containing the requisites provided by law, production of forged official seals, stamps or forms for the purpose of their sale or their sale or sale of knowingly forged official documents, including personal documents of a person, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to two hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of three to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
4. Use of a knowingly forged document -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
Note. An official document in this Article and Articles 357 and 366 of this Code shall mean documents containing information recorded on any material media that confirms or certifies certain events, phenomena or facts that have caused or are capable of causing legal consequences, or may be used as documentary evidence in law enforcement activities, prepared, issued or certified by authorized (competent) persons of state authorities, local self-government, associations of citizens, legal entities regardless of form of ownership and organizational-legal form, as well as individual citizens, including self-employed persons, who are granted the right by law in connection with their professional or official activities to prepare, issue or certify certain types of documents that are prepared in compliance with forms prescribed by law and contain the requisites provided by law.
{Article 358 as amended according to Law No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009 - amendments became invalid on the basis of Law No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended according to Law No. 1666-VIII of 06.10.2016}

Article 359

Illegal acquisition, sale or use of special technical means of obtaining information
1. Illegal acquisition or sale of special technical means of covert obtaining of information, as well as their illegal use -
shall be punished by a fine of two hundred to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to four years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to seven years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this Article, committed by an organized group or if they caused substantial harm to rights, freedoms or interests of individual citizens protected by law, state or public interests or interests of individual legal entities, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.
{Article 359 as worded by Law No. 2338-VI of 15.06.2010}

Article 360. Intentional damage or destruction of a telecommunications network

1. Intentional damage or destruction of a telecommunications network or technical means of telecommunications, or telecommunications facilities that are part of a telecommunications network, if such acts caused termination of provision of telecommunications services, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to three thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of up to one hundred twenty hours, or restriction of liberty for a term of one to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or in a generally dangerous manner, -
shall be punished by a fine of three thousand to ten thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of three to five years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, if they caused property damage on a large scale or caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine of fifteen thousand to twenty-five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of six to eight years.
1. In this Article, property damage is recognized as caused on a large scale if its amount exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by one thousand or more times.
2. Grave consequences in this Article are considered to be acts that caused termination of provision of telecommunications services to critically important infrastructure facilities.
{Article 360 as worded by Law No. 600-IX of 13.05.2020}

Section XVI

CRIMINAL OFFENSES IN THE SPHERE OF USE OF ELECTRONIC COMPUTING MACHINES (COMPUTERS), SYSTEMS AND COMPUTER NETWORKS AND TELECOMMUNICATIONS NETWORKS
{Title of Section XVI as amended according to Laws No. 908-IV of 05.06.2003, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Стаття 361

Unauthorized interference with the operation of information (automated), electronic communication, information and communication systems, electronic communication networks
1. Unauthorized interference with the operation of information (automated), electronic communication, information and communication systems, electronic communication networks -
shall be punishable by a fine of one thousand to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy -
shall be punishable by a fine of three thousand to seven thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, if they resulted in leakage, loss, forgery, blocking of information, distortion of the information processing process or violation of the established procedure for its routing -
shall be punishable by a fine of seven thousand to ten thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of three to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
4. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, if they caused significant damage or created a danger of serious technological accidents or environmental disasters, death or mass disease of the population or other serious consequences -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years or without such.
5. Acts provided for in part three or four of this article, committed during martial law -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
6. Acts provided for in parts one to four of this article shall not be considered unauthorized interference with the operation of information (automated), electronic communication, information and communication systems, electronic communication networks, if they were committed in accordance with the procedure for searching and detecting potential vulnerabilities of such systems or networks.
Note. Significant damage in Articles 361-363-1 is considered to be damage that exceeds the tax-free minimum income of citizens by three hundred times or more.
{Article 361 as amended by Laws No. 908-IV of 05.06.2003, No. 2289-IV of 23.12.2004; as amended by Law No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015; as amended by Law No. 2149-IX of 24.03.2022}

Стаття 361-1

Creation for the purpose of unlawful use, distribution or sale of malicious software or technical means, as well as their distribution or sale
1. Creation for the purpose of unlawful use, distribution or sale, as well as distribution or sale of malicious software or technical means intended for unauthorized interference with the operation of information (automated), electronic communication, information and communication systems, electronic communication networks -
shall be punishable by a fine of two thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or if they caused significant damage -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term up to five years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 361-1 in accordance with Law No. 2289-IV of 23.12.2004; as amended by Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2149-IX of 24.03.2022}

Стаття 361-2

Unauthorized sale or distribution of restricted access information stored in electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or on media of such information
1. Unauthorized sale or distribution of restricted access information stored in electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or on media of such information, created and protected in accordance with current legislation -
shall be punishable by a fine of two thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term up to two years.
2. The same acts committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or if they caused significant damage -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 361-2 in accordance with Law No. 2289-IV of 23.12.2004; as amended by Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
{Article 361-3 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}
{Article 361-4 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Стаття 362

Unauthorized actions with information processed in electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or stored on media of such information, committed by a person who has the right of access to it
1. Unauthorized modification, destruction or blocking of information processed in electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems or computer networks or stored on media of such information, committed by a person who has the right of access to it -
shall be punishable by a fine of two thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years.
2. Unauthorized interception or copying of information processed in electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or stored on media of such information, if it resulted in its leakage, committed by a person who has the right of access to such information -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term up to three years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, or if they caused significant damage -
shall be punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to six years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 362 as amended by Law No. 2289-IV of 23.12.2004; as amended by Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
{Article 362-1 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Стаття 363

Violation of the rules of operation of electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or telecommunication networks or the procedure or rules for the protection of information processed in them
Violation of the rules of operation of electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or telecommunication networks or the procedure or rules for the protection of information processed in them, if it caused significant damage, committed by a person responsible for their operation -
shall be punishable by a fine of two thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for the same term.
{Article 363 as amended by Law No. 2289-IV of 23.12.2004; as amended by Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 363-1

Obstruction of the operation of electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or telecommunications networks by mass distribution of telecommunications messages
1. Intentional mass distribution of telecommunications messages, carried out without prior consent of the addressees, which resulted in disruption or termination of the operation of electronic computing machines (computers), automated systems, computer networks or telecommunications networks, -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons upon prior conspiracy, if they caused significant damage, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 363-1 in accordance with Law No. 2289-IV of 23.12.2004; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Section XVII

CRIMINAL OFFENCES IN THE SPHERE OF OFFICIAL ACTIVITY AND PROFESSIONAL ACTIVITY RELATED TO THE PROVISION OF PUBLIC SERVICES
{The title of Section XVII as amended by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 364. Abuse of power or official position

1. Abuse of power or official position, that is, intentional, for the purpose of obtaining any unlawful benefit for oneself or another natural or legal person, use by an official of power or official position contrary to the interests of the service, if it caused significant damage to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens or state or public interests, or interests of legal persons, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term up to six months or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with a fine from two hundred fifty to seven hundred fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The same act, if it caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to six years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with a fine from five hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens.
{Part three of Article 364 was excluded on the basis of Law No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014}
Note. 1. Officials in Articles 364, 368, 368-5, 369 of this Code are persons who permanently, temporarily or by special authority exercise the functions of representatives of power or local self-government, and also occupy permanently or temporarily in bodies of state power, bodies of local self-government, at state or communal enterprises, in institutions or organizations positions related to the performance of organizational-administrative or administrative-economic functions, or perform such functions by special authority, with which a person is vested by an authorized body of state power, a body of local self-government, a central body of state administration with special status, an authorized body or authorized person of an enterprise, institution, organization, a court or by law.
For the purposes of Articles 364, 368, 368-5, 369 of this Code, legal persons in whose statutory fund the state or communal share respectively exceeds 50 percent or constitutes a value that ensures the state or territorial community the right of decisive influence on the economic activity of such enterprise are equated to state and communal enterprises.
2. Officials are also recognized as officials of foreign states (persons who occupy positions in the legislative, executive or judicial body of a foreign state, including jurors, other persons who exercise state functions for a foreign state, in particular for a state body or state enterprise), as well as foreign arbitrators, persons authorized to resolve civil, commercial or labor disputes in foreign states in a procedure alternative to judicial, officials of international organizations (employees of an international organization or any other persons authorized by such organization to act on its behalf), members of international parliamentary assemblies of which Ukraine is a participant, and judges and officials of international courts.
3. Significant damage in Articles 364, 364-1, 365, 365-2, 367 is considered to be damage that exceeds one hundred or more times the tax-free minimum income of citizens.
4. Grave consequences in Articles 364-367 are considered to be consequences that exceed two hundred fifty or more times the tax-free minimum income of citizens.
{Article 364 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009 - amendments under Law No. 1508-VI lost force on the basis of Law No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as amended by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013, No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014, No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 263-IX of 31.10.2019}

Article 364-1

Abuse of authority by an official of a legal person of private law regardless of organizational-legal form
1. Abuse of authority, that is, intentional, for the purpose of obtaining unlawful benefit for oneself or other persons, use contrary to the interests of a legal person of private law regardless of organizational-legal form by an official of such legal person of their authority, if it caused significant damage to legally protected rights or interests of individual citizens, or state or public interests, or interests of legal persons, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to three months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to two years.
2. The same act, if it caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by a fine from four thousand to six thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term up to six months, or imprisonment for a term from three to six years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
Note. In Articles 364, 364-1, 365-2, 368, 368-3, 368-4, 369, 369-2 and 370 of this Code, unlawful benefit shall be understood as funds or other property, advantages, privileges, services, intangible assets, any other benefits of intangible or non-monetary nature, which are offered, promised, provided or received without lawful grounds therefor.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 364-1 in accordance with Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013, No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014, No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 364-2

Voting by a People's Deputy of Ukraine at a plenary session of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine instead of another People's Deputy of Ukraine
Intentional voting by a People's Deputy of Ukraine at a plenary session of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine instead of another People's Deputy of Ukraine (non-personal voting) -
shall be punished by a fine from three thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 364-2 in accordance with Law No. 404-IX of 19.12.2019}

Стаття 365

Exceeding of Authority or Official Powers by a Law Enforcement Officer
1. Exceeding of authority or official powers, that is, intentional commission by a law enforcement officer of actions that clearly exceed the rights or powers granted to him or her, if they caused substantial harm to legally protected rights, interests of individual citizens, state or public interests, interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. Actions provided for in part one of this article, if they were accompanied by violence or threat of violence, use of weapons or special means or painful actions that insult the personal dignity of the victim, in the absence of signs of torture, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 365 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009, No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Laws No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011, No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014}
{Article 365-1 excluded on the basis of Law No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014}

Стаття 365-2. Abuse of Powers by Persons Providing Public Services

1. Abuse of powers by an auditor, notary, appraiser, authorized person or official of the Deposit Guarantee Fund for Individuals, another person who is not a civil servant, official of local self-government, but carries out professional activities related to the provision of public services, including services of an expert, insolvency practitioner, private executor, independent mediator, member of labor arbitration, arbitrator (while performing these functions), or state registrar, subject of state registration of rights, state executor, private executor for the purpose of obtaining unlawful benefit, if it caused substantial harm to legally protected rights or interests of individual citizens, state or public interests or interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The same act committed against a minor or incapacitated person, elderly person or repeatedly, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for a term of three to five years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with confiscation of property or without such.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 365-2 according to Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended according to Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 629-VIII of 16.07.2015, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 1403-VIII of 02.06.2016, No. 1666-VIII of 06.10.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Стаття 365-3

Inaction of a Law Enforcement Officer Regarding Illegal Activities in Organizing or Conducting Gambling, Lotteries
1. Inaction of a law enforcement officer regarding illegal activities in organizing or conducting gambling, lotteries, that is, intentional failure for mercenary motives or other personal interests or in the interests of third parties to take measures provided for by law upon a statement (notification) about the commission of illegal activities in organizing or conducting gambling, lotteries or concealment of illegal activities in organizing or conducting gambling, lotteries discovered by him or her -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of six to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 365-3 according to Law No. 768-IX of 14.07.2020}

Стаття 366. Official Forgery

1. Preparation, issuance by an official of knowingly false official documents, entry of knowingly false information into official documents, other forgery of official documents -
shall be punished by a fine of two thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with a fine of two hundred fifty to seven hundred fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
{Article 366 as amended according to Laws No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009 - amendments became invalid on the basis of Law No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended according to Laws No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013, No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
{Article 366-1 excluded on the basis of Law No. 1074-IX of 04.12.2020}

Стаття 366-2. Declaration of False Information

1. Intentional entry by a declarant of knowingly false information into the declaration of a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government, provided for by the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption", if such information differs from accurate information by an amount from 500 to 2000 subsistence minimums for able-bodied persons, -
shall be punished by a fine of three thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred fifty to two hundred forty hours or restraint of liberty for a term up to two years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. Intentional entry by a declarant of knowingly false information into the declaration of a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government, provided for by the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption", if such information differs from accurate information by an amount exceeding 2000 subsistence minimums for able-bodied persons, -
shall be punished by a fine of four thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term of one hundred fifty to two hundred forty hours, or restraint of liberty for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
Note. Declarants in this article and Article 366-3 of this Code are persons who, in accordance with parts one and two of Article 45 of the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption", are obliged to submit a declaration of a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 366-2 according to Law No. 1074-IX of 04.12.2020; as amended according to Law No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 366-3

Failure by a declarant to submit a declaration of a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government
Intentional failure by a declarant to submit a declaration of a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government, as provided for by the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption", -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand five hundred to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term from one hundred fifty to two hundred forty hours or restraint of liberty for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for a term of one year, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 366-3 in accordance with Law No. 1074-IX of 04.12.2020; as amended in accordance with Law No. 1576-IX of 29.06.2021}

Стаття 367. Official negligence

1. Official negligence, that is, failure to perform or improper performance by an official of their official duties due to unconscientious attitude towards them, which caused substantial harm to legally protected rights, freedoms and interests of individual citizens, state or public interests or interests of individual legal entities, -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or restraint of liberty for a term up to three years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The same act, if it caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to five years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years and with a fine from two hundred fifty to seven hundred fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or without such.
3. The act provided for in part one of this Article, if it caused the death of a person, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years and with a fine from two thousand to five thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or without such.
{Article 367 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009, No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 3442-IX of 08.11.2023}

Стаття 368

Acceptance of an offer, promise or receipt of unlawful benefit by an official
1. Acceptance of an offer, promise or receipt by an official of unlawful benefit, as well as a request to provide such benefit for oneself or a third person for committing or not committing by such official in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides unlawful benefit, or in the interests of a third person, any action using the power or official position granted to them -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term from three to six months, or imprisonment for a term from two to four years, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
2. The act provided for in part one of this Article, the subject of which was unlawful benefit in a significant amount, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to six years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
3. The act provided for in part one or two of this Article, the subject of which was unlawful benefit in a large amount or committed by an official who occupies a responsible position, or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or repeatedly, or combined with extortion of unlawful benefit, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with confiscation of property.
4. The act provided for in parts one, two or three of this Article, the subject of which was unlawful benefit in an especially large amount, or committed by an official who occupies an especially responsible position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from eight to twelve years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with confiscation of property.
Note. 1. Unlawful benefit in a significant amount is considered to be benefit that exceeds one hundred or more times the tax-free minimum income of citizens, in a large amount - such that exceeds two hundred or more times the tax-free minimum income of citizens, in an especially large amount - such that exceeds five hundred or more times the tax-free minimum income of citizens.
2. Officials who occupy a responsible position, in Articles 368, 368-5, 369 and 382 of this Code are persons specified in paragraph 1 of the note to Article 364 of this Code, whose positions in accordance with Article 6 of the Law of Ukraine "On Civil Service" belong to category "B", judges, prosecutors, investigators and inquiry officers, as well as other, except those specified in paragraph 3 of the note to this Article, heads and deputy heads of state authorities, local self-government bodies, their structural subdivisions and units.
3. Officials who occupy an especially responsible position, in Articles 368, 368-5, 369 and 382 of this Code are:
1) the President of Ukraine, the Prime Minister of Ukraine, members of the Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine, first deputy and deputy ministers, the Chairman and member of the National Council of Ukraine on Television and Radio Broadcasting, the Chairman and member of the National Commission for State Regulation of Financial Services Markets, the Chairman and member of the National Securities and Stock Market Commission, the Chairman and state commissioner of the Antimonopoly Committee of Ukraine, the Chairman of the State Committee for Television and Radio Broadcasting of Ukraine, the Chairman of the State Property Fund of Ukraine, their first deputy and deputies, the Chairman and member of the Central Election Commission, people's deputies of Ukraine, the Commissioner of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine for Human Rights, the Director of the National Anti-Corruption Bureau of Ukraine, the Director of the Bureau of Economic Security of Ukraine, the Director of the State Bureau of Investigation, the Prosecutor General, their first deputies and deputies, the Chairman of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine, their deputies and judges of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine, the Chairman of the Supreme Court, their deputies and judges of the Supreme Court, chairmen of higher specialized courts, their deputies and judges of higher specialized courts, the Chairman of the National Bank of Ukraine, their first deputy and deputies, the Secretary of the National Security and Defense Council of Ukraine, their first deputy and deputies, the Permanent Representative of the President of Ukraine in the Autonomous Republic of Crimea, their first deputy and deputies, advisers and assistants to the President of Ukraine, the Chairman of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, the Prime Minister of Ukraine;
2) persons whose positions in accordance with Article 6 of the Law of Ukraine "On Civil Service" belong to category "A";
3) persons whose positions in accordance with Article 14 of the Law of Ukraine "On Service in Local Self-Government Bodies" are classified as first and second categories of positions in local self-government bodies.
{Article 368 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009, No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Laws No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011, No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013; as amended in accordance with Law No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013; as worded by Law No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 1698-VII of 14.10.2014, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 889-VIII of 10.12.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 263-IX of 31.10.2019, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020, No. 1888-IX of 17.11.2021}
{Article 368-1 became invalid on the basis of Law No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010}

Article 368-2. {Article 368-2 excluded on the basis of Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Article 368-3

Bribery of an official of a legal entity of private law regardless of organizational and legal form
1. Offer or promise to an official of a legal entity of private law regardless of organizational and legal form to provide him or her or a third person with unlawful benefit, as well as provision of such benefit or request to provide it for the commission by the said official of actions or his or her inaction using the powers granted to him or her in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides such benefit, or in the interests of a third person -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term from one hundred to two hundred hours, or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons or an organized group, -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to four years, or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acceptance of an offer, promise or receipt by an official of a legal entity of private law regardless of organizational and legal form of unlawful benefit for himself or herself or a third person for the commission of actions or inaction using the powers granted to him or her in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides such benefit, or in the interests of a third person -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term up to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to two years.
4. Actions provided for in part three of this article, committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons or combined with extortion of unlawful benefit, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to seven years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with confiscation of property.
{Part five of Article 368-3 excluded on the basis of Law No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015}
{Note to Article 368-3 excluded on the basis of Law No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015}
{The Code supplemented with Article 368-3 in accordance with Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013; as worded by Law No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 368-4. Bribery of a person providing public services

1. Offer or promise to an auditor, notary, appraiser, other person who is not a civil servant, official of local self-government, but carries out professional activity related to the provision of public services, including services of an expert, insolvency administrator, private executor, independent mediator, member of labor arbitration, arbitrator (during the performance of these functions), to provide him/her or a third person with unlawful benefit, as well as provision of such benefit or request to provide it for the commission by the person providing public services of actions or his or her inaction using the powers granted to him or her in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides such benefit, or in the interests of a third person -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or community service for a term from one hundred to two hundred hours, or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same actions committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons or an organized group, -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to five thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term up to four years, or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acceptance of an offer, promise or receipt by an auditor, notary, private executor, expert, appraiser, arbitrator or other person carrying out professional activity related to the provision of public services, as well as an independent mediator or arbitrator during consideration of collective labor disputes of unlawful benefit for himself or herself or a third person for the commission of actions or inaction using the powers granted to him or her in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides such benefit, or in the interests of a third person -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term from one to two years, or arrest for a term up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
4. Actions provided for in part three of this article, committed repeatedly or by prior conspiracy by a group of persons or combined with extortion of unlawful benefit, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from four to eight years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years, with confiscation of property.
{Part five of Article 368-4 excluded on the basis of Law No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015}
{The Code supplemented with Article 368-4 in accordance with Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4025-VI of 15.11.2011, No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013; as worded by Law No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 1403-VIII of 02.06.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 368-5. Illicit enrichment

1. Acquisition by a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government of assets the value of which exceeds his or her lawful income by more than six thousand five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to ten years with deprivation of the right to occupy certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
Note. 1. Persons authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government are persons specified in point 1 of part one of Article 3 of the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption".
2. Acquisition of assets shall mean their acquisition by a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government into ownership, as well as acquisition of assets into ownership by another natural or legal person, if it is proven that such acquisition was carried out on behalf of a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government, or that the person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government can directly or indirectly perform actions with respect to such assets that are identical in content to the exercise of the right to dispose of them.
3. Assets shall mean monetary funds (including cash, funds held in bank accounts or on deposit in banks or other financial institutions), other property, property rights, intangible assets, including cryptocurrencies, the amount of reduction of financial obligations, as well as works or services provided to a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government.
4. Lawful income of a person shall mean income lawfully received by a person from lawful sources, in particular those specified in points 7 and 8 of part one of Article 46 of the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption".
5. When determining the difference between the value of acquired assets and lawful income, assets that are the subject of proceedings in cases on recognition of assets as unjustified and their recovery to state revenue shall not be taken into account, as well as assets recovered to state revenue within the framework of such proceedings.
{The Code supplemented with Article 368-5 in accordance with Law No. 263-IX of 31.10.2019}

Article 369. Offer, promise or provision of unlawful benefit to an official

1. Offer or promise to an official to provide him or her or a third person with an unlawful benefit, as well as provision of such benefit for commission or non-commission by the official in the interests of the person who offers, promises or provides such benefit, or in the interests of a third person of any action using the power or official position granted to him or her -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of two to four years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed repeatedly, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to six years with a fine from five hundred to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens, with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article, if the unlawful benefit was provided to an official who holds a responsible position, or committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years with confiscation of property or without such.
4. Acts provided for in part one, two or three of this article, if the unlawful benefit was provided to an official who holds a particularly responsible position, or committed by an organized group of persons or its member, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
{Part five of Article 369 excluded on the basis of Law No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015}
{Article 369 as amended according to Law No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009 - amendments became invalid on the basis of Law No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended according to Law No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012; as worded by Law No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013; as amended according to Law No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013; as worded by Law No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014; as amended according to Laws No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
{Article 369-1 became invalid on the basis of Law No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010}

Article 369-2. Abuse of influence

1. Offer, promise or provision of unlawful benefit to a person who offers or promises (agrees) for such benefit or for provision of such benefit to a third person to influence the decision-making by a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for a term up to two years.
2. Acceptance of offer, promise or receipt of unlawful benefit for oneself or a third person for influence on decision-making by a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government, or offer or promise to exercise influence for provision of such benefit -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to five thousand five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
3. Acceptance of offer, promise or receipt of unlawful benefit for oneself or a third person for influence on decision-making by a person authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government, combined with extortion of such benefit, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years with confiscation of property.
Note. Persons authorized to perform functions of the state or local self-government are persons defined in point 1, subparagraph "a" of point 2 of part one of Article 3 of the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Corruption", as well as officials defined in part four of Article 18 of this Code.
{The Code supplemented with Article 369-2 according to Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended according to Laws No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013, No. 222-VII of 18.04.2013, No. 1261-VII of 13.05.2014, No. 770-VIII of 10.11.2015, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 524-IX of 04.03.2020}

Article 369-3. Unlawful influence on results of official sports competitions

1. Influence on results of official sports competitions by bribery, coercion or incitement or entering into conspiracy regarding results of an official sports competition with the purpose of obtaining unlawful benefit for oneself or a third person or obtaining as a result of such acts unlawful benefit for oneself or a third person -
shall be punished by a fine from two hundred to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of one to three years, or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed regarding official sports competitions of minors or repeatedly, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Violation of the prohibition on placing sports bets related to manipulation of an official sports competition with obtaining as a result unlawful benefit for oneself or other persons in an amount exceeding twenty subsistence minimums for able-bodied persons, -
shall be punished by a fine from two thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
Note. The subject of offences in this article are persons specified in part two of Article 17 of the Law of Ukraine "On Prevention of Influence of Corruption Offences on Results of Official Sports Competitions".
{The Code supplemented with Article 369-3 according to Law No. 743-VIII of 03.11.2015; as amended according to Laws No. 1019-VIII of 18.02.2016, No. 1791-VIII of 20.12.2016, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 370. Provocation of bribery

1. Provocation of bribery, that is actions of an official to incite a person to offer, promise or provision of unlawful benefit or acceptance of offer, promise or receipt of such benefit, in order to subsequently expose the person who offered, promised, provided unlawful benefit or accepted the offer, promise or received such benefit, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term up to five years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years, and with a fine from two hundred fifty to five hundred non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
2. The same act committed by an official of law enforcement agencies, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years and with a fine from five hundred to seven hundred fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens.
{Article 370 as amended according to Laws No. 1508-VI of 11.06.2009, No. 2808-VI of 21.12.2010; as worded by Law No. 3207-VI of 07.04.2011; as amended according to Laws No. 221-VII of 18.04.2013, No. 198-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Section XVIII

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST JUSTICE
{Title of Section XVIII as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 371

Knowingly unlawful detention, summons, house arrest or keeping in custody
1. Knowingly unlawful detention or unlawful summons -
shall be punished by deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to five years or restriction of liberty for a term up to three years.
2. Knowingly unlawful house arrest or keeping in custody -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they caused grave consequences or were committed from mercenary motives or in other personal interests, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term up to three years.
{Article 371 as amended according to Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013}

Article 372. Prosecution of a Knowingly Innocent Person for Criminal Liability

1. Prosecution of a knowingly innocent person for criminal liability by an investigator, prosecutor or other person authorized by law -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same act combined with accusation of committing a grave or particularly grave crime, as well as combined with artificial creation of evidence of accusation or other falsification, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.

Article 373. Coercion to Give Testimony

1. Coercion to give testimony during interrogation by means of unlawful actions by a prosecutor, investigator, inquiry officer or employee of a unit carrying out operative-investigative activity, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts combined with the use of violence or abuse of a person, in the absence of signs of torture, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
{Article 373 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Article 374. Violation of the Right to Defense

1. Failure to admit or failure to provide a defense counsel in a timely manner, as well as other gross violation of the right of a suspect or accused to defense, committed by an investigator, inquiry officer, prosecutor or judge, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. The same acts that resulted in conviction of a person innocent of committing a criminal offense, or committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, or that caused other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 374 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Article 375. {Article 375 excluded on the basis of Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Article 376. Interference in the Activities of Judicial Bodies

1. Interference in any form in the activities of a judge with the purpose of obstructing the performance of his or her official duties or obtaining an unjust decision -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months.
2. The same acts, if they obstructed the prevention of a criminal offense or the detention of a person who committed it, or committed by a person using his or her official position, -
shall be punished by deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
{Article 376 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 376-1

Unlawful Interference in the Operation of Automated Systems in Bodies and Institutions of the Justice System
{Title of Article 376-1 as worded by Law No. 2147-VIII of 03.10.2017 - regarding entry into force see Announcement of the High Council of Justice}
1. Intentional entry of false information or untimely entry of information into the Unified Judicial Information and Telecommunication System, another automated system functioning in a court, the High Council of Justice, the High Qualifications Commission of Judges, the State Judicial Administration of Ukraine, their bodies, unauthorized actions with information contained in such systems, or other interference in the operation of such systems, committed by an official who has the right of access to this system, or by another person by means of unauthorized access to such systems, -
{First paragraph of part one of Article 376-1 as worded by Law No. 2147-VIII of 03.10.2017 - regarding entry into force see Announcement of the High Council of Justice}
shall be punished by a fine of six hundred to one thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or restraint of liberty for a term of two to five years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of one to three years.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed by prior conspiracy by a group of persons, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for a term of three to six years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of two to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 376-1 in accordance with Law No. 1475-VI of 05.06.2009}

Article 377. Threat or Violence Against a Judge, Lay Assessor or Juror

1. Threat of murder, violence or destruction or damage to property against a judge, lay assessor or juror, as well as against their close relatives in connection with their activities related to the administration of justice, -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Intentional infliction on a judge, lay assessor or juror or their close relatives of battery, light or medium gravity bodily injuries in connection with their activities related to the administration of justice, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3. Intentional infliction on a judge, lay assessor or juror or their close relatives of grave bodily injury in connection with their activities related to the administration of justice, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
{Article 377 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 378

Intentional Destruction or Damage to Property of a Judge, Lay Assessor or Juror
1. Intentional destruction or damage to property belonging to a judge, lay assessor or juror or their close relatives, in connection with their activities related to the administration of justice, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2. The same acts committed by means of arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, or that caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of six to fifteen years.
{Article 378 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Article 379

Encroachment on the Life of a Judge, Lay Assessor or Juror in Connection with Their Activities Related to the Administration of Justice
Murder or attempted murder of a judge, lay assessor or juror or their close relatives in connection with their activities related to the administration of justice, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years or life imprisonment.
{Article 379 as amended in accordance with Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended in accordance with Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Стаття 380. Failure to take security measures regarding persons taken under protection

Failure to make a decision, untimely making of a decision or making of insufficiently substantiated decisions, as well as failure to take or untimely taking of sufficient measures for the safety of court employees, law enforcement officers or persons participating in criminal proceedings, members of their families and their close relatives by an official of the body charged with the functions of ensuring the safety of the specified persons, if these actions caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
{Article 380 as amended according to Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Law No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014}

Стаття 381. Disclosure of information about security measures regarding a person taken under protection

1. Disclosure of information about security measures regarding a person taken under protection by an official who made the decision about these measures, a person who implements them, or an official to whom these decisions became known in connection with their official position, as well as by a person taken under protection, if these actions caused harm to the health of the person taken under protection, -
shall be punished by a fine from one hundred to three hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same actions, if they caused the death of the person taken under protection or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 381-1 excluded on the basis of Law No. 1697-VII of 14.10.2014}

Стаття 382. Failure to execute a court decision

1. Intentional failure to execute a sentence, decision, ruling, resolution of a court that has entered into legal force, or obstruction of their execution -
shall be punished by a fine from five hundred to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The same actions committed by an official, -
shall be punished by a fine from seven hundred and fifty to one thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or imprisonment for a term of up to five years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. Actions provided for in part one or two of this article, committed by an official who holds a responsible or particularly responsible position, or by a person previously convicted of a crime provided for in this article, or if they caused substantial harm to legally protected rights and freedoms of citizens, state or public interests or interests of legal entities, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
4. Intentional failure by an official to execute a decision of the European Court of Human Rights, a decision of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine and intentional non-compliance by them with a conclusion of the Constitutional Court of Ukraine -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 382 as worded by Law No. 2453-VI of 07.07.2010; as amended according to Law No. 721-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended according to Laws No. 767-VII of 23.02.2014, No. 2136-VIII of 13.07.2017}

Стаття 383

Knowingly false report about the commission of a criminal offence
1. Knowingly false report to a court, prosecutor, investigator, inquiry officer or pre-trial investigation body about the commission of a criminal offence -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. The same actions combined with accusation of a person of a grave or particularly grave crime or with artificial creation of evidence of accusation, as well as committed from mercenary motives, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Article 383 as amended according to Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Стаття 384. Misleading a court or other authorised body

1. Knowingly false testimony of a witness, victim, knowingly false conclusion of an expert, specialist, prepared for submission or submitted to a body conducting pre-trial investigation, enforcement proceedings, a court, the High Council of Justice, a temporary investigative or special temporary investigative commission of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, submission of knowingly unreliable or forged evidence, knowingly false report of an appraiser on the valuation of property, as well as knowingly incorrect translation made by a translator in the same cases, -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. The same actions combined with accusation of a grave or particularly grave crime, or with artificial creation of evidence of accusation or defence, as well as committed from mercenary motives, -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term of up to two years or restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years, or imprisonment for a term from two to five years.
{Article 384 as amended according to Laws No. 2456-IV of 03.03.2005, No. 890-VI of 15.01.2009 - the Law was declared unconstitutional according to the Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 20-рп/2009 of 10.09.2009; as amended according to Laws No. 2677-VI of 04.11.2010, No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013, No. 2136-VIII of 13.07.2017, No. 2147-VIII of 03.10.2017, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Стаття 385

Refusal of a witness to give testimony or refusal of an expert or translator to perform the duties assigned to them
1. Refusal of a witness to give testimony or refusal of an expert or translator without valid reasons to perform the duties assigned to them in a court, the High Council of Justice, the Constitutional Court of Ukraine or during the conduct of pre-trial investigation, enforcement proceedings, investigation by a temporary investigative commission or special temporary investigative commission of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine -
shall be punished by a fine from fifty to three hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months.
2. A person shall not be subject to criminal liability for refusal to give testimony during the conduct of pre-trial investigation or in court regarding themselves, as well as members of their family or close relatives, the circle of which is determined by law.
{Article 385 as amended according to Laws No. 2456-IV of 03.03.2005, No. 890-VI of 15.01.2009 - the Law was declared unconstitutional according to the Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 20-рп/2009 of 10.09.2009; as amended according to Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 2136-VIII of 13.07.2017, No. 2147-VIII of 03.10.2017, No. 39-IX of 10.09.2019}

Article 386

Obstructing the appearance of a witness, victim, expert, specialist, compelling them to refuse to give testimony or conclusion
Obstructing the appearance of a witness, victim, expert, specialist before a court, pre-trial investigation bodies, temporary investigative and special temporary investigative commissions of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, compelling them to refuse to give testimony or conclusion, as well as to give knowingly false testimony or conclusion by means of threat of murder, violence, destruction of property of these persons or their close relatives or disclosure of information that dishonours them, or bribery of a witness, victim or expert with the same purpose, as well as threat to commit the said acts in revenge for previously given testimony or conclusion -
shall be punished by a fine from fifty to three hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months.
{Article 386 as amended according to Law No. 890-VI of 15.01.2009 - the Law was declared unconstitutional according to the Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 20-rp/2009 of 10.09.2009; as amended according to Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 245-VII of 16.05.2013, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Article 387

Disclosure of data of operational and investigative activities, pre-trial investigation
1. Disclosure without written permission of a prosecutor, investigator or person who conducted operational and investigative activities, of data of operational and investigative activities or pre-trial investigation by a person warned in the manner established by law of the obligation not to disclose such data, -
shall be punished by a fine from fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years.
2. Disclosure of data of operational and investigative activities, pre-trial investigation, committed by a judge, prosecutor, investigator, inquiry officer, employee of an operational and investigative body regardless of whether this person took direct part in operational and investigative activities, pre-trial investigation -
shall be punished by a fine from one hundred to three hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
3. An act provided for by part two of this article, if the disclosed data dishonour a person, humiliate their honour and dignity, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term from three to five years with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.
{Article 387 as amended according to Laws No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012, No. 2213-VIII of 16.11.2017, No. 720-IX of 17.06.2020}

Article 388

Illegal actions regarding property under arrest, pledged property or property that has been inventoried or is subject to confiscation
1. Embezzlement, alienation, concealment, substitution, damage, destruction of property or other illegal actions with property under arrest, with pledged property or property that has been inventoried, or violation of restriction (encumbrance) of the right to use such property, committed by a person to whom this property was entrusted, as well as performance by a representative of a bank or other financial institution of banking operations with funds under arrest, -
shall be punished by a fine from one thousand to four thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labour for a term of up to two years, or restriction of liberty for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
2. Acts provided for by part one of this article, committed regarding property that is subject to confiscation by a court decision that has entered into legal force, -
shall be punished by a fine from four thousand to six thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for the same term, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years or without such.
{Article 388 as worded by Law No. 2456-IV of 03.03.2005; as amended according to Laws No. 3795-VI of 22.09.2011, No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 389. Evasion of punishment not related to deprivation of liberty

1. Evasion by a convicted person of payment of a fine or of serving a sentence in the form of deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities, -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term of up to two years or restriction of liberty for the same term.
2. Evasion by a convicted person of serving a sentence in the form of community service or correctional labour, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{Article 389 as amended according to Laws No. 1404-VIII of 02.06.2016, No. 1492-VIII of 07.09.2016}

Article 389-1. Intentional non-performance of a reconciliation agreement or plea agreement

1. Intentional non-performance by a convicted person of a reconciliation agreement or plea agreement -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 389-1 according to Law No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012}

Article 389-2

Malicious evasion by a person of serving an administrative penalty in the form of community service
Malicious evasion by a person of serving an administrative penalty in the form of community service -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Note. Malicious evasion by a person of serving an administrative penalty in the form of community service for the purposes of this article shall be understood as continued evasion of serving community service by a person brought to administrative liability on the basis of Article 183-2 of the Code of Ukraine on Administrative Offences, that is, failure to arrive at the place of performance of community service (enterprise, institution, organisation) within two days from the date specified in the referral by an authorised official of the authorised body on probation matters without valid reasons, failure to report for community service or refusal to perform work, the type of which is determined by the enterprise, institution, organisation, more than two times during a month without valid reasons, as well as appearance at the workplace in a state of alcoholic, narcotic or toxic intoxication.
Valid reasons are illness and other documented circumstances that actually deprive the offender of the possibility to arrive for performance of community service.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 389-2 according to Law No. 2475-VIII of 03.07.2018}

Article 390

Evasion of serving a sentence in the form of restriction of liberty and in the form of deprivation of liberty
1. Unauthorised leaving of the place of restriction of liberty or malicious evasion of work, or systematic violation of public order or established rules of residence, committed by a person serving a sentence in the form of restriction of liberty, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. Failure to return to the place of serving a sentence by a person serving a sentence in the form of restriction of liberty and who was permitted a short-term departure, after expiry of the term of departure -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years or imprisonment for the same term.
3. Failure to return to the place of serving a sentence by a person serving a sentence in the form of deprivation of liberty and who was permitted a short-term departure, after expiry of the term of departure -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
