{Article 390 as amended according to Law No. 1492-VIII of 07.09.2016}

Article 390-1

Non-performance of restrictive measures, restrictive orders or failure to undergo a programme for offenders
Intentional non-performance of restrictive measures provided for by Article 91-1 of this Code, or intentional non-performance of restrictive orders, or intentional evasion of undergoing a programme for offenders by a person against whom such measures have been applied by a court, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 390-1 according to Law No. 2227-VIII of 06.12.2017}

Стаття 391. Malicious Disobedience to the Requirements of the Administration of a Penal Institution

Malicious disobedience to the lawful requirements of the administration of a penal institution or other obstruction of the administration in the lawful performance of its functions by a person serving a sentence in the form of restriction of liberty or in the form of deprivation of liberty, if this person was subjected during the year to a penalty in the form of transfer to a cell-type premises (solitary cell) or was transferred to a more severe regime of serving the sentence for violation of the requirements of the regime of serving the sentence, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{Article 391 as amended according to Law No. 1254-VI of 14.04.2009}

Стаття 392. Acts that Disorganize the Work of Penal Institutions

Terrorizing convicts in penal institutions or attacking the administration, as well as organizing an organized group for this purpose or active participation in such a group, committed by persons serving a sentence in the form of deprivation of liberty or in the form of restriction of liberty, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 392 as amended according to Law No. 1254-VI of 14.04.2009}

Стаття 393. Escape from a Place of Deprivation of Liberty or from Custody

1. Escape from a place of deprivation of liberty or from custody, committed by a person serving a sentence in the form of deprivation of liberty or arrest or being in pre-trial detention, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to five years.
2. The same acts, if they were committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or in a manner dangerous to the life or health of other persons, or combined with seizure of weapons or their use, or with the use of violence or threat of its use, or by tunneling, as well as with damage to engineering and technical means of protection, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to eight years.

Стаття 394. Escape from a Specialized Medical Institution

Escape from a specialized medical institution, as well as on the way to it -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years.

Стаття 395. Violation of the Rules of Administrative Supervision

Unauthorized leaving by a person of the place of residence with the purpose of evading administrative supervision, as well as failure to arrive without valid reasons within the specified time to the chosen place of residence of a person in respect of whom administrative supervision has been established upon release from places of deprivation of liberty, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months.

Стаття 396. Concealment of a Crime

1. Concealment not promised in advance of a grave or especially grave crime -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to three months or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. Family members or close relatives of the person who committed the crime, the circle of which is determined by law, shall not be subject to criminal liability for concealment not promised in advance of a crime.

Стаття 397. Interference with the Activities of a Defense Counsel or Representative of a Person

1. Obstruction in any form of the lawful activities of a defense counsel or representative of a person in providing legal assistance or violation of the guarantees of their activities and professional secrecy established by law -
shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to two hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or correctional labor for a term of up to two years, or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed by an official using his or her official position, -
shall be punished by a fine of three hundred to five hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities for a term of up to three years.

Стаття 398. Threat or Violence against a Defense Counsel or Representative of a Person

1. Threat of murder, violence or destruction or damage to property against a defense counsel or representative of a person, as well as against their close relatives in connection with activities related to the provision of legal assistance, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. Intentional infliction on a defense counsel or representative of a person or their close relatives of light or medium gravity bodily injuries in connection with activities related to the provision of legal assistance, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
3. Intentional infliction on the same persons in connection with activities related to the provision of legal assistance of grave bodily injury -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of seven to twelve years.

Стаття 399. Intentional Destruction or Damage to Property of a Defense Counsel or Representative of a Person

1. Intentional destruction or damage to property belonging to a defense counsel or representative of a person or their close relatives, in connection with activities related to the provision of legal assistance, -
shall be punished by a fine of fifty to one hundred tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months, or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years, or deprivation of liberty for the same term.
2. The same acts committed by arson, explosion or other generally dangerous method, or if they caused damage in especially large amounts, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this article that caused the death of people, infliction of grave bodily injuries on them or the occurrence of other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to fifteen years.

Стаття 400

Encroachment on the Life of a Defense Counsel or Representative of a Person in Connection with Activities Related to the Provision of Legal Assistance
Murder or attempted murder of a defense counsel or representative of a person or their close relatives in connection with activities related to the provision of legal assistance, -
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to fifteen years or life imprisonment.

Стаття 400-1. Representation in Court without Authority

1. Knowingly false notification to the court of authority to represent another person in court, as well as intentional failure by an advocate to enter into the warrant information regarding limitations of authority established by the agreement on the provision of legal assistance, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to three thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to three months.
2. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed repeatedly or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to ten thousand tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or restriction of liberty for a term of up to three years.
{Section XVIII supplemented with Article 400-1 according to Law No. 2147-VIII of 03.10.2017}

Розділ XIX

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST THE ESTABLISHED PROCEDURE FOR MILITARY SERVICE (MILITARY CRIMINAL OFFENSES)
{Title of Section XIX as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 401. Concept of military criminal offence

1. Military criminal offences are criminal offences against the procedure for carrying out or undergoing military service established by legislation, provided for in this section, committed by servicepersons, as well as persons liable for military service and reservists during training assemblies.
2. Servicepersons of the Armed Forces of Ukraine, the Security Service of Ukraine, the State Border Guard Service of Ukraine, the National Guard of Ukraine and other military formations established in accordance with the laws of Ukraine, the State Special Transport Service, the State Service for Special Communications and Information Protection of Ukraine, as well as other persons determined by law, shall be held liable under the respective articles of this section.
Police officers of the special purpose police of the National Police of Ukraine who are involved in direct participation in combat operations during martial law shall be held liable under articles 402, 403, 414-416, 422, 427, 429, 430, 432-435 of this section.
3. Persons not specified in this article shall be held liable for complicity in military criminal offences under the respective articles of this section.
4. A person who has committed a criminal offence provided for by the articles of this section, except for police officers of the special purpose police of the National Police of Ukraine, may be released from criminal liability in accordance with Article 44 of this Code with the application of measures provided for by the Disciplinary Statute of the Armed Forces of Ukraine.
{Article 401 as amended in accordance with Laws No. 662-IV of 03.04.2003, No. 1414-VI of 02.06.2009, No. 877-VII of 13.03.2014, No. 1194-VII of 09.04.2014; as worded by Law No. 1952-VIII of 16.03.2017; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 3000-IX of 21.03.2023}

Article 402. Insubordination

1. Insubordination, that is, open refusal to execute an order of a superior, as well as other intentional failure to execute an order -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to two years or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. The same act, if committed by a group of persons or if it caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
3. Insubordination committed under conditions of a special period, except for martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years.
4. Insubordination committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
Note. Combat situation in Section XIX of this Code shall mean the situation of an offensive, defensive or other combined arms, tank, anti-aircraft, air, naval, etc. battle, that is, the direct use of military weapons and equipment against a military adversary or by a military adversary. The situation of a battle in which a military formation, unit (ship) or subdivision participates begins and ends with an order to enter into battle (cease battle) or with the actual commencement (completion) of battle.
{Article 402 as worded by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Article 403. Failure to execute an order

1. Failure to execute an order of a superior, committed in the absence of the elements specified in part one of Article 402 of this Code, if it caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to two years or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to one year, or imprisonment for a term up to two years.
2. The same act committed under conditions of a special period, except for martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
3. The act provided for in part one of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
{Article 403 as worded by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015; as amended in accordance with Law No. 2839-IX of 13.12.2022}

Article 404. Resistance to a superior or coercion of a superior to violate official duties

1. Resistance to a superior, as well as to another person performing duties assigned to them in military service, or coercion of them to violate these duties -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to two years or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
2. The same acts committed by a group of persons or with the use of a weapon, or such that caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
3. The acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they were committed under conditions of a special period, except for martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
4. The acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, if they were committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to twelve years.
5. The acts provided for in parts two, three or four of this article, if they were connected with intentional killing of a superior or another person performing duties in military service, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment.
{Article 404 as worded by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Article 405. Threat or violence against a superior

1. Threat of murder or infliction of bodily injuries or battery to a superior, or destruction or damage of their property in connection with their performance of duties in military service -
shall be punished by detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Infliction of bodily injuries, battery or commission of other violent acts against a superior in connection with their performance of duties in military service -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to seven years.
3. The acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed under conditions of a special period, except for martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
4. The acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by a group of persons, or with the use of a weapon, or under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 405 as worded by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Article 406. Violation of statutory rules of relations between servicepersons in the absence of relations of subordination

1. Violation of statutory rules of relations between servicepersons in the absence of relations of subordination between them, manifested in infliction of battery or commission of other violence, -
shall be punished by arrest for a term up to six months or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to one year, or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. The same act, if committed against several persons or if it caused light or medium gravity bodily injuries, as well as such that has the character of torture or mockery of a serviceperson, -
shall be punished by detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years or imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
3. The acts provided for in parts one or two of this article, committed by a group of persons or with the use of a weapon, or such that caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.

Стаття 407. Unauthorized Abandonment of Military Unit or Place of Service

1. Unauthorized abandonment of a military unit or place of service by a conscript servicemember, as well as failure to report for service on time without valid reasons in case of release from the unit, assignment or transfer, failure to return from a business trip, leave or from a medical facility for a period exceeding three days but not more than one month, -
shall be punished by confinement in a disciplinary battalion for a term of up to two years or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. Unauthorized abandonment of a military unit or place of service by a servicemember (other than conscript service), as well as failure to report for service on time without valid reasons for a period exceeding ten days but not more than one month, or even less than ten days but more than three days, committed repeatedly within one year, -
shall be punished by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or by service restriction for a term of up to two years, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. Unauthorized abandonment of a military unit or place of service, as well as failure to report for service on time without valid reasons for a period exceeding one month, committed by persons specified in parts one or two of this Article, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
4. Unauthorized abandonment of a military unit or place of service by a servicemember, as well as failure to report for service on time without valid reasons for a period exceeding three days, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
5. Unauthorized abandonment of a military unit or place of service by a servicemember, as well as failure to report for service on time without valid reasons, committed in combat conditions, as well as the same acts for a period exceeding three days, committed under conditions of martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 407 as amended in accordance with Law No. 158-VIII of 05.02.2015; as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015; as amended in accordance with Laws No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018, No. 2839-IX of 13.12.2022, No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}
1. Desertion, that is, unauthorized abandonment of a military unit or place of service with the purpose of evading military service, as well as failure to report for service with the same purpose in case of assignment, transfer, from a business trip, leave or from a medical facility -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
2. Desertion with a weapon or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
4. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed under conditions of martial law or in combat conditions, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
{Article 408 as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 409. Evasion of Military Service by Self-Mutilation or Other Means

1. Evasion by a servicemember of performance of military service duties by self-mutilation or by simulation of illness, forgery of documents or other deception -
shall be punished by confinement in a disciplinary battalion for a term of up to two years or by imprisonment for the same term.
2. Refusal to perform military service duties -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
4. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed under conditions of martial law or in combat conditions, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 409 as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 410

Theft, Misappropriation, Extortion by a Servicemember of Weapons, Ammunition, Explosives or Other Combat Materials, Means of Transportation, Military and Special Equipment or Other Military Property, as well as Taking Possession of Them by Fraud or Abuse of Official Position
1. Theft, misappropriation, extortion by a servicemember of weapons, ammunition, explosives or other combat materials, means of transportation, military and special equipment or other military property, or taking possession of them by fraud -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
2. The same acts committed by a military official with abuse of official position, or repeatedly, or by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, or those that caused substantial damage, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, if they were committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years.
4. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, if they were committed under conditions of martial law or in combat conditions, robbery for the purpose of taking possession of weapons, ammunition, explosives or other combat materials, means of transportation, military and special equipment, as well as extortion of these items combined with violence dangerous to the life and health of the victim, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years.
{Article 410 as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 411. Intentional Destruction or Damage of Military Property

1. Intentional destruction or damage of weapons, ammunition, means of transportation, military and special equipment or other military property -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term of up to two years or by confinement in a disciplinary battalion for the same term, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts committed by arson or other generally dangerous method, or if they caused death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
3. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
4. Acts provided for in parts one or two of this Article, committed under conditions of martial law or in combat conditions, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 411 as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 412. Negligent Destruction or Damage of Military Property

1. Negligent destruction or damage of weapons, ammunition, means of transportation, military and special equipment or other military property that caused damage in large amounts, -
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or by service restriction for a term of up to two years, or by confinement in a disciplinary battalion for a term of up to one year.
2. The same acts, if they caused death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by confinement in a disciplinary battalion for a term of up to two years or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Стаття 413. Loss of military property

1. Loss or damage of weapons, ammunition, means of transportation, items of technical supply or other military property entrusted for official use as a result of violation of the rules for their storage -
shall be punished by arrest for a term up to six months or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The same acts committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term up to three years.
3. Acts provided for in part one of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to five years.
{Article 413 as amended according to Law No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008; as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 414

Violation of rules for handling weapons, as well as substances and objects that pose an increased danger to the surroundings
1. Violation of rules for handling weapons, as well as ammunition, explosives, other substances and objects that pose an increased danger to the surroundings, and also radioactive materials, if this caused bodily injuries to the victim or created danger to the environment, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to two years or detention in a disciplinary battalion for the same term, or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. The same act that caused bodily injuries to several persons or death of the victim, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to ten years.
3. The act provided for in part one of this article that caused the death of several persons or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to twelve years.
{Article 414 as amended according to Law No. 1071-V of 24.05.2007}

Стаття 415. Violation of rules for driving or operating machines

1. Violation of rules for driving or operating combat, special or transport machines that caused the victim medium gravity or grave bodily injuries or death of the victim, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to one year or restriction of liberty for a term from one to three years, or detention in a disciplinary battalion up to one year, or imprisonment for a term from one to five years.
2. The act provided for in part one of this article that caused the death of several persons, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to two years or restriction of liberty for a term from three to five years, or detention in a disciplinary battalion up to two years, or imprisonment for a term from five to ten years.
{Article 415 as amended according to Law No. 3380-IX of 06.09.2023}

Стаття 416. Violation of rules for flights or preparation for them

1. Violation of rules for flights or preparation for them, as well as violation of rules for operating aircraft, that caused the victim grave bodily injuries or inflicted material damage in a large amount, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to one year or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to one year, or imprisonment for a term from two to seven years.
2. The act provided for in part one of this article that caused a catastrophe or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to two years or restriction of liberty for a term up to five years, or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for a term from five to fifteen years.
Note. Material damage in a large amount in articles 416 and 417 of this Code means damage that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by six hundred or more times.
Grave consequences in articles 416 and 417 of this Code mean consequences consisting in infliction of material damage (losses) in the form of irretrievable loss of an aircraft, ship or if the cost of restoration (repair) of the aircraft, ship exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by sixty thousand or more times.
{Article 416 as revised by Law No. 3380-IX of 06.09.2023}

Стаття 417. Violation of rules for ship navigation

1. Violation of rules for ship navigation that caused the victim grave bodily injuries or inflicted material damage in a large amount, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to one year or restriction of liberty for a term up to two years, or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to one year, or imprisonment for a term from two to seven years.
2. The act provided for in part one of this article that caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by service restriction for a term up to two years or restriction of liberty for a term up to five years, or detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years, or imprisonment for a term from five to fifteen years.
{Article 417 as revised by Law No. 3380-IX of 06.09.2023}

Стаття 418. Violation of statutory rules for guard duty or patrolling

1. Violation of statutory rules for guard (watch) duty or patrolling that caused grave consequences, for the prevention of which the given guard (watch) or patrol was assigned, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term from two to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. The act provided for in part one of this article, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to seven years.
3. The act provided for in part one of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years.
{Article 418 as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 419. Violation of rules for performing border service

1. Violation of rules for performing border service by a person who is part of a detail for protection of the state border of Ukraine, if this caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years.
2. The same act committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to ten years.
3. The act provided for in part one of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from seven to ten years.
{Article 419 as revised by Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 420. Violation of rules for performing combat duty

1. Violation of rules for performing combat duty (combat service) established for timely detection and repulsion of a sudden attack on Ukraine or for the defense and security of Ukraine, if this caused grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eight years.
2. The same act committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to eight years.
3. The act provided for in part one of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to ten years.
{Article 420 as revised by Laws No. 270-VI of 15.04.2008, No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Стаття 421. Violation of statutory rules for internal service

1. Violation by a person who is part of the daily detail of a unit (except guard and watch) of statutory rules for internal service, if it caused grave consequences, the prevention of which was part of the duties of the given person, -
shall be punished by detention in a disciplinary battalion for a term up to two years or imprisonment for a term up to three years.
2. The same act committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to five years.
3. The act provided for in part one of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in a combat situation, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term up to five years.
{Article 421 as revised by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Article 422

Disclosure of military information constituting a state secret, or loss of documents or materials containing such information
1. Disclosure of military information constituting a state secret, in the absence of signs of state treason -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
2. Loss of documents or materials containing military information constituting a state secret, objects information about which constitutes a state secret, by a person to whom they were entrusted, if the loss resulted from violation of established rules for handling the specified documents, materials or objects, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of two to five years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 423 excluded on the basis of Law No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014}
{Article 424 excluded on the basis of Law No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014}

Article 425. Negligent attitude to military service

1. Negligent attitude to service by a military official, if it caused substantial harm, -
is punishable by a fine of two hundred eighty-five to three hundred twenty-five non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or service restriction for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The same act, if it caused grave consequences, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years.
4. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in combat situation, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
Note. 1. Military officials means military commanders, as well as other military personnel who hold permanently or temporarily positions related to the performance of organizational-administrative or administrative-economic duties, or perform such duties by special assignment of authorized command.
2. In articles 425, 426 and 426-1 of this Code, substantial harm, if it consists in causing material losses, is considered harm that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by two hundred fifty or more times, and grave consequences under the same condition is considered harm that exceeds the non-taxable minimum income of citizens by five hundred or more times.
{Article 425 as amended according to Law No. 746-VII of 21.02.2014; as worded by Laws No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015, No. 1952-VIII of 16.03.2017; as amended according to Law No. 3233-IX of 13.07.2023}

Article 426. Inaction of military authority

1. Intentional failure to stop a criminal offense being committed by a subordinate, or failure by a military official to send to the pre-trial investigation body a notification about a subordinate who committed a criminal offense, as well as other intentional failure by a military official to perform actions which he or she was obliged to perform according to his or her official duties, if it caused substantial harm, -
are punishable by a fine of one thousand to four thousand non-taxable minimum incomes of citizens or service restriction for a term of up to two years, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The same acts, if they caused grave consequences, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
3. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to seven years.
4. Acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in combat situation, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.
{Article 426 as amended according to Law No. 4652-VI of 13.04.2012; as worded by Laws No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015, No. 1952-VIII of 16.03.2017; as amended according to Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}

Article 426-1. Abuse of authority or official powers by a military official

1. Abuse of authority or official powers by a military official, that is intentional commission of acts that clearly exceed the limits of rights or powers granted to this person, except those provided for by part two of this article, if these acts caused substantial harm, -
is punishable by restriction of liberty for a term of two to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Application of non-statutory measures of influence against a subordinate or abuse of disciplinary authority, if these acts caused substantial harm, as well as application of violence against a subordinate -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
3. Acts provided for by part two of this article, committed with the use of weapons, as well as acts provided for by parts one or two of this article, if they caused grave consequences, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to nine years.
4. Acts provided for by parts one, two or three of this article, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.
5. Acts provided for by parts one, two or three of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in combat situation, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years.
{The Code supplemented with article 426-1 according to Law No. 290-VIII of 07.04.2015}

Article 427. Surrender or abandonment to the enemy of means of warfare

Surrender to the enemy by a commander of military forces entrusted to him or her, as well as abandonment to the enemy not conditioned by combat situation of fortifications, combat and special equipment or other means of warfare, if the specified acts were committed not with the purpose of assisting the enemy, -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.

Article 428. Abandonment of a perishing military ship

1. Abandonment of a perishing military ship by the commander who did not fulfill his or her official duties to the end, as well as by a person from the ship's crew without proper order from the commander -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
2. The same act, committed under conditions of a special period, except martial law, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
3. The act provided for by part one of this article, committed under conditions of martial law or in combat situation, -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.
{Article 428 as worded by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Article 429. Unauthorized abandonment of the battlefield or refusal to use weapons

Unauthorized abandonment of the battlefield during battle or refusal during battle to use weapons -
are punishable by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.

Article 430. Voluntary surrender into captivity

Voluntary surrender into captivity due to cowardice or frivolity -
is punishable by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.

Stattia 431. Criminal Actions of a Servicemember in Captivity

1. Voluntary participation of a servicemember in captivity in work of military significance, or in other activities that knowingly may cause harm to Ukraine or states allied with it, in the absence of signs of state treason -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
2. Violence against other prisoners of war or cruel treatment of them by a prisoner of war who is in the position of a senior -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years.
3. Commission by a servicemember in captivity of actions directed at harming other prisoners of war, from mercenary motives or with the purpose of securing lenient treatment from the enemy -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Theft on the battlefield of items found on the killed or wounded (marauding) -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
{Article 432 as amended in accordance with Law No. 2117-IX of 03.03.2022}

Stattia 433. Violence Against the Population in the Area of Military Operations

1. Violence, unlawful destruction of property, as well as unlawful seizure of property under the pretext of military necessity, committed against the population in the area of military operations, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
2. Robbery committed against the population in the area of military operations, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to ten years.

Stattia 434. Ill-Treatment of Prisoners of War

Ill-treatment of prisoners of war that occurred repeatedly, or was associated with particular cruelty, or was directed against the sick and wounded, as well as negligent performance of duties regarding the sick and wounded by persons charged with their treatment and care for them, in the absence of signs of a more serious crime -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Stattia 435

Unlawful Use of the Symbols of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal and Abuse Thereof
1. Wearing in the area of military operations of the symbols of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal by persons who have no right to do so, as well as abuse during a special period, except martial law, of the flags or signs of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal or the coloring assigned to medical transport vehicles, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to two years.
2. Wearing in the area of military operations of the symbols of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal by persons who have no right to do so, as well as abuse under conditions of martial law of the flags or signs of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal or the coloring assigned to medical transport vehicles, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
{Article 435 as amended by Law No. 194-VIII of 12.02.2015}

Stattia 435-1. Insult to the Honor and Dignity of a Servicemember, Threat to a Servicemember

1. Insult to the honor and dignity, threat of murder, violence or destruction or damage to property of a servicemember who carries out measures to ensure national security and defense, repulsion and deterrence of armed aggression of the Russian Federation, his close relatives or family members -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
2. Production and distribution of materials containing insult to the honor and dignity, threat of murder, violence or destruction or damage to property of a servicemember who carries out measures to ensure national security and defense, repulsion and deterrence of armed aggression of the Russian Federation, his close relatives or family members, -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of three to five years or imprisonment for the same term.
{Section XIX supplemented with Article 435-1 in accordance with Law No. 2110-IX of 03.03.2022}

Rozdil XX

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST PEACE, SECURITY OF HUMANITY AND INTERNATIONAL LEGAL ORDER
{Title of Section XX as amended in accordance with Law No. 2617-VIII of 22.11.2018}
Public calls for aggressive war or for unleashing military conflict, as well as production of materials with calls to commit such actions for the purpose of their distribution or distribution of such materials -
shall be punished by correctional labor for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Stattia 436-1

Production, Distribution of Communist, Nazi Symbols and Propaganda of Communist and National Socialist (Nazi) Totalitarian Regimes
1. Production, distribution, as well as public use of symbols of communist, national socialist (Nazi) totalitarian regimes, including in the form of souvenir products, public performance of anthems of the USSR, UkrSSR (USRR), other union and autonomous Soviet republics or their fragments on the entire territory of Ukraine, except for cases provided for by parts two and three of Article 4 of the Law of Ukraine "On the Condemnation of the Communist and National Socialist (Nazi) Totalitarian Regimes in Ukraine and the Prohibition of Propaganda of Their Symbols", -
shall be punished by restriction of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with confiscation of property or without such.
2. The same actions committed by a person who is a representative of authority, or committed repeatedly, or by an organized group, or using mass media, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years with confiscation of property or without such.
{Code supplemented with Article 436-1 in accordance with Law No. 729-VII of 16.01.2014 - became invalid on the basis of Law No. 732-VII of 28.01.2014; Code supplemented with Article 436-1 in accordance with Law No. 735-VII of 28.01.2014; as amended by Law No. 317-VIII of 09.04.2015}

Стаття 436-2

Justification, recognition as lawful, denial of the armed aggression of the Russian Federation against Ukraine, glorification of its participants
1. Justification, recognition as lawful, denial of the armed aggression of the Russian Federation against Ukraine, commenced in 2014, including by representing the armed aggression of the Russian Federation against Ukraine as an internal civil conflict, justification, recognition as lawful, denial of the temporary occupation of part of the territory of Ukraine, as well as glorification of persons who carried out the armed aggression of the Russian Federation against Ukraine, commenced in 2014, representatives of the armed formations of the Russian Federation, irregular illegal armed formations, armed gangs and groups of mercenaries, created, subordinated, controlled and financed by the Russian Federation, as well as representatives of the occupation administration of the Russian Federation, which consists of its state bodies and structures, functionally responsible for the administration of the temporarily occupied territories of Ukraine, and representatives of self-proclaimed bodies controlled by the Russian Federation, which usurped the exercise of governmental functions in the temporarily occupied territories of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to six months, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. Production, dissemination of materials containing justification, recognition as lawful, denial of the armed aggression of the Russian Federation against Ukraine, commenced in 2014, including by representing the armed aggression of the Russian Federation against Ukraine as an internal civil conflict, justification, recognition as lawful, denial of the temporary occupation of part of the territory of Ukraine, as well as glorification of persons who carried out the armed aggression of the Russian Federation against Ukraine, commenced in 2014, representatives of the armed formations of the Russian Federation, irregular illegal armed formations, armed gangs and groups of mercenaries, created, subordinated, controlled and financed by the Russian Federation, as well as representatives of the occupation administration of the Russian Federation, which consists of its state bodies and structures, functionally responsible for the administration of the temporarily occupied territories of Ukraine, and representatives of self-proclaimed bodies controlled by the Russian Federation, which usurped the exercise of governmental functions in the temporarily occupied territories of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by restraint of liberty for a term of up to five years or imprisonment for the same term, with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Acts provided for in part one or two of this Article, committed by an official, or committed repeatedly, or by an organized group, or using mass media, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to eight years with confiscation of property or without such.
{The Code was supplemented with Article 436-2 in accordance with Law No. 2110-IX of 03.03.2022}

Стаття 437. Planning, preparation, unleashing and waging of aggressive war

1. Planning, preparation or unleashing of an aggressive war or military conflict, as well as participation in a conspiracy aimed at committing such acts, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years.
2. Waging of an aggressive war or aggressive military actions -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years.

Стаття 438. Violation of laws and customs of war

1. Cruel treatment of prisoners of war or civilian population, deportation of civilian population for forced labour, plundering of national valuables in occupied territory, use of means of warfare prohibited by international law, other violations of laws and customs of war provided for by international treaties, the binding nature of which has been approved by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, as well as giving an order to commit such acts -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years.
2. The same acts, if they are combined with intentional murder, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment.

Стаття 439. Use of weapons of mass destruction

1. Use of weapons of mass destruction prohibited by international treaties, the binding nature of which has been approved by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twelve years.
2. The same act, if it caused death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years or life imprisonment.

Стаття 440

Development, production, acquisition, storage, sale, transportation of weapons of mass destruction
Development, production, acquisition, storage, sale, transportation of weapons of mass destruction prohibited by international treaties, the binding nature of which has been approved by the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
Mass destruction of flora or fauna, poisoning of the atmosphere or water resources, as well as committing other acts that may cause an ecological catastrophe, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years.
1. Genocide, that is, an act intentionally committed with the purpose of complete or partial destruction of any national, ethnic, racial or religious group by depriving members of such group of life or causing them grievous bodily harm, creating living conditions for the group calculated for its complete or partial physical destruction, reducing childbirth or preventing it in such group or by forcible transfer of children from one group to another, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment.
2. Public calls for genocide, as well as production of materials with calls for genocide with the purpose of their dissemination or dissemination of such materials -
shall be punished by arrest for a term of up to six months or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

Стаття 443. Encroachment on the life of a representative of a foreign state

Murder or attempted murder of a representative of a foreign state or another person who has international protection, with the purpose of influencing the nature of their activities or the activities of states or organizations they represent, or with the purpose of provoking war or international complications -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years or life imprisonment.

Стаття 444. Crimes against persons and institutions having international protection

1. Attack on official or residential premises of persons who have international protection, as well as abduction or deprivation of liberty of these persons with the purpose of influencing the nature of their activities or the activities of states or organizations they represent, or with the purpose of provoking war or international complications -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
2. Threat of committing acts provided for in part one of this Article, -
shall be punished by correctional labour for a term of up to two years or arrest for a term of up to three months, or restraint of liberty for a term of up to three years, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Стаття 445

Unlawful use of the symbols of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal
{Title of Article 445 as amended according to Law No. 1675-VI of 22.10.2009}
Unlawful use of the symbols of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal, except in cases provided for by this Code, -
{Paragraph one of Article 445 as amended according to Law No. 1675-VI of 22.10.2009}
shall be punished by a fine of up to fifty tax-free minimum incomes of citizens or arrest for a term of up to six months.
1. Piracy, that is, the use of an armed or unarmed vessel for the purpose of obtaining material remuneration or other personal benefit to seize another sea or river vessel, the use of violence, robbery or other hostile actions against the crew or passengers of such vessel, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to twelve years with confiscation of property.
2. The same acts, if they were committed repeatedly or caused the death of people or other grave consequences, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to fifteen years with confiscation of property.
1. Recruitment, financing, material support, training of mercenaries for the purpose of using them in armed conflicts, military or violent actions aimed at the violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order, seizure of state power, obstruction of the activities of state authorities or violation of territorial integrity, as well as the use of mercenaries in armed conflicts, military or violent actions -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
2. The same acts committed by an official using their official position, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years with confiscation of property or without such.
3. Actions provided for in parts one or two of this article that resulted in the death of a person, -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to fifteen years or life imprisonment with confiscation of property or without such.
4. Participation of a mercenary in an armed conflict, military or violent actions -
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
5. A mercenary shall be released from criminal liability for actions provided for in part four of this article if, before being brought to criminal liability, he voluntarily ceased participation in an armed conflict, military or violent actions and reported his participation in the conflict, military or violent actions or otherwise contributed to the cessation or disclosure of crimes provided for in parts one to three of this article, if his actions do not contain the elements of another crime.
Note. A mercenary in this article shall be understood as a person who:
1) is specially recruited in Ukraine or abroad in order to take part on the territory of Ukraine or the territory of other states in an armed conflict, military or violent actions aimed at the violent change or overthrow of the constitutional order, seizure of state power, obstruction of the activities of state authorities or violation of territorial integrity;
2) takes part in military or violent actions for the purpose of obtaining any personal benefit;
3) is neither a citizen (subject) of a party to the conflict, nor a person who permanently resides lawfully in the territory controlled by a party to the conflict;
4) is not part of the personnel of the armed forces of the state on whose territory such actions are carried out;
5) has not been sent by a state that is not a party to the conflict to perform official duties as a person who is part of its armed forces.
{Article 447 as amended by Law No. 716-VIII of 06.10.2015}
FINAL AND TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS
1. This Code shall enter into force on September 1, 2001.
2. Upon entry into force of this Code, the following shall cease to be in force:
The Criminal Code of the Ukrainian SSR of December 28, 1960 (Bulletin of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR, 1961, No. 2, Art. 14) as amended, except for the List of property not subject to confiscation by court verdict (Annex to that Code);
The Law of the Ukrainian SSR "On Approval of the Criminal Code of the Ukrainian SSR" (Bulletin of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR, 1961, No. 2, Art. 14);
Articles 1, 2 and 5 of the Decree of the Presidium of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR of April 20, 1990 "On Liability for Actions Directed Against Public Order and Safety of Citizens" (Bulletin of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR, 1990, No. 18, Art. 278);
The Decree of the Presidium of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR of December 26, 1990 "On Liability for Violation of the Procedure for Using Consumer Cards for the Right to Purchase Goods and Other Official Documents" (Bulletin of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR, 1991, No. 3, Art. 13);
{Paragraph six of point 2 of Section I is not applied on the territory of Ukraine according to Law No. 2215-IX of 21.04.2022} Article 3 of the Decree of the Presidium of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR of January 28, 1991 "On Liability for Violation of the Requirements of the Radiation Safety Regime, Procurement, Processing and Sale of Radioactively Contaminated Food Products" (Bulletin of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR, 1991, No. 11, Art. 106);
The Decree of the Presidium of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine of January 21, 1992 "On Liability for Manufacturing for the Purpose of Sale and Sale of Counterfeit Coupons for Multiple Use" (Bulletin of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine, 1992, No. 18, Art. 246).
3. The Cabinet of Ministers of Ukraine shall, within three months from the date of publication of this Code, prepare and submit to the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine a list of legislative acts that should be recognized as having ceased to be in force in connection with the entry into force of this Code.
1. Release from punishment (principal and additional) in accordance with part two of Article 74 of this Code persons convicted under the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 for acts for which liability is not provided for by this Code:
a) persons convicted on the basis of part three of Article 5 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960;
b) persons convicted of preparation for a crime under part one of Article 17 and the relevant articles of the Special Part of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960, if these crimes are crimes of minor gravity;
c) persons convicted under Articles 108-1, 110, 134-1, 125, 126, 147-2, 147-3, 149, 154, 155-2, 155-3, 155-5, 155-6, 159, 169, 176-3, 183-2, 183-4, 187, 187-7, 187-8, 189, 189-1, 193-1, 199, 202, 206 (part one), 208-1, 208-2, 229-8, 237 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960;
d) persons convicted under part one of Article 133, Articles 147 (part one), 148-3, 161 (part one), 187-4, 187-8, 192 (part three), 196-1 (part one), 199 (parts one and two), 207-1, 227-2 (part one), the condition for application of which under the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 was the prior imposition of an administrative penalty on these persons;
e) persons convicted of crimes committed at the age of 14 to 16 years, provided for by Articles 78-1, 97, 98, 106 (part one) of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960;
f) persons convicted under Article 186 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 (except for those convicted of concealment not promised in advance of grave and especially grave crimes), as well as persons specified in part two of Article 396 of this Code, convicted of concealment not promised in advance under Article 186 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960;
g) persons convicted on the basis of the Decree of the Presidium of the Verkhovna Rada of the Ukrainian SSR of December 26, 1990 "On Liability for Violation of the Procedure for Using Consumer Cards for the Right to Purchase Goods and Other Official Documents" and the Decree of the Presidium of the Verkhovna Rada of Ukraine of January 21, 1992 "On Liability for Manufacturing for the Purpose of Sale and Sale of Counterfeit Coupons for Multiple Use".
a) persons convicted for the first time to punishment in the form of imprisonment for crimes of minor gravity committed at the age of under 18 years, in accordance with the requirements of part two of Article 12 and part two of Article 102 of this Code;
b) military personnel convicted to punishment in the form of assignment to a disciplinary battalion for a term of up to six months;
c) persons convicted on the basis of part one of Article 29 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 who are serving this punishment not at their place of work, but in other places, but in the district of residence of the convicted person, as well as persons convicted to correctional labor without imprisonment for a term of up to six months.
3. Close all criminal cases against persons who committed crimes provided for by the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960, listed in point 1 of this section.
4. Persons serving a sentence imposed by cumulative sentences, in case of their release from serving a sentence for individual crimes on the basis of point 1 of this section, shall continue to serve the sentence imposed by the court verdict for other crimes included in the cumulation, if this sentence has not yet been served by them. The imposed sentence shall also be subject to reduction in cases provided for in point 5 of this section.
5. Reduce in accordance with part three of Article 74 of this Code the measures of punishment imposed under the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960, if they exceed the sanctions of the relevant articles of this Code, to the maximum limits of punishment established by this Code. Reduce in accordance with part one of Article 55 of this Code to three years the term of punishment in the form of deprivation of the right to hold certain positions or engage in certain activities imposed as an additional punishment. Reduce in accordance with parts two and three of Article 100 of this Code to one year the term of correctional labor imposed on minors, as well as the amount of deduction from their earnings to 10 percent.
6. Persons serving a sentence by court verdict in the form of imprisonment for a term of up to five years in settlement colonies shall be considered as serving a sentence in the form of restriction of liberty provided for by Article 61 of this Code.
7. Persons deprived of parental rights in accordance with Article 38 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 may be restored to these rights only in the manner provided for by the Code on Marriage and Family of Ukraine. Persons convicted to punishment in the form of public censure (

Article 33

of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960), if their criminal record was not expunged before this Code entered into force, shall be considered as having no criminal record.
8. Not to execute court sentences in the part of confiscation of property and collection of fines, if before this Code entered into force the confiscated property was not seized and realized, and the fine was not collected in the event that for the given crime this Code does not provide for confiscation of property and imposition of a fine.
9. From the day this Code enters into force, persons recognized as such in accordance with Article 26 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 shall not be particularly dangerous repeat offenders. If these persons continue to serve the punishment assigned to them, then the court sentences concerning them are subject to modification in the part of recognizing them as particularly dangerous repeat offenders. If necessary, the qualification of the crimes committed by them shall be changed, and the punishment shall be reduced in accordance with part three of Article 74 of this Code. If the persons specified in the first sentence of this paragraph have served the punishment assigned to them but have an unexpunged criminal record, then the expungement of the criminal record of these persons shall be carried out according to the rules provided by Articles 89 and 90 of this Code.
10. Review all cases concerning crimes of persons who committed misappropriation of state or collective property in large or particularly large amounts, provided for by part four of Article 81, part four of Article 82, part four of Article 84, part two of Article 86, Article 86-1 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960, to resolve issues concerning the change of qualification of the actions of these persons to the corresponding parts and articles of this Code (Articles 185, 186, 187, 190, 191).
Bring the measures of punishment for persons who are convicted of misappropriation in large or particularly large amounts under Articles 81, 82, 83, 84, 86, 86-1 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 and have not served their punishment into conformity with the punishments established by the sanctions of Articles 185, 186, 187, 190, 191 of this Code in the event that the punishment imposed by the court for the corresponding crime is more severe than provided by this Code.
11. The rules established by the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 regarding statute of limitations, conditional early release from serving punishment, replacement of the unserved part of punishment with a milder one, expungement and removal of criminal record, shall apply to persons who committed crimes before this Code entered into force, except in cases where this Code mitigates the criminal liability of the specified persons.
12. In the case of imposing a milder punishment than provided by law for a crime committed before this Code entered into force, the court should be guided by the requirements of Article 44 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960.
13. In the case of imposing punishment for a cumulation of crimes committed before this Code entered into force, Article 42 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960 shall apply. If at least one of the crimes included in the cumulation was committed after this Code entered into force, then Article 70 or part two of Article 103 of this Code shall apply.
If a person serving punishment commits a new crime after this Code enters into force, then when imposing punishment for a cumulation of sentences, Article 71 or part two of Article 103 of this Code shall apply.
14. When resolving the issue of release from serving punishment with probation for persons who committed crimes after this Code entered into force, the court shall apply Articles 75-77 of this Code.
Reduce in accordance with part three of Article 104 of this Code the probationary period to two years for conditionally sentenced minors if the probationary period determined by the court is above this limit.
15. To persons sentenced to punishment in the form of deprivation of liberty with application of postponement of execution of the sentence in accordance with Article 46-1 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960, Article 78 of this Code shall apply.
16. In the case of release from criminal liability and punishment of persons who committed a crime before this Code entered into force, due to a change in circumstances and loss of public danger by the act or by the person who committed it, one should be guided by part one or part two of Article 50 of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960.
17. The commission by a person of a crime before this Code entered into force, as well as the presence in such person of a criminal record not expunged and not removed in the manner established by law, shall be taken into account when qualifying a new crime committed by that person, as well as in other cases provided by this Code.
18. When resolving the issue of classifying crimes provided by the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960, which were committed before this Code entered into force, as crimes of minor gravity, medium gravity, serious or particularly serious, one should be guided by Article 12 of this Code, if this mitigates the criminal liability of persons who committed crimes before this Code entered into force. In other cases it is necessary to apply the corresponding provisions of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960.
19. Review of cases concerning persons who were convicted on the basis of the Criminal Code of Ukraine of 1960, as well as closure of cases concerning persons who committed crimes before this Code entered into force and whose cases are in the proceedings of courts, pre-trial investigation bodies or inquiry bodies, shall be carried out by the court.
20. Bodies that execute court sentences are obliged to provide courts with necessary materials concerning persons serving punishment.
21. Issues provided in paragraphs 3, 4, 6, 7, 9, 10, 15 of this section shall be considered by the court upon submission by the administration of the place of execution of punishment or the prosecutor in an open court session with the participation of the prosecutor and a representative of the administration of the place of execution of punishment in cases where the case is considered upon its submission.
The decision (ruling) of the court on these issues may be appealed by the convicted person or their defense counsel or a corresponding submission may be made on it by the prosecutor in the manner provided by the Criminal Procedure Code of Ukraine.
{Paragraph two of paragraph 21 of Section II "Final and Transitional Provisions" as amended by Law No. 1130-IV of 11.07.2003}
22. Civilians shall not bear criminal liability for the use of firearms against persons who carry out armed aggression against Ukraine, if such weapons are used in accordance with the requirements of the Law of Ukraine "On Ensuring the Participation of Civilians in the Defense of Ukraine".
{Section II supplemented with paragraph 22 in accordance with Law No. 2114-IX of 03.03.2022}
